			if key.begins_with(person.id + "|"):
				theories[key] = clone.theories[key]
		var evil = Support.assess(view, evil_case, baseline, theory.bias, prepared)
		var good_case = evil_case.duplicate(true)
		good_case.conclusion.type = "good"
		var good_prepared = _prepared(view, attention, good_case, baseline)
		var good = Support.assess(view, good_case, baseline, theory.bias, good_prepared)
		# Subtract what the listener's private evidence and priors already explained.
		# Comparing evil and good also lets exonerating evidence reduce suspicion.
		var contrast: float = evil.evidence_agreement - good.evidence_agreement
		var alignment: float = 0.45 * (clampf(contrast, -1.0, 1.0) if Movement.available(view) else contrast)
		var pattern_case = evil_case.duplicate(true)
		pattern_case.conclusion.type = "suspicious"
		pattern_case.cards = pattern_case.cards.filter(func(id): return id not in prepared.private_checks.rejected)
		var pattern: Dictionary = pattern_fn.call(view, pattern_case, baseline)
		var circumstantial: float = 0.0 if Movement.available(view) else 0.3 * pattern.weight
		# Existing pattern tuning and the world comparison can describe the SAME
		# incident. Use the stronger route, never sum them. Slice 4 replaces this
		# provisional envelope with a causal movement/trace model.
		var net_weight: float = circumstantial if circumstantial > absf(alignment) else alignment
		if prepared.private_checks.fatal or not attention.case.get("graph_errors", []).is_empty() or not Solver.validate_answers(view, attention.case).is_empty():
			net_weight = 0.0
		if TimeModel.known_good(view, baseline.knowledge, person.id, value.conclusion.at):
			net_weight = 0.0
		if value.conclusion.type == "conflict":
			net_weight = 0.0 # Identifying inconsistent testimony is not an accusation.
		var confidence: float = 1.0 if baseline.get("confidence_mode", "scale") == "persistence" else 1.4 - 0.8 * baseline.confidence
		var effect: float = net_weight * (0.5 + baseline.trust) * confidence
		effects[person.id] = {"requested": effect, "alignment_signal": alignment, "pattern_signal": circumstantial,
			"route": "circumstantial" if circumstantial > absf(alignment) else "alignment",
			"evil_agreement": evil.agreement, "good_agreement": good.agreement,
			"evil_change": evil.evidence_agreement, "good_change": good.evidence_agreement,
			"cards": attention.considered.duplicate(), "theory": theory, "reasoning": reasoning.status}
		projections[person.id] = {"evil": evil, "good": good, "bias": theory.bias}
	var arguments = _arguments(view, value)
	var claims: Array = []
	var by_id = {}
	for argument in arguments:
		var scoped = _scope(view, argument.case, attention.considered)
		var prepared = _prepared(view, attention, scoped, baseline)
		var thought = Support.explain(view, scoped, baseline, prepared)
		var bias: Dictionary = projections[scoped.conclusion.target].bias
		var support = Support.assess(view, scoped, baseline, bias, prepared)
		var pattern_case = scoped.duplicate(true)
		pattern_case.cards = pattern_case.cards.filter(func(id): return id not in prepared.private_checks.rejected)
		var pattern: Dictionary = pattern_fn.call(view, pattern_case, baseline)
		var agreement: float = support.agreement
		if scoped.conclusion.type == "suspicious":
			agreement = maxf(agreement, pattern.agreement if pattern.weight > 0 else 0.0)
			if Movement.available(view) and support.evidence_agreement > 0:
				agreement = maxf(agreement, 0.5)
		elif scoped.conclusion.type == "conflict":
			agreement = 1.0 if thought.status == "inconsistent" or not thought.private_checks.rejected.is_empty() else 0.0
		if thought.private_checks.fatal:
			agreement = 0.0
		var note = "Another explanation remains possible."
		if scoped.cards.is_empty():
			note = "No attended evidence was offered for this claim."
		elif thought.private_checks.fatal:
			note = " ".join(PackedStringArray(thought.private_checks.objections))
		elif scoped.conclusion.type == "suspicious" and pattern.weight > 0:
			note = "The observations give a circumstantial reason for suspicion; they do not establish evil."
		elif scoped.conclusion.type == "suspicious" and Movement.available(view) and support.evidence_agreement > 0:
			note = "The joint movement and trace comparison gives a circumstantial reason for suspicion; it does not establish evil."
		elif scoped.conclusion.type == "conflict":
			note = "The considered accounts conflict; that does not identify deliberate deception or evil." if agreement > 0 else "I have not established a conflict in the considered accounts."
		elif thought.status == "entailed_in_attention":
			note = "This follows from the reports I granted, if those reports are sound."
		elif thought.status == "provisional":
			note = "This fits the evidence I checked; I have not checked another explanation."
		elif thought.status == "inconsistent":
			note = "The premises I checked cannot all hold together."
		elif Movement.available(view) and agreement >= 0.6 and thought.status == "rival_found":
			note = "Another explanation still fits, but the joint movement and trace comparison favors this claim."
		var item = {"id": argument.id, "label": argument.label, "conclusion": scoped.conclusion.duplicate(true),
			"parents": argument.parents.duplicate(), "cards": scoped.cards.duplicate(),
			"ignored": Graph.compile_case(view, argument.case).cards.filter(func(id): return id not in scoped.cards),
			"agreement": agreement, "stance": _stance(agreement), "status": thought.status,
			"conditional_mass": support.conditional_mass, "forced_mass": support.forced_mass,
			"note": note, "links": [], "support_model": support, "reasoning": thought}
		claims.append(item)
		by_id[argument.id] = item
	# Link diagnostics remove only evidence exclusive to that input. Shared roots
	# remain through sibling inputs. These differences are explanatory, not bonuses.
	for argument in arguments:
		var child: Dictionary = by_id[argument.id]
		for parent_id in argument.parents:
			if not by_id.has(parent_id):
				continue
			var parent: Dictionary = by_id[parent_id]
			var parent_argument: Dictionary = arguments.filter(func(a): return a.id == parent_id)[0]
			var retained: Array = argument.get("without", {}).get(parent_id, [])
			if not argument.has("without"):
				for input in parent_argument.get("inputs", []):
					if input.id != argument.id:
						retained.append_array(input.cards)
			var exclusive: Array = child.cards.filter(func(id): return id not in retained)
			var without = _scope(view, parent_argument.case, parent.cards.filter(func(id): return id not in exclusive))
			var reduced = Support.assess(view, without, baseline, projections[parent.conclusion.target].bias, _prepared(view, attention, without, baseline))
			var contribution: float = parent.support_model.agreement - reduced.agreement
			var relation = "shared" if exclusive.is_empty() else ("strengthens" if contribution > 0.0000001 else ("weakens" if contribution < -0.0000001 else "no_preference"))
			var remaining: bool = not parent.reasoning.rival.is_empty()
			var note = {"shared": "These original sources are already included through another connection.",
				"strengthens": "This branch's evidence strengthens the parent claim.",
				"weakens": "This branch's evidence weighs against the parent claim.",
				"no_preference": "This branch does not add a preference for the parent claim."}[relation]
			if child.stance == "reject":
				note += " I do not accept the intermediate claim; its label supplies no extra evidence."
			if remaining:
				note += " An alternative to the parent claim still fits what I checked."
			child.links.append({"parent": parent_id, "parent_label": parent.label, "relation": relation,
				"evidence_difference": contribution, "exclusive_cards": exclusive, "alternative_remains": remaining, "note": note})
	return {"effects": effects, "claims": claims, "theories": theories, "considered": attention.considered,
		"rule": "One signed effect per person from the same starting beliefs and attended evidence. Intermediate claims add no evidence. Alignment and overlapping pattern routes use the stronger absolute effect, never their sum."}

static func _prepared(view: Dictionary, attention: Dictionary, scoped: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var result = attention.duplicate(true)
	result.case = scoped
	result.considered = scoped.cards.duplicate()
	result.private_checks = Reasoner.direct_checks(view, scoped, listener.knowledge)
	return result

static func _scope(view: Dictionary, value: Dictionary, ids: Array) -> Dictionary:
	var result = Graph.compile_case(view, value)
	result.cards = result.cards.filter(func(id): return id in ids)
	result.test_factors = result.test_factors.filter(func(f): return f.routes.any(func(route): return route.all(func(id): return id in result.cards)))
	for factor in result.test_factors:
		factor.routes = factor.routes.filter(func(route): return route.all(func(id): return id in result.cards))
	result.quoted_cards = result.quoted_cards.filter(func(id): return id in result.cards)
	return result

static func _arguments(view: Dictionary, value: Dictionary) -> Array:
	if value.has("arguments"):
		return value.arguments
	# List-lab cases have one level of lines. Preserve the same claim semantics.
	var result: Array = [{"id": "root", "label": "%s is %s" % [Solver.person_name(view, value.conclusion.target), value.conclusion.type], "parents": [], "case": value, "inputs": []}]
	for line in value.get("lines", []):
		if line.relation != "supports":
			continue
		result[0].inputs.append({"id": line.id, "cards": Graph.roots(value, line.cards)})
		if not line.has("conclusion"):
			continue
		var scoped = value.duplicate(true)
		scoped.cards = Graph.roots(value, line.cards)
		scoped.lines = [line]
		scoped.conclusion = line.conclusion.duplicate(true)
		result.append({"id": line.id, "label": line.label, "parents": ["root"], "case": scoped, "inputs": []})
	return result

static func _stance(agreement: float) -> String:
	return "accept" if agreement >= 0.6 else ("partial" if agreement >= 0.35 else "reject")

static func _add(view: Dictionary, card: Dictionary) -> void:
	card["source"] = card.get("source", "player")
	card["data"] = card.get("data", {})
	var after_night: bool = card.kind in ["trace", "item", "coverage"]
	card["incident_at"] = {"day": maxi(card.night, 1), "phase": "night"}
	card["observed_at"] = card.get("observed_at", {"day": maxi(card.night + int(after_night), 1), "phase": "morning" if after_night else ("evening" if card.kind == "evening" else "night")})
	card["acquired_day"] = card.observed_at.day
	card["reported_at"] = card.observed_at.duplicate(true)
	card["learned_at"] = card.reported_at.duplicate(true)
	card["fact_id"] = "fixture:" + card.id
	card["event_ids"] = ["%s:%s:%d" % [card.kind, card.subject, card.night]]
	card["provenance"] = {"source": card.source, "channel": "firsthand" if card.source == "player" else "testimony", "origin": card.id}
	view.cards.append(card)

==> workshop/ghost_juror.gd <==
## A fixed reference audience, without a town identity or private knowledge.
extends RefCounted
const Jury = preload("res://workshop/jury.gd")
const Assess = preload("res://workshop/live_assessment.gd")

static func profile(people: Array) -> Dictionary:
	var suspicion = {}
	var trust = {}
	for person in people:
		suspicion[person.id] = 0.1
		trust[person.id] = 0.5
	return {"id": "ghost", "name": "Neutral ghost juror", "trust": 0.5, "confidence": 0.5,
		"insight": 0.5, "reasoning_mode": "full", "confidence_mode": "scale", "theories": {},
		"npc_trust": trust, "suspicion": suspicion, "reading_reliability": {},
		"knowledge": {"self": "", "good": [], "own_role": "", "own_reports": [], "own_outings": {}},
		"ledger": {}, "case_memory": {}}

static func evaluate(view: Dictionary, value: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	return evaluate_with_profile(view, value, profile(view.people))

static func live(snapshot: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var input = snapshot.duplicate(true)
	input.private_evidence = []
	input.evidence = input.evidence.filter(func(e): return e.id in input.selected)
	var ghost = profile(input.people)
	input.listener = {"id": ghost.id, "name": ghost.name, "trust": ghost.trust, "confidence": ghost.confidence,
		"insight": ghost.insight, "npc_trust": ghost.npc_trust, "suspicion": ghost.suspicion,
		"discounted": {}, "honesty": {}, "own_gift": "", "legacy_evidence": []}
	var prepared = Assess.prepare(input)
	if not prepared.ready:
		return {"ready": false, "issues": prepared.issues, "proposal": {}, "elapsed_ms": 0, "scope": "Player-held evidence only; unsupported mechanics need an adapter."}
	var listener = profile(input.people)
	listener.knowledge.good = prepared.listener.knowledge.good.filter(func(id): return id != ghost.id)
	return evaluate_with_profile(prepared.view, prepared.case, listener)

static func evaluate_with_profile(view: Dictionary, value: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var started = Time.get_ticks_msec()
	var proposal = Jury.evaluate(view, value, listener)
	return {"ready": true, "issues": [], "proposal": proposal, "elapsed_ms": Time.get_ticks_msec() - started,
		"scope": "Neutral reference: player trust 0.5, witness trust 0.5, suspicion 0.1, confidence 0.5; full reasoning and public process knowledge only. Experimental strength, not an NPC or vote prediction. Narrowing worlds is not a probability of guilt."}

==> workshop/jury.gd <==
## Public-case interpretation -> assessment -> replaceable ledger effect -> response.
## Deliberately keeps the existing game's trust/suspicion/confidence/stance vocabulary.
## This is a workshop adapter, not a balance change to Mind or Argue.
extends RefCounted

const Solver = preload("res://workshop/solver.gd")
const Graph = preload("res://workshop/case_graph.gd")
const Support = preload("res://workshop/support_model.gd")
const Theory = preload("res://workshop/theory.gd")
const Reasoner = preload("res://workshop/reasoner.gd")
const Consequences = preload("res://workshop/consequences.gd")
const Movement = preload("res://workshop/movement_model.gd")
const Profiler = preload("res://workshop/profiler.gd")

static func evaluate(view: Dictionary, case_data: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, context: Dictionary = {}) -> Dictionary:
	var span = Profiler.open("jury")
	var result = _evaluate(view, case_data, listener, context)
	Profiler.close(span)
	return result

static func _evaluate(view: Dictionary, case_data: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, context: Dictionary = {}) -> Dictionary:
	var before: Dictionary = listener.suspicion.duplicate(true)
	var old: Dictionary = listener.ledger.get(case_data.id, {})
	var old_effects: Dictionary = old.get("effects", {})
	if not old.is_empty() and old_effects.is_empty():
		old_effects = {old.target: {"applied": old.applied}}
	# Retract the ENTIRE previous vector before evaluating any conclusion. Every
	# projection sees this same snapshot, including source credibility and priors.
	var baseline = listener.duplicate(true)
	for person in old_effects:
		baseline.suspicion[person] = snappedf(baseline.suspicion.get(person, 0.1) - old_effects[person].applied, 0.000000001)
	baseline.ledger = {}
	var primary_listener = baseline.duplicate(true)
	var result = _assess_target(view, case_data, primary_listener, context)
	var signature_case = Graph.compile_case(view, case_data)
	signature_case.conclusion = {"type": "evil", "target": "all", "at": case_data.conclusion.at}
	var common_key = JSON.stringify(_canonical([_signature(view, signature_case), baseline.suspicion, baseline.npc_trust,
		baseline.knowledge, baseline.reading_reliability, baseline.trust, baseline.confidence,
		baseline.get("confidence_mode"), baseline.get("reasoning_mode"), baseline.get("insight")]))
	var consequences = Consequences.assess(view, case_data, baseline, _pattern, common_key, result.theory)
	var effects = {}
	for person in view.people:
		var id: String = person.id
		var entry: Dictionary = consequences.effects[id].duplicate(true)
		entry["baseline"] = baseline.suspicion.get(id, 0.1)
		entry["before"] = snappedf(before.get(id, 0.1), 0.000000001)
		entry["after"] = snappedf(clampf(entry.baseline + entry.requested, 0.0, 1.5), 0.000000001)
		entry["applied"] = snappedf(entry.after - entry.baseline, 0.000000001)
		entry["delta"] = snappedf(entry.after - entry.before, 0.000000001)
		entry["previous"] = old_effects.get(id, {}).get("applied", 0.0)
		effects[id] = entry
		listener.suspicion[id] = entry.after
	var target: String = case_data.conclusion.target
	listener.ledger[case_data.id] = primary_listener.ledger[case_data.id].duplicate(true)
	listener.ledger[case_data.id].effects = effects.duplicate(true)
	listener.ledger[case_data.id].applied = effects[target].applied
	listener.case_memory = primary_listener.case_memory.duplicate(true)
	listener.theories = consequences.theories
	# The headline's original confidence decision remains the reported decision.
	var theory_key: String = target + "|" + case_data.conclusion.at
	if case_data.conclusion.type == "marked":
		theory_key += "|marked"
	if primary_listener.theories.has(theory_key):
		listener.theories[theory_key] = primary_listener.theories[theory_key].duplicate(true)
	result["effects"] = effects
	result["claims"] = consequences.claims
	result["effect_rule"] = consequences.rule
	result.before_suspicion = effects[target].before
	result.after_suspicion = effects[target].after
	result.suspicion_delta = effects[target].delta
	result.calculation.old_case_effect = effects[target].previous
	result.calculation.new_case_effect = effects[target].applied
	result.calculation.rule = consequences.rule
	# Speak about accepted intermediate inferences separately from the headline.
	if not result.repeated:
		for claim in consequences.claims:
			if claim.conclusion.target != target and claim.conclusion.type == "evil" and claim.status == "entailed_in_attention" and claim.forced_mass > 0:
				result.response += " The reports I granted implicate %s, if sound. That conclusion does not by itself settle %s's alignment." % [Solver.person_name(view, claim.conclusion.target), Solver.person_name(view, target)]
				break
	return result

static func applied_to(entry: Dictionary, person: String) -> float:
	if entry.has("effects"):
		return float(entry.effects.get(person, {}).get("applied", 0.0))
	return float(entry.get("applied", 0.0)) if entry.get("target") == person else 0.0

static func _assess_target(view: Dictionary, case_data: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, context: Dictionary = {}) -> Dictionary:
	var span = Profiler.open("headline")
	var result = _assess_target_inner(view, case_data, listener, context)
	Profiler.close(span)
	return result

static func _assess_target_inner(view: Dictionary, case_data: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, context: Dictionary = {}) -> Dictionary:
	var original = case_data.duplicate(true)
	case_data = Graph.compile_case(view, case_data)
	var conclusion: Dictionary = case_data.conclusion
	var target: String = conclusion.target
	var before_suspicion: float = listener.suspicion.get(target, 0.1)
	var before_trust: float = listener.trust
	var old: Dictionary = listener.ledger.get(case_data.id, {})
	# Assess from the baseline without this case's own previous persuasion. Otherwise
	# accusing a witness would lower their credibility on every repetition.
	if not old.is_empty():
		listener.suspicion[old.target] = snappedf(listener.suspicion.get(old.target, 0.1) - old.applied, 0.000000001)
	var previous: Dictionary = listener.case_memory.get(case_data.id, {})
	var signature = _signature(view, case_data)
	var input_key = JSON.stringify(_canonical([signature, listener.get("reasoning_mode", "full"), listener.get("insight", 0.5),
		listener.get("confidence_mode", "scale"), listener.confidence, listener.trust, listener.npc_trust,
		listener.suspicion, listener.reading_reliability, listener.knowledge]))
	var repeated: bool = previous.get("input_key", "") == input_key
	var knowledge: Dictionary = listener.knowledge
	var full_case = case_data.duplicate(true)
	for id in knowledge.get("own_reports", []):
		if id not in full_case.cards:
			full_case.cards.append(id)
	var assessment = Solver.analyze(view, full_case, knowledge)
	var shared = Solver.analyze(view, case_data)
	var overall = Solver.solve(view, Solver.selected(view, full_case), {}, {}, knowledge)
	var logical_cards: Array = Solver.selected(view, case_data).filter(func(c): return c.kind == "report" and c.role in ["surveyor", "rumormonger", "dowser"])
	var best = {"weight": 0.0, "agreement": 0.0, "quality": 0.0, "cards": [], "kind": "none"}
	# Consider independent support paths. Adding an optional premise cannot erase an
	# already valid smaller path; a contradiction in the asserted case can reopen it.
	# The legacy diagnostic enumerates every report subset. Live comparisons already
	# retain the real Argue result; avoid computing this superseded experiment too.
	var diagnostic_paths: int = 1 if Solver.Domain.live(view) else 1 << logical_cards.size()
	for mask in range(1, diagnostic_paths):
		var path: Array = []
		for i in range(logical_cards.size()):
			if mask & (1 << i):
				path.append(logical_cards[i].id)
		var subcase = Solver.empty_case()
		subcase.cards = path.duplicate()
		subcase.conclusion = conclusion
		for id in knowledge.get("own_reports", []):
			if id not in subcase.cards:
				subcase.cards.append(id)
		var analysis = Solver.analyze(view, subcase, knowledge)
		var agreement = 1.0 if analysis.entailment == "forced" else 0.0
		if analysis.entailment == "possible":
			agreement = _possibility_agreement(view, overall.worlds, conclusion, listener)
		if conclusion.type == "conflict":
			agreement = 0.0 # handled using the whole asserted set below
		var quality = _quality(view, path, listener)
		var weight = agreement * quality
		if weight > best.weight:
			best = {"weight": weight, "agreement": agreement, "quality": quality, "cards": path, "kind": "reading"}
		if shared.entailment == "possible" and conclusion.type != "conflict":
			var positions: Dictionary = case_data.positions.duplicate(true)
			positions.erase(target)
			var answers: Dictionary = case_data.answers.duplicate(true)
			if conclusion.type == "marked":
				for id in answers.keys():
					if Solver.card_by_id(view, id).get("role", "") == "rumormonger":
						answers.erase(id)
			var conditional_path = Solver.solve(view, Solver.selected(view, subcase), positions, answers, knowledge)
			if not conditional_path.worlds.is_empty() and conditional_path.worlds.all(func(w): return Solver.holds(view, w, conclusion)):
				if 0.4 * quality > best.weight:
					best = {"weight": 0.4 * quality, "agreement": 0.4, "quality": quality, "cards": path, "kind": "conditional"}
	var pattern = _pattern(view, case_data, listener)
	var legacy = best.duplicate(true)
	if Solver.Domain.live(view):
		legacy["omitted"] = true
	var reasoning = Support.explain(view, original, listener)
	if listener.get("reasoning_mode", "full") == "bounded":
		var attended: Dictionary = Reasoner.prepare(view, original, listener).case
		attended.cards = attended.cards.filter(func(id): return id not in reasoning.private_checks.rejected)
		pattern = _pattern(view, attended, listener)
		var considered_facts: Array = reasoning.considered.map(func(id): return Solver.semantic_key(Solver.card_by_id(view, id)))
		considered_facts.sort()
		input_key += JSON.stringify([considered_facts, reasoning.connections])
		repeated = previous.get("input_key", "") == input_key
	var theory = Theory.prepare(view, case_data, listener, reasoning, input_key)
	var combined = Support.assess(view, original, listener, theory.bias)
	var paths: Array = []
	for path in combined.paths:
		for id in path:
			if id not in paths:
				paths.append(id)
	if paths.is_empty():
		paths = logical_cards.map(func(c): return c.id)
	best = {"weight": combined.agreement, "agreement": combined.agreement, "quality": 1.0,
		"cards": paths, "kind": "conditional" if combined.conditional_mass > combined.forced_mass else "reading"}
	if conclusion.type == "suspicious" and pattern.weight > best.weight:
		best = pattern
	if conclusion.type == "suspicious" and Movement.available(view) and combined.evidence_agreement > 0:
		best.agreement = maxf(best.agreement, 0.5)
		best.weight = maxf(best.weight, 0.5)
	var conflict = assessment.status == "conflicting"
	if conclusion.type == "conflict":
		var detected: bool = (reasoning.status == "inconsistent" or not reasoning.private_checks.rejected.is_empty()) if listener.get("reasoning_mode", "full") == "bounded" else assessment.entailment == "forced"
		var agreement = 1.0 if detected else 0.0
		best = {"weight": agreement * _quality(view, case_data.cards, listener), "agreement": agreement, "quality": _quality(view, case_data.cards, listener), "cards": case_data.cards, "kind": "conflict"}
	elif listener.get("reasoning_mode", "full") != "bounded" and (not assessment.errors.is_empty() or (assessment.proposed == 0 and assessment.compatible > 0)):
		best.weight = 0.0
		best.agreement = 0.0
	if reasoning.private_checks.fatal:
		best.weight = 0.0
		best.agreement = 0.0
	var player_factor: float = 0.5 + listener.trust
	var confidence_factor: float = 1.0 if listener.get("confidence_mode", "scale") == "persistence" else 1.4 - 0.8 * listener.confidence
	# Venue is deliberately the same: no private/public/nomination duplicate effects.
	var strength: float = best.weight * player_factor * confidence_factor
	var coefficient: float = {"evil": 0.45, "good": -0.4, "marked": -0.1, "suspicious": 0.3, "conflict": 0.0}.get(conclusion.type, 0.0)
	var effect: float = strength * coefficient
	# Replace this case's complete prior effect, even if target/conclusion changed.
	var baseline: float = listener.suspicion.get(target, 0.1)
	listener.suspicion[target] = snappedf(clampf(baseline + effect, 0.0, 1.5), 0.000000001)
	var applied: float = snappedf(listener.suspicion[target] - baseline, 0.000000001)
	listener.ledger[case_data.id] = {"target": target, "applied": applied, "cards": best.cards.duplicate(), "version": case_data.version,
		"conclusion": conclusion.duplicate(true), "signature": signature, "report_acceptance": combined.report_acceptance.duplicate(true)}
	# No recurring trust reward for restating a case. Workshop trust is a controlled input.
	var changes = _changes(view, previous.get("case", {}), case_data)
	listener.case_memory[case_data.id] = {"signature": signature, "input_key": input_key, "version": case_data.version, "cards": case_data.cards.duplicate(), "strength": strength, "case": case_data.duplicate(true)}
	var objection = _objection(view, case_data, listener, shared, assessment, best, pattern)
	var stance = "accept" if best.agreement >= 0.6 else ("partial" if best.agreement >= 0.35 else "reject")
	var delta: float = listener.suspicion[target] - before_suspicion
	var response = ""
	if repeated:
		response = "That is the same case. Repeating it adds no new reason."
	elif conclusion.type == "conflict":
		response = "Those accounts cannot all be accurate. I have reopened which report to believe; this does not establish anyone's alignment." if best.agreement > 0 else "I don't see a conflict among the reports you selected."
	elif best.kind == "pattern" and best.weight > 0:
		response = "That gives me a reason to be suspicious, though it does not establish evil."
	elif conflict and conclusion.type != "conflict":
		response = "I cannot accept all those premises together. I give weight only to the explanations that survive doubting a report." if best.weight > 0 else "Those premises or choices do not fit together. I have withdrawn this case's earlier weight."
	elif best.kind == "conditional":
		response = "That follows if I grant your extra choices. You still need to establish those choices."
	elif stance == "accept" and Movement.available(view) and assessment.entailment == "possible":
		response = "These observations make your explanation convincing to me, though an alternative remains logically possible."
	elif stance == "accept":
		response = "The conclusion follows if those reports are sound. My doubt is in the premises."
	elif combined.agreement < 0:
		response = "The supplied evidence weighs against that conclusion."
	elif assessment.entailment == "forced":
		response = "The inference follows if the reports are accurate. I give it some weight, but I still doubt the supporting reports."
	else:
		response = "I can see that possibility, but the reports also allow another explanation."
	if not combined.factors.is_empty():
		if combined.factors.any(func(label): return label.begins_with("Movement / trace")):
			response += " The routes, observation coverage and traces change which explanation I favor. Lawful activity and eligible passers remain alternatives; this is circumstantial evidence."
		else:
			response += " If the linked observation is accurate, the home alibi is false. That is circumstantial evidence, not proof of deliberate deception or evil." if combined.factors.any(func(label): return label.begins_with("Challenged home alibi")) else " The supplied test factor changes which remaining explanation I favor; it does not prove it."
	if listener.get("reasoning_mode", "full") == "bounded":
		objection = _bounded_objection(reasoning)
		if reasoning.private_checks.fatal:
			response = reasoning.private_checks.objections[0]
		elif conclusion.type == "conflict":
			response = "The accounts I considered cannot all be accurate. That does not identify anyone as evil." if best.agreement > 0 else "I have not established a conflict among the accounts I considered."
		elif best.weight < 0:
			response = "The evidence I considered weighs against this conclusion; my search was limited."
		elif not reasoning.support.is_empty() and reasoning.status == "provisional":
			response = "Your explanation fits what I considered. I am provisionally persuaded, but I have not checked another explanation." if stance == "accept" else "I found your explanation coherent, but I am not convinced by its support. I have not checked another explanation."
		elif reasoning.status == "rival_found":
			var alternative = Solver.describe_world(view, reasoning.rival, conclusion.at)
			response = "I checked another explanation and it also fits: %s evil; %s Marked. I cannot settle it from this alone." % [", ".join(PackedStringArray(alternative.evil.map(func(id): return Solver.person_name(view, id)))), Solver.person_name(view, alternative.marked)]
			if Movement.available(view) and stance == "accept":
				response = "I checked another explanation that also fits, but the movement and trace evidence makes your explanation more convincing. This is persuasion, not a proof that the alternative is impossible."
		elif reasoning.status == "entailed_in_attention":
			response = "The reports I considered force this if they are accurate."
		else:
			response = "I could not follow your explanation through within what I considered. That does not prove it wrong."
		if not reasoning.ignored.is_empty() or not reasoning.ignored_lines.is_empty():
			response += " I did not combine all the evidence in this case."
		if repeated:
			response = "That is the same case; I made the same checks. Repeating it adds no new reason."
	if not reasoning.private_checks.objections.is_empty():
		objection = " ".join(PackedStringArray(reasoning.private_checks.objections))
	if listener.get("reasoning_mode", "full") == "bounded" and combined.factors.any(func(label): return label.begins_with("Challenged home alibi")):
		response += " The linked observation challenges the home alibi; it does not by itself prove evil."
	if theory.mode == "persistence" and not repeated:
		response += " " + theory.note
	if not previous.is_empty() and not repeated and not changes.is_empty():
		response += " " + " ".join(PackedStringArray(changes))
	if not case_data.get("faults", {}).is_empty():
		response += " Your explanation needs impairment in the selected interval. That remains a hypothesis; readings outside it keep their own support."
	return {"listener": listener.id, "name": listener.name, "version": case_data.version, "stance": stance,
		"agreement": best.agreement, "quality": best.quality, "strength": strength, "path": best.cards,
		"path_kind": best.kind, "repeated": repeated, "response": response, "objection": objection, "changes": changes,
		"assessment": assessment, "shared_assessment": shared, "pattern": pattern, "support_model": combined, "legacy_path": legacy, "reasoning": reasoning, "theory": theory,
		"before_suspicion": before_suspicion, "after_suspicion": listener.suspicion[target], "suspicion_delta": delta,
		"before_trust": before_trust, "after_trust": listener.trust, "trust_delta": 0.0,
		"calculation": {"agreement": best.agreement, "premise_quality": best.quality, "player_factor": player_factor,
			"confidence_factor": confidence_factor, "coefficient": coefficient, "old_case_effect": old.get("applied", 0.0), "new_case_effect": applied,
			"venue": context.get("venue", "private"), "rule": "Replace this case's prior effect; never add it again. Trust is held fixed for controlled comparisons."}}

static func _bounded_objection(reasoning: Dictionary) -> String:
	# Spoken objections use only this search, never the referee's alternative.
	var result: String = {"provisional": "I have not established that this is the only explanation.",
		"rival_found": "Both explanations fit the reports I granted for this comparison.",
		"entailed_in_attention": "This follows within the reports I considered, if they are accurate.",
		"inconsistent": "The accounts I checked cannot all be accurate together.",
		"counterexample_found": "I checked an alternative, but did not establish your proposed explanation.",
		"unresolved": "I could not make all the pieces fit, but I may be missing something."}.get(reasoning.status, "")
	if not reasoning.doubted.is_empty():
		result += " I still doubt these reports: " + ", ".join(PackedStringArray(reasoning.doubted)) + "."
	return result

static func _quality(view: Dictionary, ids: Array, listener: Dictionary) -> float:
	var sources: Array = []
	var quality = 1.0
	for id in ids:
		var card = Solver.card_by_id(view, id)
		if card.is_empty():
			continue
		var source: String = card.source
		if source == listener.id or source in sources:
			continue
		sources.append(source)
		if source == "player":
			quality *= 0.9
		else:
			quality *= clampf(0.2 + 0.65 * listener.npc_trust.get(source, 0.5) - 0.35 * listener.suspicion.get(source, 0.1), 0.05, 0.95)
	return quality

static func _possibility_agreement(view: Dictionary, worlds: Array, conclusion: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary) -> float:
	# Compare the listener's strongest supporting and rival explanations, as Argue
	# does. This is an uncalibrated disposition score, not a fraction of worlds.
	var supporting = 0.0
	var rival = 0.0
	for world in worlds:
		var score = 1.0
		for i in range(view.people.size()):
			var id: String = view.people[i].id
			var suspicion: float = listener.suspicion.get(id, 0.1)
			var trust: float = listener.npc_trust.get(id, 0.5)
			if world.evil & (1 << i):
				score *= maxf(0.15 + suspicion * 1.2 - (trust - 0.5) * 0.5, 0.02)
			else:
				score *= maxf(0.3 + (0.6 - suspicion) * 0.6 + (trust - 0.5) * 0.4, 0.05)
		if Solver.holds(view, world, conclusion):
			supporting = maxf(supporting, score)
		else:
			rival = maxf(rival, score)
	return 0.02 + 0.16 * supporting / maxf(supporting + rival, 0.000000001)

static func _pattern(view: Dictionary, case_data: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	if Movement.available(view):
		return {"weight": 0.0, "agreement": 0.0, "quality": 0.0, "cards": [], "kind": "joint_movement", "incidents": 0, "alibi_conflicts": []}
	var target: String = case_data.conclusion.target
	var incidents = {}
	var contradictions: Array = []
	var cards = Solver.selected(view, case_data)
	for card in cards:
		var out: bool = (card.kind == "sighting" and card.subject == target) or (card.kind == "report" and card.get("role", "") == "nightwatch" and target in card.data.seen)
		if not out:
			continue
		var key = "%s:%d" % [target, card.night]
		var q = _quality(view, [card.id], listener)
		if not incidents.has(key) or q > incidents[key].quality:
			incidents[key] = {"quality": q, "card": card.id, "night": card.night}
	var weight = 0.0
	var path: Array = []
	for key in incidents:
		var incident: Dictionary = incidents[key]
		var explanation: String = case_data.reasons.get(key, "unexplained")
		var explained = 0.0
		if explanation == "gift":
			var claim = Graph.valid_citation(view, case_data, target, incident.night)
			if not claim.is_empty():
				explained = 0.8 * _quality(view, [claim.id], listener)
				path.append(claim.id)
		# Merely selecting 'gift' without supporting testimony is not an explanation.
		weight += 0.3 * incident.quality * (1.0 - explained)
		path.append(incident.card)
		for card in cards:
			if card.kind == "alibi" and card.subject == target and card.night == incident.night:
				weight += 0.2 * minf(incident.quality, _quality(view, [card.id], listener))
				contradictions.append(card.id)
				path.append(card.id)
	return {"weight": minf(weight, 0.8), "agreement": 0.5 if weight > 0 else 0.0, "quality": minf(weight / 0.5, 1.0),
		"cards": path, "kind": "pattern", "incidents": incidents.size(), "alibi_conflicts": contradictions}

static func _objection(view: Dictionary, case_data: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, shared: Dictionary, assessment: Dictionary, best: Dictionary, pattern: Dictionary) -> String:
	if best.kind == "pattern" and best.weight > 0:
		if not pattern.alibi_conflicts.is_empty():
			return "The outing conflicts with the home alibi. Someone's account is wrong; that adds suspicion but does not identify evil."
		return "These are %d distinct outings. A credible night gift can explain an outing; seeing someone twice on one night is one incident." % pattern.incidents
	if assessment.status == "conflicting":
		if not assessment.errors.is_empty():
			return assessment.errors[0]
		if shared.status != "conflicting":
			return "That conflicts with my own gift or my knowledge that I am good."
		return "The asserted reports, answers or home alibis cannot all hold together. Remove or challenge a premise."
	if best.kind == "conditional":
		return "Your selected people narrow the world; choosing an answer does not establish it."
	if best.quality < 0.45 and not best.cards.is_empty():
		var weakest = ""
		var lowest = 2.0
		for id in best.cards:
			var q = _quality(view, [id], listener)
			if q < lowest:
				lowest = q
				weakest = id
		return "I am least convinced by %s's report (%s); its source and health assumptions still matter." % [Solver.person_name(view, Solver.card_by_id(view, weakest).source), weakest]
	if not assessment.alternative.is_empty() and assessment.entailment == "possible":
		var a: Dictionary = assessment.alternative
		return "An alternative still fits: %s evil; %s Marked." % [", ".join(PackedStringArray(a.evil.map(func(id): return Solver.person_name(view, id)))), Solver.person_name(view, a.marked)]
	if assessment.entailment == "unsupported":
		return "The accepted reports exclude that conclusion."
	return "This depends on the named sources being honest, holding their claimed gifts and being healthy at the reported times."

static func _signature(view: Dictionary, case_data: Dictionary) -> String:
	var keys: Array = Solver.selected(view, case_data).map(func(c): return Solver.semantic_key(c))
	keys.sort()
	var answers = {}
	for card in Solver.selected(view, case_data):
		var positive: Array = case_data.answers.get(card.id, {}).get("positive", []).duplicate()
		if not positive.is_empty():
			positive.sort()
			answers[Solver.semantic_key(card)] = positive
	# Ignore wrapper ids, card/slot order, version and venue.
	var citations = {}
	for key in case_data.get("citations", {}):
		citations[key] = Solver.semantic_key(Solver.card_by_id(view, case_data.citations[key]))
	var scope: Array = Graph.roots(case_data, case_data.get("conflict_scope", [])).map(func(id): return Solver.semantic_key(Solver.card_by_id(view, id)))
	scope.sort()
	var factors: Array = []
	for factor in case_data.get("test_factors", []):
		if not is_zero_approx(float(factor.log2_ratio)):
			var routes: Array = []
			for route in factor.routes:
				var facts: Array = route.map(func(id): return Solver.semantic_key(Solver.card_by_id(view, id)))
				facts.sort()
				routes.append(JSON.stringify(facts))
			routes.sort()
			factors.append(JSON.stringify([factor.target, factor.log2_ratio, factor.events, routes]))
	factors.sort()
	var timed: Array = []
	for assignment in case_data.get("temporal_positions", []):
		for id in assignment.positions:
			var atom = [assignment.at, id, assignment.positions[id]]
			if atom not in timed:
				timed.append(atom)
	timed.sort_custom(func(a, b): return JSON.stringify(a) < JSON.stringify(b))
	return JSON.stringify(_canonical([keys, case_data.positions, answers, case_data.reasons, citations, scope, case_data.get("challenges", {}), factors, case_data.conclusion, case_data.get("graph_errors", []), case_data.get("faults", {}), timed]))

static func _canonical(value):
	if value is Dictionary:
		var result = {}
		var keys = value.keys()
		keys.sort()
		for key in keys:
			result[key] = _canonical(value[key])
		return result
	if value is Array:
		return value.map(func(item): return _canonical(item))
	return value

static func _changes(view: Dictionary, previous: Dictionary, current: Dictionary) -> Array:
	var changes: Array = []
	if previous.is_empty():
		return changes
	for id in current.cards:
		if id not in previous.cards:
			var card = Solver.card_by_id(view, id)
			changes.append("You added %s's %s." % [Solver.person_name(view, card.source), card.get("role", card.kind)])
	for id in previous.cards:
		if id not in current.cards:
			var card = Solver.card_by_id(view, id)
			if card.is_empty():
				changes.append("You removed a previously cited report.")
			else:
				changes.append("You removed %s's %s." % [Solver.person_name(view, card.source), card.get("role", card.kind)])
	if previous.positions != current.positions:
		changes.append("You changed the assumed alignments or Marked identity.")
	if previous.answers != current.answers:
		changes.append("You changed the answers on your cards.")
	if previous.reasons != current.reasons:
		changes.append("You changed the explanations for the outings.")
	if previous.conclusion != current.conclusion:
		changes.append("You changed the conclusion.")
	return changes

==> workshop/ledger_audit.gd <==
## Diagnose overlapping ownership before a shared belief ledger can replace old effects.
## Coarse source/night links identify potential overlap, never permission to delete it.
extends RefCounted

static func inspect(snapshot: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var readings = {}
	for card in snapshot.get("evidence", []):
		if card.kind != "info":
			continue
		var legacy_id = "info:%s:%d:%s" % [card.source, card.observed_at.day, card.content.role]
		if not readings.has(legacy_id):
			readings[legacy_id] = []
		readings[legacy_id].append(card.id)
	var result = {"reading_entries": [], "potential_arguments": [], "aliases": [],
		"safe_to_apply_additively": false,
		"note": "The old ledger can already include these reports and dependent arguments. Overlap must be replaced, not added again; coarse source/night links cannot safely identify every original contribution."}
	for id in readings:
		if readings[id].size() > 1:
			result.aliases.append({"legacy_id": id, "facts": readings[id]})
	for entry in snapshot.get("listener", {}).get("legacy_evidence", []):
		if entry.kind == "reading" and readings.has(entry.id):
			result.reading_entries.append({"legacy_id": entry.id, "facts": readings[entry.id]})
		elif entry.kind in ["arg", "tell"]:
			var supports: Array = entry.supports.duplicate()
			if not entry.rel.is_empty():
				supports.append(entry.rel)
			var matches: Array = []
			for support in supports:
				if support.size() < 2:
					continue
				if support.size() > 2 and support[2] != "reading":
					continue
				for card in snapshot.get("evidence", []):
					if card.kind == "info" and card.source == support[0] and (support[1] == -1 or card.observed_at.day == support[1]) and card.id not in matches:
						matches.append(card.id)
			if not matches.is_empty():
				result.potential_arguments.append({"legacy_id": entry.id, "facts": matches, "certainty": "coarse_dependency"})
	return result

==> workshop/live_assessment.gd <==
## Restricted live comparison. Pure inputs/outputs; no simulation objects or effects.
extends RefCounted
const Solver = preload("res://workshop/solver.gd")
const Jury = preload("res://workshop/jury.gd")
const Domain = preload("res://workshop/world_domain.gd")
const LedgerAudit = preload("res://workshop/ledger_audit.gd")
const ROLES = ["surveyor", "rumormonger", "dowser"]
const CONCLUSIONS = ["evil", "good", "marked", "suspicious", "conflict"]

static func prepare(snapshot: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var result = {"ready": false, "issues": [], "view": {}, "case": {}, "listener": {},
		"scope": "Conditional reading comparison with legal original alignment/modifier worlds. Accepting a report assumes a good, correctly claimed, healthy source. No live movement likelihood or alignment-transition history is inferred. Venue is recorded; proposed effects use the unscaled Workshop response."}
	if snapshot.get("format") != "demonologist-live-evidence-1":
		result.issues.append("Unknown live evidence format.")
		return result
	if snapshot.listener.is_empty():
		result.issues.append("Select a listener with an available good or cover mind.")
	if snapshot.people.size() not in [7, 10]:
		result.issues.append("Only seven- and ten-person live setups are profiled.")
	for issue in snapshot.issues:
		if issue.code in ["missing_card", "cyclic_argument", "argument_depth", "unknown_listener", "cover_unavailable", "invalid_venue"]:
			result.issues.append(issue.code + ": " + issue.detail)
	if snapshot.public_events.any(func(e): return e.kind in ["curse", "curse_lifted"]):
		result.issues.append("This live case has alignment-transition history that needs a temporal adapter.")
	var needed: Array = snapshot.selected.duplicate()
	for id in snapshot.private_evidence:
		if id not in needed:
			needed.append(id)
	var cards: Array = []
	for e in snapshot.evidence:
		if e.id not in needed:
			continue
		if e.kind in ["claim", "home"]:
			continue # Role assertions are not healthy readings; homes are in the public roster.
		if e.id not in snapshot.selected and e.kind == "sighting":
			continue # This comparison makes no movement claim or movement effect.
		if e.id not in snapshot.selected and e.kind == "info" and e.content.get("role", "") not in ROLES:
			continue # Independent unsupported readings keep their native baseline owner.
		if e.kind != "info" or e.content.get("role", "") not in ROLES:
			result.issues.append("Not yet supported: %s %s." % [e.kind, e.content.get("role", "")])
			continue
		var data: Dictionary = e.content.data
		var ids: Array = snapshot.people.map(func(p): return p.id)
		var role: String = e.content.role
		var valid = reading_valid(e, ids, snapshot.rules.modifier_options)
		if not valid:
			result.issues.append("Incomplete or invalid reading payload: " + e.id)
			continue
		cards.append({"id": e.id, "fact_id": e.fact_id, "kind": "report", "source": e.source, "role": role,
			"data": data.duplicate(true), "night": e.observed_at.day if e.observed_at.phase == "night" else -1,
			"observed_at": e.observed_at, "reported_at": e.reported_at, "learned_at": e.learned_at,
			"acquired_day": e.learned_at.day if e.learned_at != null else e.observed_at.day,
			"provenance": e.provenance, "text": e.text})
	if not cards.any(func(c): return c.id in snapshot.selected):
		result.issues.append("No supported reading is presented.")
	if cards.size() > 8 or snapshot.arguments.size() > 6:
		result.issues.append("This comparison exceeds the initial eight-reading / six-claim workload budget.")
	var epoch: String = "live through day %d" % snapshot.now.day
	var view = {"seed": 0, "day": snapshot.now.day, "people": snapshot.people.duplicate(true), "cards": cards,
		"evil_count": snapshot.rules.initial_evil_count, "alignment_epoch": epoch, "history": [],
		"world_options": {"modifiers": snapshot.rules.modifier_options.duplicate()}, "scope": result.scope}
	var root: Dictionary = snapshot.arguments.filter(func(a): return a.board_number == snapshot.root)[0] if snapshot.arguments.any(func(a): return a.board_number == snapshot.root) else {}
	if root.is_empty():
		result.issues.append("Select a filed deduction as the comparison's headline claim.")
	else:
		if snapshot.arguments.any(func(a): return a.board_number != root.board_number and a.has("workshop_case")):
			result.issues.append("A canvas graph cited inside a different filed deduction needs a graph composition adapter.")
		var value = _case(snapshot, root, epoch, [])
		value["arguments"] = []
		for argument in snapshot.arguments:
			var scoped = _case(snapshot, argument, epoch, [])
			var inputs: Array = []
			var direct: Array = argument.supports.filter(func(number): return snapshot.board_map.has(str(number))).map(func(number): return snapshot.board_map[str(number)])
			if not direct.is_empty():
				inputs.append({"id": "direct:%d" % argument.board_number, "cards": direct})
			for child in snapshot.arguments.filter(func(a): return a.board_number in argument.supports):
				inputs.append({"id": "argument:%d" % child.board_number, "cards": _case(snapshot, child, epoch, []).cards})
			value.arguments.append({"id": "argument:%d" % argument.board_number, "label": "Filed claim #%d" % argument.board_number,
				"case": scoped, "inputs": inputs, "parents": snapshot.arguments.filter(func(a): return argument.board_number in a.supports).map(func(a): return "argument:%d" % a.board_number)})
		result.case = value
		if root.has("workshop_case"):
			result.case = root.workshop_case.duplicate(true)
			result.case.id = "live:" + snapshot.case_id
			result.view = view
			view.alignment_epoch = "Original alignment"
			result.issues.append_array(canvas_issues(root.workshop_case, snapshot))
		for argument in snapshot.arguments:
			var conclusion = argument.conclusion
			if conclusion.get("type") not in CONCLUSIONS or _target(conclusion) not in snapshot.people.map(func(p): return p.id):
				result.issues.append("Unsupported claim: " + str(conclusion.get("type", "unknown")))
		if not snapshot.listener.is_empty():
			result.listener = _listener(snapshot, cards)
			var uncertain = cards.filter(func(c): return c.source != snapshot.listener.id).map(func(c): return c.source)
			var sources = {}
			for source in uncertain:
				sources[source] = true
			var discounts: int = result.listener.reading_reliability.values().filter(func(v): return v > 0 and v < 1).size()
			if sources.size() + discounts > 6:
				result.issues.append("This comparison exceeds the initial 64-state credibility budget.")
	result.view = view
	result.ready = result.issues.is_empty()
	return result

## Explicit capability gate: no graph feature may silently disappear when the
## restricted live evaluator replaces the native argument path.
static func canvas_issues(value: Dictionary, snapshot: Dictionary) -> Array:
	var issues: Array = []
	if value.cards != snapshot.selected and not (value.cards.size() == snapshot.selected.size() and value.cards.all(func(id): return id in snapshot.selected)):
		issues.append("Canvas roots differ from the original evidence receipts.")
	for field in ["reasons", "citations", "disputes", "faults", "temporal_positions", "derived", "challenges"]:
		if not value.get(field, {}).is_empty():
			issues.append("Not yet handled by the live evaluator: " + field + ".")
	var claims: Array = [value] + value.get("arguments", []).map(func(a): return a.case)
	if claims.size() > 7:
		issues.append("Canvas exceeds the initial six-claim budget.")
	for argument in value.get("arguments", []):
		if argument.id == value.get("argument_root") or argument.case.conclusion.type != "conflict":
			continue
		# The shared owner can distinguish quoted roots, but the scoped response's
		# attention still excludes them. Flag the missing joint interpretation.
		issues.append("Embedded conflict questions need a live quoted-evidence assessment adapter; present the conflict as its own headline to use the new evaluator.")
	var fields: Array = Solver.empty_case().keys() + ["arguments", "argument_root"]
	for claim in claims:
		for field in claim:
			if field not in fields:
				issues.append("Unrecognized canvas feature needs a live adapter: " + str(field) + ".")
		if claim.conclusion.get("type") not in CONCLUSIONS or claim.conclusion.get("target") not in snapshot.people.map(func(p): return p.id) or claim.conclusion.get("at") != "Original alignment":
			issues.append("Unsupported canvas claim or alignment time.")
		for line in claim.get("lines", []):
			if line.get("relation", "supports") not in ["supports", "quotes"] or line.has("test_factor"):
				issues.append("This reasoning-line relationship/factor has no live adapter.")
	for receipt in snapshot.evidence:
		if receipt.id in snapshot.selected and receipt.kind in ["claim", "home"]:
			issues.append("Not yet evaluated as a separate canvas premise: " + receipt.kind + ".")
		if receipt.id in snapshot.selected and receipt.kind == "info" and receipt.content.get("role") in ROLES:
			if not Domain.gift_time({"role": receipt.content.role, "observed_at": receipt.observed_at}):
				issues.append("Reading time is outside the live adapter's supported gift interval: " + receipt.content.role + ".")
	return issues

static func evaluate(snapshot: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var started = Time.get_ticks_msec()
	var prepared = prepare(snapshot)
	var result = {"ready": prepared.ready, "issues": prepared.issues, "scope": prepared.scope,
		"format": "demonologist-live-comparison-1", "listener": snapshot.get("listener", {}).get("id", ""),
		"proposal": {}, "elapsed_ms": 0}
	result["ledger_audit"] = LedgerAudit.inspect(snapshot)
	if not result.ledger_audit.aliases.is_empty():
		result.ready = false
		result.issues.append("Several statements share an unresolved original reading identity; numeric comparison is deferred.")
	if result.ready:
		result.proposal = Jury.evaluate(prepared.view, prepared.case, prepared.listener, {"venue": snapshot.venue})
		result["world_count"] = Domain.worlds(prepared.view).size()
	result.elapsed_ms = Time.get_ticks_msec() - started
	return result

static func _case(snapshot: Dictionary, argument: Dictionary, epoch: String, path: Array) -> Dictionary:
	var result = Solver.empty_case()
	result.id = "live:" + snapshot.case_id
	result.conclusion = {"type": argument.conclusion.get("type", "unsupported"), "target": _target(argument.conclusion), "at": epoch}
	if argument.board_number in path:
		return result
	var direct: Array = []
	for number in argument.supports:
		if snapshot.board_map.has(str(number)):
			var id: String = snapshot.board_map[str(number)]
			if snapshot.evidence.any(func(e): return e.id == id and e.kind == "info"):
				result.cards.append(id)
				direct.append(id)
		else:
			var child: Array = snapshot.arguments.filter(func(a): return a.board_number == number)
			if child.is_empty():
				continue
			var sub = _case(snapshot, child[0], epoch, path + [argument.board_number])
			result.cards.append_array(sub.cards.filter(func(id): return id not in result.cards))
			result.lines.append({"id": "argument:%d" % number, "label": "Supporting claim", "relation": "supports",
				"conclusion": sub.conclusion, "cards": sub.cards, "answers": {}, "positions": {}, "reasons": {}})
			if sub.conclusion.type == "conflict":
				result.lines[-1].relation = "quotes"
	if not direct.is_empty():
		# A quoted child may contain the same report as a direct endorsed path.
		# Preserve both routes so quoting cannot erase that independent endorsement.
		result.lines.append({"id": "direct:%d" % argument.board_number, "label": "Direct reports", "relation": "supports",
			"cards": direct, "answers": {}, "positions": {}, "reasons": {}})
	if result.conclusion.type == "conflict":
		result.conflict_scope = result.cards.duplicate()
	return result

static func _listener(snapshot: Dictionary, cards: Array) -> Dictionary:
	var src: Dictionary = snapshot.listener
	var reliability = {}
	var good: Array = [src.id]
	for event in snapshot.public_events:
		if event.kind in ["demon_death", "martyr"]:
			for person in event.subjects:
				if person not in good and person in snapshot.people.map(func(p): return p.id):
					good.append(person)
	for card in cards:
		reliability[card.id] = 1.0 - maxf(src.discounted.get("%s:%d" % [card.source, card.observed_at.day], 0.0), src.discounted.get(card.source + ":*", 0.0))
	return {"id": src.id, "name": src.name, "trust": src.trust, "confidence": src.confidence,
		"insight": src.insight, "reasoning_mode": "full", "confidence_mode": "scale", "theories": {},
		"npc_trust": src.npc_trust.duplicate(true), "suspicion": src.suspicion.duplicate(true),
		"reading_reliability": reliability, "knowledge": {"self": src.id, "good": good, "own_role": src.own_gift,
			"own_reports": cards.filter(func(c): return c.id in snapshot.private_evidence).map(func(c): return c.id), "own_outings": {}},
		"ledger": {}, "case_memory": {}}

static func _target(conclusion: Dictionary) -> String:
	return conclusion.get("a", "") if conclusion.get("a", "") != "" else str(conclusion.get("group", [""])[0]) if not conclusion.get("group", []).is_empty() else ""

static func _count_valid(value, maximum: int) -> bool:
	return (value is int or value is float) and is_finite(float(value)) and value >= 0 and value <= maximum and int(value) == value

static func reading_valid(receipt: Dictionary, ids: Array, modifiers: Array) -> bool:
	if receipt.kind != "info" or receipt.content.get("role") not in ROLES or receipt.source not in ids or receipt.observed_at.day < 0 or receipt.observed_at.phase == "unknown":
		return false
	var data: Dictionary = receipt.content.data
	match receipt.content.role:
		"surveyor":
			return _count_valid(data.get("count"), 4)
		"dowser":
			return _group_valid(data.get("group"), ids) and _count_valid(data.get("count"), data.get("group", []).size())
		_:
			return _group_valid(data.get("pair"), ids) and data.get("pair", []).size() == 2 and data.get("secret") in modifiers

static func _group_valid(value, ids: Array) -> bool:
	if not value is Array or value.is_empty():
		return false
	var unique = {}
	for id in value:
		if id not in ids or unique.has(id):
			return false
		unique[id] = true
	return true

==> workshop/live_evidence.gd <==
## Versioned transport contract. No World/NPC access and no belief mutations.
## A future interpreter can consume the same receipts without using Workshop fixtures.
extends RefCounted

const FORMAT = "demonologist-live-evidence-1"
const REPORT_FIELDS = {
	"surveyor": ["count"], "dowser": ["group", "count", "block"],
	"hearthwitch": ["nbrs", "count"], "rumormonger": ["pair", "secret"],
	"nightwatch": ["block", "seen"], "lamplighter": ["count"],
	"seer": ["pair", "yes"], "archivist": ["pair", "role"], "clerk": ["pair", "role"],
	"gravedigger": ["executed", "role"], "psychologist": ["subject", "trusts", "suspects"],
	"veteran": ["attacked", "block"], "martyr": ["target", "chosen", "evil"],
}
const FIELDS = ["block", "loc", "home", "night", "role", "duty_claim", "duty", "vigil", "dropped",
	"nominee", "yes", "no", "alive_before", "owner", "material", "crime_night", "scene"]
const KINDS = ["home", "haunt", "info", "claim", "sighting", "conversation", "vigil", "alibi",
	"trace", "dropped", "totem", "death", "execution", "quiet", "vote", "martyr", "curse",
	"curse_lifted", "silenced", "knell", "secret", "hint", "refusal", "opinion", "accusation", "case"]

static func normalize(input: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var result = {"format": FORMAT, "case_id": input.case_id, "now": stamp(input.now),
		"rules": input.rules.duplicate(true), "people": input.people.duplicate(true),
		"venue": input.venue, "evidence": [], "selected": [], "board_map": {},
		"private_evidence": [], "public_events": input.public_events.duplicate(true),
		"arguments": [], "root": input.get("root", 0), "listener": input.get("listener", {}).duplicate(true),
		"issues": input.get("issues", []).duplicate(true), "assessment_ready": false}
	var by_id = {}
	for receipt in input.get("receipts", []):
		var card = normalize_card(receipt.card)
		var id: String = card.id
		if not by_id.has(id):
			by_id[id] = card
			result.evidence.append(card)
		if card.provenance not in by_id[id].provenance_paths:
			by_id[id].provenance_paths.append(card.provenance.duplicate(true))
		for disclosure in receipt.card.get("learned", []):
			var learned = {"at": stamp(disclosure), "via": disclosure.get("via", ""), "public": disclosure.get("public", false)}
			if learned not in by_id[id].disclosures:
				by_id[id].disclosures.append(learned)
		if by_id[id].learned_at == null and card.learned_at != null:
			by_id[id].learned_at = card.learned_at
		if by_id[id].reported_at == null and card.reported_at != null:
			by_id[id].reported_at = card.reported_at
		var number: int = receipt.card.get("n", 0)
		if receipt.get("private", false):
			if id not in result.private_evidence:
				result.private_evidence.append(id)
		else:
			result.board_map[str(number)] = id
			if number in input.get("selected", []) and id not in result.selected:
				result.selected.append(id)
		for issue in card.issues:
			_issue(result.issues, issue, id)
	for argument in input.get("arguments", []):
		result.arguments.append(argument.duplicate(true))
	# These are capability declarations, not guesses about this world's hidden state.
	_issue(result.issues, "modifier_alternatives", "", "Live setup permits Marked, Delusional, Lovers or Sleepwalker; the fixture solver assumes one Marked.")
	if result.public_events.any(func(e): return e.kind in ["curse", "curse_lifted", "execution"]):
		_issue(result.issues, "live_chronology", "", "Live event histories need an adapter; fixed fixture chronology is not sufficient.")
	if result.evidence.any(func(c): return c.kind in ["trace", "dropped", "vigil", "sighting", "alibi"]):
		_issue(result.issues, "live_movement", "", "Fixture activity priors and eligible routes have not been derived for this live case.")
	return result

static func normalize_card(card: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var kind: String = card.get("kind", "unknown")
	var observed = stamp(card.get("when", {"day": card.get("day", -1), "phase": card.get("phase", 3 if kind == "info" else -1)}))
	var content = {"kind": kind, "source": card.get("source", ""),
		"subjects": card.get("subject", []).duplicate(), "observed_at": observed}
	content.subjects.sort()
	for field in FIELDS:
		if card.has(field):
			content[field] = card[field].duplicate(true) if card[field] is Array or card[field] is Dictionary else card[field]
	var issues: Array = []
	if kind == "info":
		content["data"] = report_data(card.get("role", ""), card.get("data", {}))
		# The reading's payload identifies its subjects, regardless of whether it was
		# collected from a board card or the listener's private reading log.
		content.subjects = []
		for field in ["pair", "group", "nbrs", "seen"]:
			for person in content.data.get(field, []):
				if person not in content.subjects:
					content.subjects.append(person)
		for field in ["executed", "subject", "trusts", "suspects", "target"]:
			if content.data.has(field) and content.data[field] not in content.subjects:
				content.subjects.append(content.data[field])
		content.subjects.sort()
		if card.get("role", "") not in REPORT_FIELDS:
			issues.append("report_semantics_unsupported")
		issues.append("original_report_time_unknown")
	if kind not in KINDS:
		issues.append("evidence_kind_unsupported")
	if kind not in ["info", "claim", "sighting", "alibi", "conversation", "home", "dropped"] or "report_semantics_unsupported" in issues:
		# Several legacy cards carry semantics only in their visible text. Retain that
		# receipt; do not invent a structured interpretation or merge distinct scenes.
		content["statement"] = card.get("text", "")
		issues.append("structured_payload_incomplete")
	if observed.phase == "unknown" or observed.day < 0:
		issues.append("observation_time_unknown")
	var id = "fact:" + JSON.stringify(canonical(content)).sha256_text()
	var incidents: Array = []
	if observed.phase == "night":
		for person in content.subjects:
			if kind in ["sighting", "alibi"]:
				incidents.append("outing:%s:%d" % [person, observed.day])
		if kind == "info" and card.get("role") == "nightwatch":
			for person in content.data.get("seen", []):
				incidents.append("outing:%s:%d" % [person, observed.day])
	var result = {"id": id, "kind": kind, "source": content.source, "subjects": content.subjects,
		"observed_at": observed, "reported_at": null, "learned_at": null, "disclosures": [],
		"fact_id": id, "incident_ids": incidents, "content": content, "issues": issues, "provenance_paths": [],
		"text": card.get("text", ""), "provenance": {"original_source": content.source,
			"via": card.get("via", content.source), "origin_certainty": "legacy_receipt"}}
	if not card.get("learned", []).is_empty():
		result.learned_at = stamp(card.learned[0])
	# A non-relayed statement's first receipt is its first witnessed report. Relay time
	# cannot stand in for the original speaker's report time.
	if card.get("via", content.source) == content.source and result.learned_at != null:
		result.reported_at = result.learned_at.duplicate(true)
	return result

static func report_data(role: String, data: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var result = {}
	for key in REPORT_FIELDS.get(role, []):
		if data.has(key):
			result[key] = data[key].duplicate(true) if data[key] is Array or data[key] is Dictionary else data[key]
			if key in ["pair", "group", "nbrs", "seen"]:
				result[key].sort()
	return result

static func stamp(value: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var phase = value.get("phase", -1)
	return {"day": int(value.get("day", -1)), "phase": ["morning", "afternoon", "evening", "night"][phase] if phase is int and phase >= 0 and phase < 4 else "unknown"}

static func canonical(value):
	if value is Dictionary:
		var result = {}
		var keys: Array = value.keys()
		keys.sort()
		for key in keys:
			result[key] = canonical(value[key])
		return result
	if value is Array:
		return value.map(func(item): return canonical(item))
	return value

static func _issue(issues: Array, code: String, fact: String = "", detail: String = "") -> void:
	var item = {"code": code, "fact": fact, "detail": detail}
	if item not in issues:
		issues.append(item)

==> workshop/movement_model.gd <==
## Bounded generative experiment. Hypotheses persist across nights; observations
## of one incident are conjoined, not multiplied as independent outings.
extends RefCounted
const C = preload("res://sim/content.gd")
static var cache: Dictionary = {}

static func available(view: Dictionary) -> bool:
	return view.has("movement_experiment")

static func claim_refs(view: Dictionary, value: Dictionary) -> Array:
	if not available(view):
		return []
	return value.get("citations", {}).values()

static func evidence(view: Dictionary, cards: Array) -> Array:
	return cards.filter(func(c): return c.kind in ["sighting", "coverage", "evening", "trace", "item"] or (c.kind == "alibi" and c.get("quoted", false)) or c.get("role") == "nightwatch") if available(view) else []

static func factors(view: Dictionary, cards: Array, value: Dictionary) -> Array:
	if not available(view):
		return []
	var model: Dictionary = view.movement_experiment
	var result: Array = []
	for subject in model.subjects:
		var observed = evidence(view, cards).filter(func(c): return c.get("subject", "") == subject or subject in c.get("data", {}).get("seen", []))
		if observed.is_empty():
			continue
		var cited: Array = value.get("citations", {}).values()
		var gift: bool = cards.any(func(c): return c.id in cited and c.kind == "claim" and c.subject == subject and C.ROLES.get(c.get("role", ""), {}).get("night_walk", false))
		var secret: bool = cards.any(func(c): return c.id in cited and c.kind == "secret_claim" and c.subject == subject)
		var good = likelihood(view, observed, subject, false, gift, secret)
		var marked_good = likelihood(view, observed, subject, false, gift, secret, true)
		var evil = likelihood(view, observed, subject, true, gift, secret)
		result.append({"target": subject, "log2_ratio": log(maxf(evil, 1e-12) / maxf(good, 1e-12)) / log(2.0),
			"label": "Movement / trace comparison: " + subject, "good_likelihood": good, "good_marked_likelihood": marked_good, "evil_likelihood": evil,
			"cards": observed.map(func(c): return c.id), "origin": "movement_model"})
	return result

static func likelihood(view: Dictionary, cards: Array, subject: String, evil: bool, gift: bool = false, secret: bool = false, marked: bool = false) -> float:
	var rules: Dictionary = view.movement_experiment
	if marked:
		secret = false # Secret modifiers are exclusive in the actual NPC model.
	var inputs = [view.people, rules, cards, subject, evil, gift, secret, marked]
	var key = hash(inputs)
	if cache.has(key) and cache[key].inputs == inputs:
		return cache[key].value
	var profiles: Array = [{"kind": "hunt", "weight": 1.0 / 3.0}, {"kind": "minion", "weight": 2.0 / 3.0}] if evil else [
		{"kind": "ordinary", "weight": 0.75 if not gift and not secret else 0.2},
		{"kind": "gift", "weight": 0.15 if not gift and not secret else (0.7 if gift else 0.1)},
		{"kind": "secret", "weight": 0.1 if not gift and not secret else (0.7 if secret else 0.1)}]
	if marked and not evil:
		profiles = profiles.filter(func(p): return p.kind != "secret")
		var normalization = 0.0
		for profile in profiles:
			normalization += profile.weight
		for profile in profiles:
			profile.weight /= normalization
	var total = 0.0
	# A common evening haunt is one latent cause shared by repeated soot reports.
	for profile in profiles:
		for haunt in C.BLOCKS:
			var p: float = profile.weight / float(C.BLOCKS.size())
			var nights: Array = []
			for card in cards:
				if card.night not in nights:
					nights.append(card.night)
			for night in nights:
				var reports: Array = cards.filter(func(c): return c.night == night)
				var night_p = 0.0
				var out_rate: float = {"hunt": 1.0, "minion": 0.25, "gift": 1.0, "secret": 0.55, "ordinary": 0.05}[profile.kind]
				var eligible: Array = rules.gift_destinations[subject][str(int(night))]
				if profile.kind == "gift" and eligible.is_empty():
					out_rate = 0.0
				for out in [false, true]:
					for destination in C.BLOCKS:
						var dest_p: float = 1.0 / C.BLOCKS.size()
						if profile.kind == "hunt":
							dest_p = 1.0 if destination == rules.scenes[str(int(night))] else 0.0
						elif profile.kind == "gift" and not eligible.is_empty():
							dest_p = 1.0 / eligible.size() if destination in eligible else 0.0
						var home: String = view.people.filter(func(p0): return p0.id == subject)[0].home
						var path: Array = C.route(home, destination) if out else []
						var route_p: float = (out_rate if out else 1.0 - out_rate) * dest_p
						var incident = _incident(profile.kind, haunt, rules.get("haunt_visit", 0.7), out, path, reports)
						night_p += route_p * incident
				p *= night_p
			total += p
	if cache.size() > 256:
		cache.clear()
	cache[key] = {"inputs": inputs.duplicate(true), "value": total}
	return total

static func _incident(kind: String, haunt: String, haunt_visit: float, out: bool, path: Array, reports: Array) -> float:
	var total = 0.0
	for evening in C.BLOCKS:
		var incident: float = haunt_visit if evening == haunt else (1.0 - haunt_visit) / 3.0
		var facts: Array = []
		for card in reports:
			var fact_key: String = card.get("fact_id", card.id)
			if fact_key in facts:
				continue
			facts.append(fact_key)
			match card.kind:
				"sighting": incident *= 1.0 if out and card.block in path else 0.0
				"report":
					var block: String = card.get("block", card.data.get("block", ""))
					incident *= 1.0 if out and (block == "" or block in path) else 0.0
				"coverage":
					var detection: float = card.data.detection if out and card.block in path else 0.0
					incident *= detection if card.data.seen else 1.0 - detection
				"evening": incident *= 1.0 if card.block == evening else 0.0
				"alibi":
					# Both evil activity and a good private errand can explain concealment.
					incident *= {"hunt": 0.7, "minion": 0.7, "secret": 0.65, "gift": 0.15, "ordinary": 0.2}[kind] if out else 0.9
				"trace":
					var q: Dictionary = trace_distribution(evening, card.data.passers) if kind == "hunt" else passer_distribution(card.data.background, card.data.passers, out and card.data.scene_block in path, evening)
					incident *= q.get(card.data.material, 0.0)
				"item":
					var drop: float = (0.35 if kind == "hunt" else 0.15) * 0.12
					incident *= drop / maxf(path.size(), 1.0) / card.data.locations_in_block if out and card.block in path else 0.0
		total += incident
	return total

static func trace_distribution(demon_evening: String, passers: Array) -> Dictionary:
	var result = {}
	for block in C.BLOCKS:
		result[block] = 0.0
	result[demon_evening] = 1.0 if passers.is_empty() else 0.6
	for passer in passers:
		result[passer] += 0.4 / passers.size()
	return result

static func passer_distribution(background: Dictionary, passers: Array, subject_passed: bool, evening: String) -> Dictionary:
	var result = {}
	var eligible: Array = passers.duplicate()
	if subject_passed:
		eligible.append(evening)
	for demon_evening in background:
		var conditional = trace_distribution(demon_evening, eligible)
		for block in conditional:
			result[block] = result.get(block, 0.0) + background[demon_evening] * conditional[block]
	return result

==> workshop/profiler.gd <==
## Opt-in synchronous spans. Exclusive time avoids double-counting nested stages.
## No world/NPC references, RNG calls, timing thresholds or inference decisions.
extends RefCounted
static var enabled = false
static var stack: Array = []
static var rows: Dictionary = {}
static var counters: Dictionary = {}
static var started = 0

static func start() -> void:
	stack = []
	rows = {}
	counters = {}
	started = Time.get_ticks_usec()
	enabled = true

static func open(stage: String, listener: String = "") -> int:
	if not enabled:
		return -1
	if listener == "" and not stack.is_empty():
		listener = stack[-1].listener
	var path: String = stage if stack.is_empty() else stack[-1].path + "/" + stage
	stack.append({"stage": stage, "path": path, "listener": listener, "at": Time.get_ticks_usec(), "children": 0})
	return stack.size() - 1

static func close(token: int) -> void:
	if token < 0:
		return
	assert(token == stack.size() - 1, "Unbalanced profiling spans")
	var span: Dictionary = stack.pop_back()
	var elapsed: int = Time.get_ticks_usec() - span.at
	if not stack.is_empty():
		stack[-1].children += elapsed
	var key: String = span.listener + "|" + span.path
	if not rows.has(key):
		rows[key] = {"listener": span.listener, "stage": span.stage, "path": span.path, "calls": 0, "total_us": 0, "self_us": 0}
	rows[key].calls += 1
	rows[key].total_us += elapsed
	rows[key].self_us += elapsed - span.children

static func count(label: String, amount: int = 1) -> void:
	if not enabled:
		return
	var listener: String = stack[-1].listener if not stack.is_empty() else ""
	var path: String = stack[-1].path if not stack.is_empty() else ""
	var key = listener + "|" + path + "|" + label
	counters[key] = counters.get(key, 0) + amount

static func finish() -> Dictionary:
	assert(stack.is_empty(), "Profiling ended inside a span")
	enabled = false
	return {"format": "workshop-profile-1", "elapsed_us": Time.get_ticks_usec() - started,
		"rows": rows.values().duplicate(true), "counters": counters.duplicate(true)}

==> workshop/reasoner.gd <==
## Deterministic, bounded hypothesis testing. Never consumes the referee's worlds.
extends RefCounted
const Solver = preload("res://workshop/solver.gd")
const Graph = preload("res://workshop/case_graph.gd")
const TimeModel = preload("res://workshop/chronology.gd")
const Domain = preload("res://workshop/world_domain.gd")
const Profiler = preload("res://workshop/profiler.gd")
static var matches: Dictionary = {}
static var reuse_matches = true # developer A/B switch, not a reasoning-budget change

static func profile(insight: float) -> Dictionary:
	if insight < 0.34:
		return {"name": "Low", "cards": 3, "lines": 1, "support": 8, "rivals": 0, "complete": false}
	if insight < 0.67:
		return {"name": "Medium", "cards": 5, "lines": 2, "support": 48, "rivals": 16, "complete": false}
	return {"name": "High", "cards": 10, "lines": 6, "support": 840, "rivals": 840, "complete": true}

static func prepare(view: Dictionary, value: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var full = Graph.compile_case(view, value)
	if full.conclusion.type == "conflict":
		var scope = Graph.roots(value, full.get("conflict_scope", []))
		full.cards = full.cards.filter(func(id): return id in scope)
	var limits = profile(listener.get("insight", 0.5))
	var bounded: bool = listener.get("reasoning_mode", "full") == "bounded"
	var cards = Solver.selected(view, full)
	var groups: Array = []
	var assigned: Array = []
	for line in value.get("lines", []):
		if line.relation == "supports":
			var roots = Graph.roots(value, line.cards).filter(func(id): return id in full.cards)
			if roots.is_empty():
				continue
			assigned.append_array(roots)
			_add_group(view, groups, line.id, roots, full.conclusion.target, listener)
	var unassigned: Array = cards.filter(func(c): return c.id not in assigned).map(func(c): return c.id)
	if not unassigned.is_empty():
		_add_group(view, groups, "", unassigned, full.conclusion.target, listener)
	groups.sort_custom(func(a, b): return a.score > b.score if not is_equal_approx(a.score, b.score) else a.key < b.key)
	var keep: Array = []
	var kept_lines: Array = []
	var connections: Array = []
	for i in range(groups.size()):
		if bounded and i >= limits.lines:
			continue
		keep.append_array(groups[i].cards)
		kept_lines.append_array(groups[i].ids)
		connections.append(groups[i].key)
	var ranked: Array = cards.filter(func(c): return c.id in keep and c.id not in listener.knowledge.get("own_reports", []))
	ranked.sort_custom(func(a, b):
		var x = _salience(a, full.conclusion.target, listener)
		var y = _salience(b, full.conclusion.target, listener)
		return x > y if not is_equal_approx(x, y) else Solver.semantic_key(a) < Solver.semantic_key(b))
	keep = ranked.slice(0, limits.cards if bounded else ranked.size()).map(func(c): return c.id)
	for id in listener.knowledge.get("own_reports", []):
		if cards.any(func(c): return c.id == id):
			keep.append(id)
	var raw = value.duplicate(true)
	raw.cards = keep
	raw.derived = []
	raw.lines = raw.get("lines", []).filter(func(line): return line.relation == "supports" and line.id in kept_lines)
	for line in raw.lines:
		line.cards = Graph.roots(value, line.cards).filter(func(id): return id in keep)
	var attended = Graph.compile_case(view, raw)
	# Graph expansion must not reintroduce an unattended source or fault premise.
	attended.cards = keep
	attended.lines = []
	return {"case": attended, "profile": limits.name if bounded else "Full",
		"limits": limits, "considered": keep, "ignored": cards.filter(func(c): return c.id not in keep).map(func(c): return c.id),
		"lines": kept_lines, "connections": connections,
		"ignored_lines": value.get("lines", []).filter(func(line): return line.relation == "supports" and line.id not in kept_lines).map(func(line): return line.id),
		"private_reports": listener.knowledge.get("own_reports", []).duplicate(),
		"private_checks": direct_checks(view, full, listener.knowledge)}

static func _add_group(view: Dictionary, groups: Array, id: String, roots: Array, target: String, listener: Dictionary) -> void:
