	var facts: Array = roots.map(func(root): return Solver.semantic_key(Solver.card_by_id(view, root)))
	facts.sort()
	var key = JSON.stringify(facts)
	for group in groups:
		if group.key == key:
			group.ids.append(id)
			return
	groups.append({"ids": [id], "key": key, "cards": roots, "score": _group_score(view, roots, target, listener)})

static func _salience(card: Dictionary, target: String, listener: Dictionary) -> float:
	var score = 1.0 + listener.npc_trust.get(card.source, 0.5) * 0.1
	if card.kind == "report" and card.role in ["surveyor", "rumormonger", "dowser"]:
		score += 3.0
	if card.get("subject", "") == target or target in card.get("data", {}).get("pair", []) or target in card.get("data", {}).get("group", []) or target in card.get("data", {}).get("seen", []):
		score += 2.0
	return score

static func _group_score(view: Dictionary, ids: Array, target: String, listener: Dictionary) -> float:
	var score = 0.0
	for id in ids:
		var card = Solver.card_by_id(view, id)
		if not card.is_empty():
			score = maxf(score, _salience(card, target, listener))
	return score

static func search(view: Dictionary, case_data: Dictionary, cards: Array, listener: Dictionary, limits: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var span = Profiler.open("candidate_search")
	var result = _search(view, case_data, cards, listener, limits)
	Profiler.count("candidates_tested", result.tested)
	Profiler.close(span)
	return result

static func _search(view: Dictionary, case_data: Dictionary, cards: Array, listener: Dictionary, limits: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var local = _local_conflict(cards)
	if not local.is_empty() or not case_data.get("graph_errors", []).is_empty() or not Solver.validate_answers(view, case_data).is_empty():
		return {"status": "inconsistent", "support": {}, "rival": {}, "tested": 0, "support_tested": 0,
			"rivals_tested": 0, "rivals_exhausted": true, "support_exhausted": true, "limited": false, "local_conflict": local}
	var support = _find(view, cards, case_data, listener, limits.support, true, case_data.positions, case_data.answers)
	var rival = {"world": {}, "tested": 0, "exhausted": false}
	if limits.rivals > 0:
		if limits.complete or support.world.is_empty():
			rival = _find(view, cards, case_data, listener, limits.rivals, false, {}, {})
		else:
			rival = _swaps(view, cards, case_data, listener, support.world, limits.rivals)
	var result = "unresolved"
	if not support.world.is_empty():
		result = "rival_found" if not rival.world.is_empty() else ("entailed_in_attention" if rival.exhausted else "provisional")
	elif not rival.world.is_empty():
		result = "counterexample_found"
	elif support.exhausted and rival.exhausted:
		result = "inconsistent"
	return {"status": result, "support": support.world, "rival": rival.world, "tested": support.tested + rival.tested,
		"support_tested": support.tested, "rivals_tested": rival.tested, "rivals_exhausted": rival.exhausted,
		"support_exhausted": support.exhausted, "limited": not rival.exhausted}

static func _local_conflict(cards: Array) -> Array:
	# Direct two-account contradiction, not a complete multi-card theorem search.
	for home in cards:
		if home.kind != "alibi" or home.get("quoted", false):
			continue
		for sight in cards:
			if home.get("night", -1) != sight.get("night", -2):
				continue
			if (sight.kind == "sighting" and sight.subject == home.subject) or (sight.kind == "report" and sight.role == "nightwatch" and home.subject in sight.data.seen):
				return [home.id, sight.id]
	return []

static func _find(view: Dictionary, cards: Array, case_data: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, budget: int, want: bool, positions: Dictionary, answers: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	if Domain.live(view):
		return _find_live(view, cards, case_data, listener, budget, want, positions, answers)
	var ids: Array = Solver.person_ids(view)
	var good: Array = listener.knowledge.get("good", []).duplicate()
	for card in cards:
		if card.kind == "report" and card.source not in good:
			good.append(card.source)
	var marked_options = ids.duplicate()
	for card in cards:
		if card.kind == "report" and card.role == "rumormonger":
			marked_options = marked_options.filter(func(id): return id in card.data.pair)
	var target: String = case_data.conclusion.target
	marked_options.sort_custom(func(a, b):
		var prefer_target: bool = not want if case_data.conclusion.type in ["evil", "suspicious"] else want
		return a == target if prefer_target and (a == target or b == target) else (b == target if not prefer_target and (a == target or b == target) else a < b))
	var tested = 0
	var movement = Solver.movement_for(view, cards, answers, listener.knowledge)
	for marked in marked_options:
		var pool: Array = ids.filter(func(id): return id not in good and id != marked)
		pool.sort_custom(func(a, b):
			var x = _candidate_priority(a, target, cards, listener, want, case_data.conclusion.type)
			var y = _candidate_priority(b, target, cards, listener, want, case_data.conclusion.type)
			return x > y if not is_equal_approx(x, y) else a < b)
		for mask in range(1 << pool.size()):
			if Solver.popcount(mask) != view.evil_count:
				continue
			var evil = 0
			for i in range(pool.size()):
				if mask & (1 << i):
					evil |= 1 << ids.find(pool[i])
			var world = {"evil": evil, "marked": ids.find(marked)}
			for candidate in TimeModel.expand(view, world):
				if Solver.holds(view, candidate, case_data.conclusion) != want:
					continue
				if tested >= budget:
					return {"world": {}, "tested": tested, "exhausted": false}
				tested += 1
				if movement.okay and _matches(view, candidate, cards, listener.knowledge, positions, answers, case_data.conclusion.at, case_data.get("temporal_positions", [])):
					return {"world": candidate, "tested": tested, "exhausted": false}
	return {"world": {}, "tested": tested, "exhausted": true}

static func _candidate_priority(id: String, target: String, cards: Array, listener: Dictionary, want: bool, type: String) -> float:
	var score: float = listener.suspicion.get(id, 0.1)
	for card in cards:
		if card.kind == "report" and card.role == "dowser" and id in card.data.group:
			score += 2.0
	var evil_target: bool = want if type in ["evil", "suspicious"] else not want
	if id == target:
		score += 10.0 if evil_target else -10.0
	return score

static func _matches(view: Dictionary, world: Dictionary, cards: Array, knowledge: Dictionary, positions: Dictionary, answers: Dictionary, epoch: String = "", temporal_positions: Array = []) -> bool:
	if Domain.live(view) and not Domain.claims_compatible(cards, knowledge):
		return false
	for id in knowledge.get("good", []):
		if not Solver.position_holds(view, world, id, "good", knowledge.get("good_epoch", epoch)):
			return false
	for id in positions:
		if not Solver.position_holds(view, world, id, positions[id], epoch):
			return false
	for assignment in temporal_positions:
		for id in assignment.positions:
			if not Solver.position_holds(view, world, id, assignment.positions[id], assignment.at):
				return false
	for card in cards:
		if not Solver._report_holds(view, world, card, answers.get(card.id, {})):
			return false
	return true

static func _swaps(view: Dictionary, cards: Array, case_data: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, seed: Dictionary, budget: int) -> Dictionary:
	if Domain.live(view):
		return _find_live(view, cards, case_data, listener, budget, false, {}, {})
	var ids: Array = Solver.person_ids(view)
	var evil: Array = ids.filter(func(id): return seed.evil & (1 << ids.find(id)))
	var good: Array = ids.filter(func(id): return not seed.evil & (1 << ids.find(id)))
	evil.sort_custom(func(a, b): return a == case_data.conclusion.target if a == case_data.conclusion.target or b == case_data.conclusion.target else a < b)
	good.sort_custom(func(a, b): return a == ids[seed.marked] if a == ids[seed.marked] or b == ids[seed.marked] else a < b)
	var tested = 0
	var movement = Solver.movement_for(view, cards, {}, listener.knowledge)
	for outgoing in evil:
		for incoming in good:
			var candidate = {"evil": (seed.evil & ~(1 << ids.find(outgoing))) | (1 << ids.find(incoming)), "marked": seed.marked}
			if incoming == ids[seed.marked]:
				candidate.marked = ids.find(outgoing)
			if seed.has("converted"):
				candidate["converted"] = seed.converted
				if candidate.evil & (1 << candidate.converted):
					continue
			if Solver.holds(view, candidate, case_data.conclusion):
				continue
			if tested >= budget:
				return {"world": {}, "tested": tested, "exhausted": false}
			tested += 1
			if movement.okay and _matches(view, candidate, cards, listener.knowledge, {}, {}, case_data.conclusion.at):
				return {"world": candidate, "tested": tested, "exhausted": false}
	return {"world": {}, "tested": tested, "exhausted": false} # swaps are never an exhaustive proof

static func _find_live(view: Dictionary, cards: Array, value: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, budget: int, want: bool, positions: Dictionary, answers: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var tested = 0
	# A candidate's constraint check is independent of which person's alignment
	# is being queried. Reuse only checks the search actually elects to perform:
	# candidate order, tested counts, limits and exhaustion remain unchanged.
	var inputs = [view.people, view.evil_count, view.get("chronology", {}), view.world_options,
		listener.knowledge, cards, positions, answers, value.conclusion.at, value.get("temporal_positions", [])]
	var key = hash(inputs)
	if reuse_matches and (not matches.has(key) or matches[key].inputs != inputs):
		if matches.size() >= 32:
			matches.clear()
		var n_worlds = Domain.worlds(view).size()
		var table = PackedByteArray()
		table.resize(n_worlds)
		table.fill(255) # 255 = unknown, 0 = false, 1 = true
		matches[key] = {"inputs": inputs.duplicate(true), "table": table}
	var table: PackedByteArray = matches[key].table if reuse_matches else PackedByteArray()
	var use_table: bool = reuse_matches and not table.is_empty()
	var target_idx: int = Solver.person_index(view, value.conclusion.target)
	var type: String = value.conclusion.type
	var has_curse: bool = view.get("chronology", {}).has("curse")
	var target_bit: int = (1 << target_idx) if target_idx >= 0 else 0

	# Enumerate the public legal domain, not worlds filtered by the referee. The
	# same hard test budget applies, and exhaustion alone can certify a conclusion.
	for candidate in Domain.worlds(view):
		var holds_val: bool
		if not has_curse:
			match type:
				"evil", "suspicious": holds_val = (candidate.evil & target_bit) != 0
				"good": holds_val = (candidate.evil & target_bit) == 0
				"marked": holds_val = candidate.marked == target_idx
				_: holds_val = Solver.holds(view, candidate, value.conclusion)
		else:
			holds_val = Solver.holds(view, candidate, value.conclusion)
		if holds_val != want:
			continue
		if tested >= budget:
			return {"world": {}, "tested": tested, "exhausted": false}
		tested += 1
		var c_idx: int = int(candidate.get("__i", -1))
		var fits: bool
		if use_table and c_idx >= 0 and table[c_idx] != 255:
			Profiler.count("candidate_cache_hit")
			fits = table[c_idx] == 1
		else:
			Profiler.count("candidate_cache_miss")
			fits = _matches(view, candidate, cards, listener.knowledge, positions, answers, value.conclusion.at, value.get("temporal_positions", []))
			if use_table and c_idx >= 0:
				table[c_idx] = 1 if fits else 0
		if fits:
			return {"world": candidate, "tested": tested, "exhausted": false}
	return {"world": {}, "tested": tested, "exhausted": true}

static func direct_checks(view: Dictionary, case_data: Dictionary, knowledge: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var objections: Array = []
	var rejected: Array = []
	var fatal = false
	if case_data.conclusion.type == "evil" and TimeModel.known_good(view, knowledge, case_data.conclusion.target, case_data.conclusion.at):
		objections.append("I know I was good at that time; this accusation cannot be right about me.")
		fatal = true
	for id in knowledge.get("good", []):
		if case_data.positions.get(id, "") == "evil" and TimeModel.known_good(view, knowledge, id, case_data.conclusion.at):
			objections.append("Your explanation makes someone I know was good evil.")
			fatal = true
	var self_id: String = knowledge.get("self", "")
	for assignment in case_data.get("temporal_positions", []):
		for id in assignment.positions:
			if assignment.positions[id] == "evil" and TimeModel.known_good(view, knowledge, id, assignment.at):
				objections.append("Your dated explanation makes someone evil when I know they were good.")
				fatal = true
	for card in Solver.selected(view, case_data):
		if card.get("quoted", false):
			continue
		if card.kind == "report" and card.source == self_id:
			for id in knowledge.get("own_reports", []):
				var received = Solver.card_by_id(view, id)
				if received.get("role") == card.role and received.get("observed_at") == card.observed_at and received.get("data") != card.data:
					objections.append("%s is not the result I received. That does not tell us whether my actual gift result was healthy." % card.id)
					rejected.append(card.id)
		var night: String = str(card.get("night", -1))
		if self_id == "" or not knowledge.get("own_outings", {}).has(night):
			continue
		var was_out: bool = knowledge.own_outings[night]
		if card.kind == "report" and card.role == "lamplighter" and self_id in case_data.answers.get(card.id, {}).get("positive", []) and not was_out:
			objections.append("Your answer to %s says I was out, but I know I was home." % card.id)
			fatal = true
		var says_out: bool = (card.kind == "sighting" and card.subject == self_id) or (card.kind == "report" and card.role == "nightwatch" and self_id in card.data.seen)
		if says_out and not was_out:
			objections.append("%s conflicts with my firsthand knowledge that I was home on night %s; the report may be wrong." % [card.id, night])
			rejected.append(card.id)
		if card.kind == "alibi" and card.subject == self_id and was_out:
			objections.append("%s says I was home, but I know I went out on night %s." % [card.id, night])
			rejected.append(card.id)
	return {"fatal": fatal, "objections": objections, "rejected": rejected}

==> workshop/scenario.gd <==
## Frozen, scripted Day 3 world. Reports come from the live simulation's gift routines.
## Only `public_view` is passed to the interpreter; truth is an optional dev inspector.
extends RefCounted

const World = preload("res://sim/world.gd")
const NPC = preload("res://sim/npc.gd")
const C = preload("res://sim/content.gd")
const Gifts = preload("res://sim/gifts.gd")
const Fixtures = preload("res://workshop/fixtures.gd")

const IDS = ["constance", "bart", "nisha", "odette", "agnes", "hollis", "merrin", "lian", "greta", "fenwick"]
const ROLES = ["demon", "surveyor", "warden", "nightwatch", "lamplighter", "scion", "rumormonger", "revenant", "veteran", "dowser"]
const HOMES = ["hill", "hill", "square", "square", "square", "market", "market", "docks", "docks", "docks"]

static func generate(seed_value: int = 692, fixture: String = "core") -> Dictionary:
	var w = World.new()
	w.seed_value = seed_value
	w.rng.seed = seed_value
	w.n_npcs = 10
	w.detailed_logging = false
	var ids = IDS.duplicate()
	if seed_value != 692:
		ids = w.shuffled(ids)
	var slots = {}
	var people: Array = []
	for i in range(ids.size()):
		var cast: Dictionary = C.CAST.filter(func(c): return c.id == ids[i])[0]
		var n = NPC.new()
		n.id = ids[i]
		n.name = cast.name
		n.role = ROLES[i]
		n.claimed_role = n.role if i not in [0, 5, 7] else ["medium", "martyr", "psychologist"][[0, 5, 7].find(i)]
		n.alignment = "evil" if i in [0, 5, 7] else "good"
		n.home_block = HOMES[i]
		n.loc = "home"
		n.secret_mod = "marked" if i == 2 else ""
		for t in cast.traits:
			n.traits[t] = cast.traits[t][0]
		w.npcs.append(n)
		w.by_id[n.id] = n
		slots[n.role] = n.id
		people.append({"id": n.id, "name": n.name, "home": n.home_block, "claim": n.claimed_role, "alive": true})
	w.demon_id = ids[0]
	w.first_demon_id = ids[0]
	w.minion_ids = [ids[5], ids[7]]
	w.minion_id = ids[5]
	w.evil_plan = {"bluffs": {ids[0]: "medium", ids[5]: "martyr", ids[7]: "psychologist"}}
	var cards: Array = []
	w.day = 0
	w.phase = C.NIGHT
	# Rejection-sample actual results to retain the deliberately ambiguous teaching fixture.
	# Marked registration is per reading, not permanent evil alignment.
	var survey = w.by_id[slots.surveyor]
	for attempt in range(200):
		w._receive_info(survey, "")
		if survey.info_log[-1].data.count == 4:
			break
	_add_report(cards, survey, "survey", 1)
	var rumor = w.by_id[slots.rumormonger]
	for attempt in range(200):
		w._receive_info(rumor, "")
		if ids[0] in rumor.info_log[-1].data.pair and ids[2] in rumor.info_log[-1].data.pair:
			break
	_add_report(cards, rumor, "rumor", 1)
	# The Dowser visits the Market on day 2 with exactly this group present.
	w.day = 2
	w.phase = C.AFTERNOON
	for n in w.npcs:
		n.loc = "home"
	var market_location: String = C.LOCATIONS.keys().filter(func(l): return C.loc_block(l) == "market")[0]
	for i in [2, 5, 6, 7, 9]:
		w.npcs[i].loc = market_location
	var dowser = w.by_id[slots.dowser]
	for attempt in range(200):
		dowser.dowsed_day = -1
		w._day_gifts()
		if dowser.info_log[-1].data.count == 3:
			break
	_add_report(cards, dowser, "dowse", 2)
	# Two scripted, legal nights. The Veteran survives the first attack, dies on the next.
	# Night Watch / Lamplighter still compute their own observations from these routes.
	var timeline: Array = []
	for night in [1, 2]:
		w.day = night
		w.phase = C.NIGHT
		w.night_walkers = []
		for role in ["demon", "nightwatch", "lamplighter", "warden"]:
			var n = w.by_id[slots[role]]
			var destination = "hill" if role == "warden" and night == 1 else "docks"
			var path: Array = C.BLOCKS.keys() if role == "lamplighter" else [n.home_block, destination]
			w.night_walkers.append({"id": n.id, "block": destination, "path": path, "why": role})
		var watch = w.by_id[slots.nightwatch]
		var lamp = w.by_id[slots.lamplighter]
		w._receive_info(watch, "docks")
		w._receive_info(lamp, "", "", lamp)
		_add_report(cards, watch, "watch%d" % night, night + 1)
		_add_report(cards, lamp, "lamp%d" % night, night + 1)
		timeline.append({"night": night, "walkers": w.night_walkers.duplicate(true), "attack": ids[8], "survived": night == 1})
	# Additional statements are testimony, not facts endorsed by the validator.
	cards.append({"id": "alibi1", "kind": "alibi", "source": ids[0], "subject": ids[0], "night": 1,
		"observed_at": {"day": 1, "phase": "night"}, "acquired_day": 2, "text": "%s says they stayed home on night 1." % w.npcs[0].name})
	cards.append({"id": "gift_claim", "kind": "claim", "source": ids[0], "subject": ids[0], "role": "medium", "night": -1,
		"observed_at": {"day": 1, "phase": "day"}, "acquired_day": 1, "text": "%s claims Medium: visiting the dead could explain night work." % w.npcs[0].name})
	cards.append({"id": "warden_claim", "kind": "claim", "source": ids[2], "subject": ids[2], "role": "warden", "night": -1,
		"observed_at": {"day": 1, "phase": "day"}, "acquired_day": 1, "text": "%s claims Warden: guarding a home could explain night work. This fixture's quiet night was not a confirmed Warden save." % w.npcs[2].name})
	cards.append({"id": "vigil1", "kind": "sighting", "source": "player", "subject": ids[0], "night": 1, "block": "docks",
		"observed_at": {"day": 1, "phase": "night"}, "acquired_day": 2, "text": "You also saw %s at the Docks on night 1 (the same outing as the Watch report)." % w.npcs[0].name})
	# Reporting an attributed gift result also conveys an implicit role assertion.
	# Keep it separately citable, linked to its origin, rather than a second witness.
	var claims: Array = []
	for card in cards:
		if card.kind != "report":
			continue
		var claim_id: String = "claim_" + card.role
		card["claim_id"] = claim_id
		if claims.any(func(c): return c.id == claim_id):
			continue
		claims.append({"id": claim_id, "kind": "claim", "source": card.source, "subject": card.source,
			"role": card.role, "night": -1, "observed_at": {"day": card.acquired_day, "phase": "day"},
			"acquired_day": card.acquired_day, "implicit": true, "origin": card.id,
			"text": "%s implicitly claims %s by reporting this gift result. This does not establish the result's accuracy." % [w.by_id[card.source].name, C.role_name(card.role)]})
	cards.append_array(claims)
	for card in cards:
		card["fact_id"] = "report:%s:%s" % [card.source, card.id] if card.kind != "claim" else "claim:%s:%s" % [card.subject, card.role]
		card["reported_at"] = {"day": card.acquired_day, "phase": "day"}
		card["learned_at"] = card.reported_at.duplicate(true)
		card["provenance"] = {"source": card.source, "channel": "firsthand" if card.source == "player" else "testimony", "origin": card.get("origin", card.id)}
		card["event_ids"] = ["outing:%s:%s" % [card.subject, card.night]] if card.kind in ["sighting", "alibi"] else [card.fact_id]
		if card.kind == "report" and card.role == "nightwatch":
			card.event_ids = card.data.seen.map(func(person): return "outing:%s:%s" % [person, card.night])
		if card.kind == "claim":
			card["applies"] = {"from": 0, "through": 3}
	people[8].alive = false
	w.npcs[8].alive = false
	w.npcs[8].vet_used = true
	w.npcs[8].death_day = 3
	w.npcs[8].death_cause = "demon"
	w.day = 3
	w.phase = C.MORNING
	var public_view = {"seed": seed_value, "day": 3, "people": people, "cards": cards, "evil_count": 3,
		"secret": "marked", "secret_count": 1, "alignment_epoch": "opening through day 3",
		"history": ["Night 1: no death was announced.", "Dawn 3: %s is found dead." % w.npcs[8].name],
		"scope": "Ten people, three original evil, one good Marked. No alignment changes in this fixture. Reports may be lies or impaired; accepting a report assumes its source was good, held the claimed gift and was healthy when it was made."}
	var truth: Array = []
	for n in w.npcs:
		truth.append({"id": n.id, "role": n.role, "alignment": n.alignment, "secret": n.secret_mod})
	return Fixtures.configure({"public_view": public_view, "truth": truth, "timeline": timeline, "slots": slots, "jury": _jury(w, slots, cards)}, fixture, w)

static func _add_report(cards: Array, n, id: String, acquired: int) -> void:
	var e: Dictionary = n.info_log[-1]
	cards.append({"id": id, "kind": "report", "source": n.id, "role": n.role, "data": e.data.duplicate(true),
		"night": e.day if not e.has("phase") else -1,
		"observed_at": {"day": e.day, "phase": "day" if e.has("phase") else "night"}, "acquired_day": acquired,
		"text": Gifts.short_text(n.role, e.data, n.name, func(pid): return n.name if pid == n.id else C.CAST.filter(func(c): return c.id == pid)[0].name)})

static func _jury(w, slots: Dictionary, cards: Array) -> Array:
	var result: Array = []
	var roles = ["surveyor", "rumormonger", "dowser", "nightwatch"]
	var presets = ["Trusts you", "Distrusts you", "Trusts the accused", "Suspects a rival"]
	for i in range(4):
		var n = w.by_id[slots[roles[i]]]
		var suspicion = {}
		var trust = {}
		for other in w.npcs:
			suspicion[other.id] = 0.1
			trust[other.id] = 0.5
		suspicion[n.id] = 0.0
		trust[n.id] = 1.0
		if i == 2:
			trust[slots.demon] = 0.9
		if i == 3:
			suspicion[slots.warden] = 0.65
		var own: Array = cards.filter(func(c): return c.kind == "report" and c.source == n.id).map(func(c): return c.id)
		var reading_reliability = {}
		for card in cards:
			if card.kind == "report":
				reading_reliability[card.fact_id] = 1.0
		result.append({"id": n.id, "name": n.name, "preset": presets[i], "trust": [0.85, 0.2, 0.5, 0.5][i],
			"confidence": n.traits.confidence, "npc_trust": trust, "suspicion": suspicion,
			"insight": n.traits.insight, "reasoning_mode": "full", "confidence_mode": "scale", "theories": {},
			"reading_reliability": reading_reliability,
			"knowledge": {"self": n.id, "good": [n.id], "own_reports": own,
				"own_outings": {"1": roles[i] == "nightwatch", "2": roles[i] == "nightwatch"}}, "ledger": {}, "case_memory": {}})
	return result

==> workshop/session.gd <==
## UI-independent controlled experiment, version history and reproducible export.
extends RefCounted

const Scenario = preload("res://workshop/scenario.gd")
const Solver = preload("res://workshop/solver.gd")
const Jury = preload("res://workshop/jury.gd")
const Fixtures = preload("res://workshop/fixtures.gd")
const TimeModel = preload("res://workshop/chronology.gd")

var scenario: Dictionary
var case_data: Dictionary
var baseline: Array = []
var listeners: Array = []
var history: Array = []
var last_results: Array = []

func _init(seed_value: int = 692, fixture: String = "core") -> void:
	generate(seed_value, fixture)

func generate(seed_value: int, fixture: String = "core") -> void:
	scenario = Scenario.generate(seed_value, fixture)
	case_data = Solver.empty_case()
	case_data.conclusion.at = scenario.public_view.alignment_epoch
	case_data.conclusion.target = scenario.slots.demon
	baseline = scenario.jury.duplicate(true)
	reset_discussion()

func reset_discussion() -> void:
	listeners = baseline.duplicate(true)
	history = []
	last_results = []

func revise() -> void:
	case_data.version += 1

func present(remember: bool = false, venue: String = "private") -> Array:
	if not remember:
		listeners = baseline.duplicate(true)
	last_results = []
	for listener in listeners:
		last_results.append(Jury.evaluate(scenario.public_view, case_data, listener, {"venue": venue}))
	history.append({"case": case_data.duplicate(true), "remember": remember, "venue": venue, "jury_settings": baseline.duplicate(true), "results": last_results.duplicate(true)})
	return last_results

func compare_reasoning(index: int) -> Array:
	# Independent clones hold identity, private knowledge and every social input fixed.
	# Comparisons never become presentations or alter remembered theories.
	var result: Array = []
	for spec in [["full", 0.9], ["bounded", 0.1], ["bounded", 0.5], ["bounded", 0.9]]:
		var clone: Dictionary = baseline[index].duplicate(true)
		clone.reasoning_mode = spec[0]
		clone.insight = spec[1]
		clone.confidence_mode = "scale"
		result.append(Jury.evaluate(scenario.public_view, case_data, clone))
	return result

func export_data(include_truth: bool = false) -> Dictionary:
	var result = {"format": "demonologist-case-workshop-5", "seed": scenario.public_view.seed, "fixture": scenario.fixture,
		"public_view": scenario.public_view.duplicate(true), "case": case_data.duplicate(true),
		"jury_settings": baseline.duplicate(true), "history": history.duplicate(true)}
	if include_truth:
		result["developer_truth"] = {"people": scenario.truth, "timeline": scenario.timeline}
	return result

func restore(data: Dictionary) -> String:
	# Load only this fixture format. Validate the complete experiment before mutating
	# the session, then replay its presentations instead of trusting stored outcomes.
	if data.get("format", "") != "demonologist-case-workshop-5" or not data.get("seed") is float and not data.get("seed") is int or data.get("fixture") not in Fixtures.KINDS:
		return "This experiment uses a different Workshop model. Version 5 is required for reliable movement and chronology replay."
	if not is_finite(float(data.seed)) or data.seed < 1 or data.seed > 2147483647 or float(int(data.seed)) != float(data.seed):
		return "Invalid scenario seed."
	var candidate = get_script().new(int(data.seed), data.fixture)
	if data.get("public_view") != JSON.parse_string(JSON.stringify(candidate.scenario.public_view)):
		return "The scenario differs from this Workshop version; it cannot be replayed reliably."
	var error = candidate.validate_case(data.get("case"))
	if error != "":
		return error
	error = candidate.validate_jury(data.get("jury_settings"))
	if error != "":
		return error
	if not data.get("history") is Array or data.history.size() > 200:
		return "Invalid or oversized presentation history (maximum 200)."
	for entry in data.history:
		if not entry is Dictionary or not entry.get("remember") is bool or entry.get("venue", "") not in ["private", "meeting", "nomination"]:
			return "Invalid presentation history."
		error = candidate.validate_case(entry.get("case"))
		if error == "":
			error = candidate.validate_jury(entry.get("jury_settings"))
		if error != "":
			return error
		candidate.case_data = _normalized_case(entry.case)
		candidate.baseline = entry.jury_settings.duplicate(true)
		# Settings may have been changed during remembered discussion.
		for i in range(candidate.listeners.size()):
			var listener: Dictionary = candidate.listeners[i]
			listener.trust = candidate.baseline[i].trust
			listener.confidence = candidate.baseline[i].confidence
			for field in ["insight", "reasoning_mode", "confidence_mode"]:
				listener[field] = candidate.baseline[i][field]
			listener.npc_trust = candidate.baseline[i].npc_trust.duplicate(true)
			listener.reading_reliability = candidate.baseline[i].reading_reliability.duplicate(true)
			for person in candidate.scenario.public_view.people:
				var applied = 0.0
				for effect in listener.ledger.values():
					applied += Jury.applied_to(effect, person.id)
				listener.suspicion[person.id] = snappedf(candidate.baseline[i].suspicion[person.id] + applied, 0.000000001)
		candidate.present(entry.remember, entry.venue)
	scenario = candidate.scenario
	case_data = _normalized_case(data.case)
	baseline = data.jury_settings.duplicate(true)
	listeners = candidate.listeners
	history = candidate.history
	last_results = candidate.last_results
	# Also restore settings edited after the final recorded presentation.
	for i in range(listeners.size()):
		listeners[i].trust = baseline[i].trust
		listeners[i].confidence = baseline[i].confidence
		for field in ["insight", "reasoning_mode", "confidence_mode"]:
			listeners[i][field] = baseline[i][field]
		listeners[i].npc_trust = baseline[i].npc_trust.duplicate(true)
		listeners[i].reading_reliability = baseline[i].reading_reliability.duplicate(true)
		for person in scenario.public_view.people:
			var applied = 0.0
			for effect in listeners[i].ledger.values():
				applied += Jury.applied_to(effect, person.id)
			listeners[i].suspicion[person.id] = snappedf(baseline[i].suspicion[person.id] + applied, 0.000000001)
	return ""

func _normalized_case(value: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var result = value.duplicate(true)
	result.version = int(result.version)
	for argument in result.get("arguments", []):
		argument.case.version = int(argument.case.version)
	return result

func validate_case(value) -> String:
	if not value is Dictionary:
		return "Missing case."
	if value.get("id") != "case-1" or not value.get("version") is float and not value.get("version") is int:
		return "Invalid case identity or version."
	if not is_finite(float(value.version)) or value.version < 1 or float(int(value.version)) != float(value.version):
		return "Invalid case version."
	if not value.get("cards") is Array or not value.get("positions") is Dictionary or not value.get("answers") is Dictionary or not value.get("reasons") is Dictionary:
		return "Invalid case choices."
	var ids: Array = scenario.public_view.people.map(func(p): return p.id)
	var card_ids: Array = scenario.public_view.cards.map(func(c): return c.id)
	if not value.get("derived", []) is Array or value.get("derived", []).size() > 8:
		return "Invalid derived questions (maximum eight)."
	for question in value.get("derived", []):
		if not question is Dictionary or not question.get("id") is String or question.id in card_ids or not question.get("text") is String or question.text.length() > 240 or not question.get("supports") is Array or question.supports.is_empty() or question.supports.size() > scenario.public_view.cards.size() or not question.supports.all(func(id): return id in scenario.public_view.cards.map(func(c): return c.id)):
			return "Invalid derived question provenance."
		card_ids.append(question.id)
	if not value.cards.all(func(id): return id in card_ids) or value.cards.size() > card_ids.size():
		return "Unknown evidence card."
	var conclusion = value.get("conclusion")
	if not conclusion is Dictionary or conclusion.get("type") not in ["evil", "good", "marked", "suspicious", "conflict"] or conclusion.get("target") not in ids or conclusion.get("at") not in TimeModel.epochs(scenario.public_view):
		return "Invalid conclusion or time."
	for id in value.positions:
		if id not in ids or value.positions[id] not in ["good", "evil", "marked"]:
			return "Invalid alignment choice."
	if not value.get("temporal_positions", []) is Array or value.get("temporal_positions", []).size() > 48:
		return "Invalid dated assumptions."
	for assignment in value.get("temporal_positions", []):
		if not assignment is Dictionary or assignment.get("at") not in TimeModel.epochs(scenario.public_view) or not assignment.get("positions") is Dictionary or not assignment.positions.keys().all(func(id): return id in ids and assignment.positions[id] in ["good", "evil", "marked"]):
			return "Invalid dated alignment choice."
	for id in value.answers:
		var answer = value.answers[id]
		if id not in card_ids or not answer is Dictionary or not answer.get("positive") is Array:
			return "Invalid card answer."
		if not answer.positive.all(func(p): return p in ids) or answer.positive.size() > ids.size():
			return "Unknown person in a card answer."
		if answer.has("slots") and (not answer.slots is Array or answer.slots.size() > ids.size() or not answer.slots.all(func(p): return p == null or p == "" or p in ids)):
			return "Invalid answer slots."
		if answer.has("slots") and answer.slots.filter(func(p): return p is String and p != "") != answer.positive:
			return "The displayed slots and recorded answers disagree."
	for key in value.reasons:
		var valid_keys: Array = []
		for id in ids:
			valid_keys.append(id + ":1")
			valid_keys.append(id + ":2")
			valid_keys.append(id + ":3")
		if key not in valid_keys or value.reasons[key] not in ["unexplained", "gift", "evil", "secret"]:
			return "Invalid outing explanation."
	if not value.get("citations", {}) is Dictionary or not value.get("conflict_scope", []) is Array or not value.get("challenges", {}) is Dictionary:
		return "Invalid citations or conflict scope."
	for key in value.get("citations", {}):
		if key not in ids.map(func(id): return id + ":1") + ids.map(func(id): return id + ":2") + ids.map(func(id): return id + ":3") or value.citations[key] not in scenario.public_view.cards.filter(func(c): return c.kind in ["claim", "secret_claim"]).map(func(c): return c.id):
			return "Invalid claim citation."
	if not value.get("conflict_scope", []).all(func(id): return id in card_ids):
		return "Unknown report in conflict scope."
	for id in value.get("challenges", {}):
		if id not in scenario.public_view.cards.map(func(c): return c.id) or not value.challenges[id] is Array or not value.challenges[id].all(func(root): return root in card_ids):
			return "Invalid challenged report or grounds."
	if not value.get("lines", []) is Array or value.get("lines", []).size() > 6:
		return "Invalid reasoning lines (maximum six)."
	if not value.get("disputes", []) is Array or value.get("disputes", []).size() > 8:
		return "Invalid challenged accounts."
	for dispute in value.get("disputes", []):
		if not Solver.Graph.valid_dispute(scenario.public_view, dispute) or not dispute.reports.all(func(id): return id in value.cards):
			return "A challenged home alibi needs its same-night contradicting observation."
	if not value.get("faults", {}) is Dictionary or value.get("faults", {}).size() > 4:
		return "Invalid fault explanations."
	for key in value.get("faults", {}):
		var fault = value.faults[key]
		if not TimeModel.valid_fault(scenario.public_view, fault) or key != fault.window or not fault.get("basis", "") is String:
			return "Unsupported poison interval; this fixture permits only the configured Night 1 to Night 2 window."
		if fault.get("basis", "") != "" and fault.basis not in scenario.public_view.cards.filter(func(c): return c.kind == "fault_evidence" and c.window == key).map(func(c): return c.id):
			return "The poisoning hypothesis needs evidence about the same interval."
	var line_ids: Array = []
	for line in value.get("lines", []):
		if not line is Dictionary or not line.get("id") is String or line.id in line_ids or not line.get("label") is String or line.label.length() > 120 or line.get("relation") not in ["supports", "alternative", "quotes"] or not line.get("cards") is Array or line.cards.size() > card_ids.size() or not line.cards.all(func(id): return id in value.cards):
			return "Invalid reasoning line or evidence reference."
		line_ids.append(line.id)
		var choices = Solver.empty_case()
		choices.cards = line.cards
		choices.derived = value.get("derived", [])
		choices.positions = line.get("positions", {})
		choices.answers = line.get("answers", {})
		choices.reasons = line.get("reasons", {})
		choices.citations = line.get("citations", {})
		choices.faults = line.get("faults", {})
		choices.conclusion = line.get("conclusion", value.conclusion)
		var error = validate_case(choices)
		if error != "":
			return "Invalid line choices: " + error
		if not line.get("assumption_sets", []) is Array or line.get("assumption_sets", []).size() > 12:
			return "Invalid nested premise assumptions."
		for positions in line.get("assumption_sets", []):
			var nested = Solver.empty_case()
			nested.conclusion.at = scenario.public_view.alignment_epoch
			nested.positions = positions
			if validate_case(nested) != "":
				return "Invalid nested premise assumptions."
		if line.has("test_factor"):
			var factor = line.test_factor
			if not factor is Dictionary or factor.get("target") not in ids or not factor.get("log2_ratio") is float and not factor.get("log2_ratio") is int:
				return "Invalid test factor."
			if not is_finite(float(factor.log2_ratio)) or absf(float(factor.log2_ratio)) > 4.0 or line.cards.is_empty():
				return "Test factor must cite evidence and stay between 1/16 and 16."
	if not value.get("arguments", []) is Array or value.get("arguments", []).size() > 48:
		return "Invalid argument graph (maximum 48 claims)."
	var argument_ids: Array = []
	for argument in value.get("arguments", []):
		if not argument is Dictionary or not argument.get("id") is String or argument.id in argument_ids or not argument.get("label") is String or argument.label.length() > 120 or not argument.get("parents") is Array or not argument.get("without") is Dictionary:
			return "Invalid intermediate claim."
		argument_ids.append(argument.id)
		if not argument.get("case") is Dictionary or argument.case.has("arguments") or argument.case.has("argument_root"):
			return "Intermediate claims cannot contain another serialized argument graph."
		var error = validate_case(argument.case)
		if error != "" or not argument.case.cards.all(func(id): return id in value.cards):
			return "Invalid intermediate claim evidence: " + error
	for argument in value.get("arguments", []):
		if argument.parents.size() > 48 or not argument.parents.all(func(id): return id in argument_ids and id != argument.id) or argument.without.size() != argument.parents.size():
			return "Invalid intermediate claim links."
		for parent in argument.parents:
			if not argument.without.get(parent) is Array or argument.without[parent].size() > card_ids.size() or not argument.without[parent].all(func(id): return id in value.cards):
				return "Invalid shared evidence provenance."
	if value.has("arguments"):
		if value.get("argument_root") not in argument_ids:
			return "Missing argument root."
		var root = value.arguments.filter(func(a): return a.id == value.argument_root)[0]
		var expected = value.duplicate(true)
		expected.erase("arguments")
		expected.erase("argument_root")
		if root.case != expected or not root.parents.is_empty():
			return "The root claim and presented case disagree."
		var memo = {}
		for argument in value.arguments:
			if not _argument_reaches_root(argument.id, value.argument_root, value.arguments, [], memo):
				return "Intermediate claim links must lead to the root without cycles."
	return ""

func _argument_reaches_root(id: String, root: String, arguments: Array, seen: Array, memo: Dictionary) -> bool:
	if id in seen:
		return false
	if id == root:
		return true
	if memo.has(id):
		return memo[id]
	var item: Dictionary = arguments.filter(func(a): return a.id == id)[0]
	var result: bool = not item.parents.is_empty() and item.parents.all(func(parent): return _argument_reaches_root(parent, root, arguments, seen + [id], memo))
	memo[id] = result
	return result

func validate_jury(value) -> String:
	if not value is Array or value.size() != scenario.jury.size():
		return "Invalid jury settings."
	for i in range(value.size()):
		var juror = value[i]
		if not juror is Dictionary or juror.get("id") != scenario.jury[i].id or juror.get("knowledge") != scenario.jury[i].knowledge:
			return "Jury identity or personal knowledge differs from the fixture."
		if juror.get("name") != scenario.jury[i].name or juror.get("preset") != scenario.jury[i].preset or juror.get("ledger") != {} or juror.get("case_memory") != {} or juror.get("theories") != {}:
			return "Invalid baseline jury identity or memory."
		for field in ["trust", "confidence", "insight"]:
			if not _unit_number(juror.get(field)):
				return "Invalid jury trait."
		if juror.get("reasoning_mode") not in ["full", "bounded"] or juror.get("confidence_mode") not in ["scale", "persistence"]:
			return "Invalid reasoning or confidence mode."
		for field in ["npc_trust", "suspicion"]:
			if not juror.get(field) is Dictionary:
				return "Invalid jury relationship settings."
			for person in scenario.public_view.people:
				if not _unit_number(juror[field].get(person.id)):
					return "Invalid jury relationship value."
		if not juror.get("reading_reliability") is Dictionary or juror.reading_reliability.size() != scenario.jury[i].reading_reliability.size():
			return "Invalid reading reliability settings."
		for key in scenario.jury[i].reading_reliability:
			if not _unit_number(juror.reading_reliability.get(key)):
				return "Invalid reading reliability value."
	return ""

func _unit_number(value) -> bool:
	return (value is float or value is int) and is_finite(float(value)) and value >= 0.0 and value <= 1.0

==> workshop/shared_ledger.gd <==
## Portable shared ownership plan. No NPC/World references and no additive case awards.
extends RefCounted
const Evidence = preload("res://workshop/live_evidence.gd")
const Assess = preload("res://workshop/live_assessment.gd")
const Jury = preload("res://workshop/jury.gd")

static func reading(source: String, entry: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var card = {"kind": "info", "source": source, "role": entry.get("role", ""),
		"day": entry.get("day", -1), "data": entry.get("data", {}), "text": entry.get("text", "")}
	if entry.has("phase"):
		card.phase = entry.phase
	return Evidence.normalize_card(card)

## Exact original receipts, independent of legacy source/night support references.
static func roots(cards: Array, board: Array, path: Array = []) -> Dictionary:
	var result = {"complete": true, "receipts": {}}
	for card in cards:
		if card.get("kind") == "deduction":
			var number = card.get("n", 0)
			if number in path or path.size() >= 64:
				result.complete = false
				continue
			var children: Array = []
			for support in card.get("supports", []):
				var found = board.filter(func(c): return c.get("n") == support and not c.get("deleted", false))
				if found.is_empty():
					result.complete = false
				else:
					children.append(found[0])
			var nested = roots(children, board, path + [number])
			result.complete = result.complete and nested.complete
			result.receipts.merge(nested.receipts)
		elif card.get("kind") == "info" and card.get("role") in Assess.ROLES:
			var receipt = Evidence.normalize_card(card)
			result.receipts[receipt.id] = receipt
		elif card.get("kind") not in ["claim", "home"]:
			result.complete = false
	return result

static func ordinary(target: String) -> bool:
	return not target.begins_with("disc:") and not target.begins_with("pref:") and not target.begins_with("role:")

## A presentation is an endorsement path, not an additional copy of its facts.
## Removing it removes only roots with no remaining learned/endorsed owner.
static func prepare(current: Dictionary, presentations: Dictionary, ledger: Dictionary, withdrawn: Dictionary = {}) -> Dictionary:
	var input = current.duplicate(true)
	var facts = {}
	var owners = {}
	var quotations = {}
	var issues: Array = []
	for receipt in input.evidence:
		if receipt.id in input.private_evidence and supported(receipt):
			_add(facts, owners, receipt, "learned")
	for eid in ledger:
		var entry: Dictionary = ledger[eid]
		if entry.get("meta", {}).get("kind") == "reading":
			for receipt in entry.meta.get("workshop_receipts", {}).values():
				if supported(receipt):
					_add(facts, owners, receipt, "reading:" + eid)
		if entry.get("meta", {}).get("kind") == "tell" and entry.meta.has("workshop_card") and supported(entry.meta.workshop_card):
			_add(facts, owners, entry.meta.workshop_card, "testimony:" + eid)
	for key in presentations:
		var presented: Dictionary = presentations[key]
		for argument in presented.arguments:
			if argument.conclusion.get("type") not in Assess.CONCLUSIONS:
				issues.append("Unsupported endorsed claim: " + str(argument.conclusion.get("type")))
		for issue in presented.issues:
			if issue.code in ["missing_card", "cyclic_argument", "argument_depth"]:
				issues.append("Incomplete endorsement: " + issue.code)
		var endorsed = endorsed_facts(presented, presented.root)
		for receipt in presented.evidence:
			if receipt.id in presented.selected:
				if receipt.id in endorsed:
					_add(facts, owners, receipt, "case:" + str(key))
				else:
					quotations[receipt.id] = receipt.duplicate(true)
	input.evidence = facts.values()
	input.selected = facts.keys()
	input.private_evidence = input.private_evidence.filter(func(id): return facts.has(id))
	input.board_map = {}
	var numbers: Array = []
	for i in range(input.evidence.size()):
		var number = -2 - i
		input.board_map[str(number)] = input.evidence[i].id
		numbers.append(number)
	# Claim labels are questions, never extra evidence. All consequences project the
	# same union; the actual presentation retains its separately scoped diagnostics.
	input.root = -1
	input.arguments = [{"board_number": -1, "conclusion": {"type": "evil", "a": input.people[0].id}, "supports": numbers}]
	var prepared = Assess.prepare(input)
	issues.append_array(prepared.issues.filter(func(issue): return not facts.is_empty() or issue != "No supported reading is presented."))
	var aliases = {}
	for receipt in input.evidence:
		if receipt.kind != "info":
			continue
		var coarse = "info:%s:%d:%s" % [receipt.source, receipt.observed_at.day, receipt.content.role]
		if aliases.has(coarse) and aliases[coarse] != receipt.id:
			issues.append("Unresolved original reading identity: " + coarse)
		aliases[coarse] = receipt.id
	var owned = {}
	for eid in ledger:
		var entry: Dictionary = ledger[eid]
		var meta: Dictionary = entry.get("meta", {})
		var kind: String = meta.get("kind", "")
		if kind == "tell" and meta.get("workshop_card", {}).get("kind") == "deduction" and not facts.is_empty():
			issues.append("Unresolved deduction wrapper in old card testimony: " + eid)
		if kind == "reading" and aliases.has(eid):
			var receipts: Dictionary = meta.get("workshop_receipts", {})
			if receipts.size() != 1 or not receipts.has(aliases[eid]):
				issues.append("Reading ownership lacks an exact receipt: " + eid)
			else:
				owned[eid] = true
		elif kind == "arg" and (_overlaps(entry, input.evidence) or _withdrawn(entry, withdrawn) or _presented(entry, presentations)):
			var justifications: Array = entry.get("justs", [])
			var certified = not justifications.is_empty() and meta.get("workshop_conclusion", "") in Assess.CONCLUSIONS
			for justification in justifications:
				certified = certified and not justification.get("workshop_uncertified", false)
				var routes: Dictionary = justification.get("workshop_cases", {})
				certified = certified and not routes.is_empty()
				for number in routes:
					if withdrawn.has(number):
						continue
					var proof: Dictionary = routes[number]
					certified = certified and proof.get("complete", false) and not proof.get("receipts", {}).is_empty()
					for id in proof.get("receipts", {}):
						# A re-presented board argument replaces its old endorsement.
						certified = certified and (presentations.has(number) or facts.has(id))
			if certified:
				owned[eid] = true
			else:
				issues.append("Mixed or uncertified argument ownership: " + eid)
		elif kind == "tell" and _overlaps(entry, input.evidence):
			if meta.has("workshop_card") and supported(meta.workshop_card) and facts.has(meta.workshop_card.id):
				owned[eid] = true
			else:
				issues.append("Overlapping card testimony lacks an exact original receipt: " + eid)
	# Unsupported effects can coexist only when their reading source is separate.
	# Two claimed gifts from one source share role/credibility uncertainty that the
	# restricted model cannot safely split between independent owners.
	for eid in ledger:
		var entry: Dictionary = ledger[eid]
		if owned.has(eid) or entry.get("meta", {}).get("kind") != "reading" or entry.get("weight", 0.0) <= 0.01:
			continue
		var relation: Array = entry.get("rel", [])
		if not relation.is_empty() and input.evidence.any(func(e): return supported(e) and e.source == relation[0]):
			issues.append("Unsupported reading shares a source with owned reports: " + eid)
	for eid in owned:
		if ledger[eid].get("workshop_clipped", false):
			issues.append("A legacy contribution was clipped; its reversible baseline is unavailable: " + eid)
		for i in range(ledger[eid].effects.size()):
			var effect: Array = ledger[eid].effects[i]
			if not ordinary(effect[0]):
				continue
			for person in input.people:
				var key = i if effect[0] == person.id else "%d:%s" % [i, person.id]
				if absf(float(ledger[eid].get("applied", {}).get(key, 0.0))) < 0.000001:
					continue
				# Without an outside layer, a saturated legacy target cannot promise
				# exact reversal. Unrelated cleared people don't block migration.
				var suspicion: float = input.listener.suspicion.get(person.id, 0.1)
				if suspicion <= 0.0 or suspicion >= 1.5:
					issues.append("Saturated legacy beliefs need a reversible baseline before migration: " + eid)
	if prepared.ready or (facts.is_empty() and issues.is_empty()):
		# TOTAL learned evidence, not the marginal value of a presentation over private
		# reports. Self/role/public good knowledge remains; reports move into the union.
		prepared.case.cards = facts.keys()
		prepared.case.id = "shared:" + input.case_id
		prepared.case.arguments = []
		prepared.listener.knowledge.own_reports = []
		# Raw discoveries must not change strength when player trust changes. Player
		# trust discounts only facts whose sole route is the player's presentation.
		var player_weight = clampf(0.5 + prepared.listener.trust, 0.0, 1.0)
		prepared.listener.trust = 0.5
		for id in facts:
			if facts[id].kind != "info":
				continue
			var weight = player_weight
			if owners[id].any(func(owner): return owner == "learned" or owner.begins_with("reading:")):
				weight = 1.0
				for owner in owners[id]:
					if owner.begins_with("reading:"):
						weight = minf(weight, clampf(ledger[owner.trim_prefix("reading:")].weight, 0.0, 1.0))
			prepared.listener.reading_reliability[id] *= weight
		# Presentation weights add per-report uncertainty; enforce the budget AFTER
		# introducing those dimensions as well as during ordinary preparation.
		var sources = {}
		for card in prepared.view.cards:
			if card.source != prepared.listener.id:
				sources[card.source] = true
		var uncertain = prepared.listener.reading_reliability.values().filter(func(v): return v > 0 and v < 1).size()
		if sources.size() + uncertain > 6:
			issues.append("Shared evidence exceeds the 64-state credibility budget.")
	return {"ready": issues.is_empty(), "empty": facts.is_empty(), "issues": issues, "owned": owned if issues.is_empty() else {},
		"prepared": prepared, "facts": facts.keys(), "owners": owners, "quotations": quotations, "input": input}

static func supported(receipt: Dictionary) -> bool:
	return receipt.get("kind") == "info" and receipt.get("content", {}).get("role") in Assess.ROLES

static func endorsed_facts(snapshot: Dictionary, number: int, path: Array = []) -> Array:
	if number in path or path.size() >= 64:
		return []
	if snapshot.board_map.has(str(number)):
		return [snapshot.board_map[str(number)]]
	var arguments: Array = snapshot.arguments.filter(func(a): return a.board_number == number)
	if arguments.is_empty() or arguments[0].conclusion.get("type") == "conflict":
		return []
	if arguments[0].has("workshop_case"):
		var value: Dictionary = arguments[0].workshop_case
		var assigned: Array = []
		var endorsed: Array = []
		for line in value.get("lines", []):
			assigned.append_array(line.cards)
			if line.get("relation", "supports") == "supports":
				for id in line.cards:
					if id not in endorsed:
						endorsed.append(id)
		for id in value.cards:
			if id not in assigned and id not in endorsed:
				endorsed.append(id)
		return endorsed
	var result: Array = []
	for child in arguments[0].supports:
		for id in endorsed_facts(snapshot, child, path + [number]):
			if id not in result:
				result.append(id)
	return result

## Same original facts and credibility inputs as the applied union. A scoped claim
## is assessed against the other learned reports, without applying a second vector.
static func response(plan: Dictionary, snapshot: Dictionary, baseline: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var scoped = Assess.prepare(snapshot)
	if not plan.ready or not scoped.ready:
		return {}
	var view: Dictionary = scoped.view.duplicate(true)
	for card in plan.prepared.view.cards:
		if not view.cards.any(func(c): return c.id == card.id):
			view.cards.append(card.duplicate(true))
	var listener: Dictionary = plan.prepared.listener.duplicate(true)
	listener.suspicion = baseline.duplicate(true)
	for card in view.cards:
		if not listener.reading_reliability.has(card.id):
			listener.reading_reliability[card.id] = scoped.listener.reading_reliability.get(card.id, 1.0)
	var endorsed = endorsed_facts(snapshot, snapshot.root)
	listener.knowledge.own_reports = plan.facts.filter(func(id): return id not in endorsed)
	if scoped.case.conclusion.type == "conflict":
		listener.knowledge.own_reports = [] # Test the quoted set, not an unrelated background inconsistency.
	return Jury.evaluate(view, scoped.case, listener)

static func evaluate(plan: Dictionary, suspicion: Dictionary, discounted: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	if not plan.ready:
		return {}
	if plan.empty:
		return {"effects": {}}
	var prepared: Dictionary = plan.prepared.duplicate(true)
	prepared.listener.suspicion = suspicion.duplicate(true)
	for card in prepared.view.cards:
		var doubt = maxf(discounted.get("%s:%d" % [card.source, card.observed_at.day], 0.0), discounted.get(card.source + ":*", 0.0))
		# Keep hearing-route discount, replacing only the stale interval reliability.
		var previous = maxf(plan.input.listener.discounted.get("%s:%d" % [card.source, card.observed_at.day], 0.0), plan.input.listener.discounted.get(card.source + ":*", 0.0))
		var route_weight = prepared.listener.reading_reliability[card.id] / (1.0 - previous) if previous < 1.0 else 0.0
		prepared.listener.reading_reliability[card.id] = route_weight * (1.0 - doubt)
	return Jury.evaluate(prepared.view, prepared.case, prepared.listener)

static func _add(facts: Dictionary, owners: Dictionary, receipt: Dictionary, owner: String) -> void:
	facts[receipt.id] = receipt.duplicate(true)
	if not owners.has(receipt.id):
		owners[receipt.id] = []
	if owner not in owners[receipt.id]:
		owners[receipt.id].append(owner)

static func _overlaps(entry: Dictionary, receipts: Array) -> bool:
	if entry.get("meta", {}).has("workshop_card"):
		return receipts.any(func(e): return e.id == entry.meta.workshop_card.id)
	var supports: Array = [entry.rel] if not entry.get("rel", []).is_empty() else []
	for justification in entry.get("justs", []):
		supports.append_array(justification.get("supports", []))
	for support in supports:
		if support.size() < 2 or (support.size() > 2 and support[2] != "reading"):
			continue
		if receipts.any(func(e): return e.kind == "info" and e.source == support[0] and (support[1] == -1 or e.observed_at.day == support[1])):
			return true
	return false

static func _withdrawn(entry: Dictionary, withdrawn: Dictionary) -> bool:
	for justification in entry.get("justs", []):
		for number in justification.get("workshop_cases", {}):
			if withdrawn.has(number):
				return true
	return false

static func _presented(entry: Dictionary, presentations: Dictionary) -> bool:
	for justification in entry.get("justs", []):
		for number in justification.get("workshop_cases", {}):
			if presentations.has(number):
				return true
	return false

==> workshop/solver.gd <==
## Small exhaustive constraint solver. Receives public evidence only, never hidden truth.
## Counts measure compatible assignments, NOT posterior probabilities.
extends RefCounted
const Domain = preload("res://workshop/world_domain.gd")

const Graph = preload("res://workshop/case_graph.gd")
const TimeModel = preload("res://workshop/chronology.gd")
const Profiler = preload("res://workshop/profiler.gd")
static var universes: Dictionary = {}
static var report_matches: Dictionary = {}
static var movement_cache: Dictionary = {}
static var _cached_people = null
static var _cached_ids: Array = []
static var _cached_idx: Dictionary = {}
static var _cached_block_masks: Dictionary = {}

static func _ensure_people(view: Dictionary) -> void:
	if view.people == _cached_people:
		return
	_cached_people = view.people
	_cached_ids = []
	_cached_idx = {}
	_cached_block_masks = {}
	for i in range(view.people.size()):
		var p: Dictionary = view.people[i]
		_cached_idx[p.id] = i
		_cached_ids.append(p.id)
		_cached_block_masks[p.home] = _cached_block_masks.get(p.home, 0) | (1 << i)

static func person_index(view: Dictionary, id: String) -> int:
	_ensure_people(view)
	return _cached_idx.get(id, -1)

static func person_ids(view: Dictionary) -> Array:
	_ensure_people(view)
	return _cached_ids

static func block_masks(view: Dictionary) -> Array:
	_ensure_people(view)
	return _cached_block_masks.values()

static func empty_case() -> Dictionary:
	return {"id": "case-1", "version": 1, "cards": [], "positions": {}, "answers": {}, "reasons": {},
		"citations": {}, "conflict_scope": [], "challenges": {}, "derived": [], "lines": [], "disputes": [], "faults": {}, "temporal_positions": [],
		"conclusion": {"type": "evil", "target": "constance", "at": "opening through day 3"}}

static func card_by_id(view: Dictionary, id: String) -> Dictionary:
	for card in view.cards:
		if card.id == id:
			return card
	return {}

static func selected(view: Dictionary, case_data: Dictionary) -> Array:
	# Semantic identity is independent of wrapper id and presentation venue.
	var cards: Array = []
	var keys: Array = []
	for id in case_data.cards:
		var card = card_by_id(view, id)
		if not card.is_empty() and semantic_key(card) not in keys:
			if id in case_data.get("quoted_cards", []):
				card = card.duplicate(true)
				card["quoted"] = true
			cards.append(card)
			keys.append(semantic_key(card))
	return cards

static func semantic_key(card: Dictionary) -> String:
	if card.has("fact_id"):
		return card.fact_id
	var data: Dictionary = card.get("data", {}).duplicate(true)
	for key in ["pair", "group", "seen"]:
		if data.has(key):
			data[key].sort()
	return JSON.stringify([card.get("kind"), card.get("source"), card.get("role"), card.get("observed_at"), data, card.get("subject"), card.get("block")])

static func analyze(view: Dictionary, case_data: Dictionary, knowledge: Dictionary = {}) -> Dictionary:
	case_data = Graph.compile_case(view, case_data)
	var cards = selected(view, case_data)
	if case_data.conclusion.type == "conflict":
		cards = cards.filter(func(c): return c.id in Graph.roots(case_data, case_data.conflict_scope) or c.id in knowledge.get("own_reports", []))
	var base = solve(view, cards, {}, {}, knowledge)
	var proposed_cards = TimeModel.hypothetical_cards(view, case_data, cards)
	var proposed = solve(view, proposed_cards, case_data.positions, case_data.answers, knowledge, case_data.conclusion.at, case_data.get("temporal_positions", []))
	var errors = validate_answers(view, case_data) + case_data.get("graph_errors", [])
	var conclusion: Dictionary = case_data.conclusion
	if conclusion.type in ["evil", "good", "marked"] and not proposed.worlds.is_empty() and not proposed.worlds.any(func(w): return holds(view, w, conclusion)):
		errors.append("The conclusion contradicts every world allowed by these premises and choices.")
	var n = 0
	var alternative = {}
	for world in base.worlds:
		if holds(view, world, conclusion):
			n += 1
		elif alternative.is_empty():
			alternative = describe_world(view, world, conclusion.at)
	var status = "incomplete"
	if not errors.is_empty() or proposed.worlds.is_empty() or not proposed.movement_ok:
		status = "conflicting"
	elif proposed.worlds.size() == 1:
		status = "coherent"
	var entailment = "possible"
	if base.worlds.is_empty() or not base.movement_ok:
		entailment = "conflicting"
	elif conclusion.type == "conflict":
		entailment = "unsupported"
	elif n == base.worlds.size():
		entailment = "forced"
	elif n == 0:
		entailment = "unsupported"
	if conclusion.type == "conflict" and (base.worlds.is_empty() or not base.movement_ok):
		entailment = "forced"
	var conflicts: Array = []
	if base.worlds.is_empty() or not base.movement_ok:
		conflicts = minimal_conflicts(view, cards, knowledge)
	return {"status": status, "entailment": entailment, "compatible": base.worlds.size(), "supporting": n, "hypothetically_released": cards.filter(func(c): return c not in proposed_cards).map(func(c): return c.id),
		"proposed": proposed.worlds.size(), "alternative": alternative, "errors": errors,
		"movement": proposed.movement, "movement_ok": proposed.movement_ok,
		"assumptions": assumption_labels(view, cards), "unresolved": unresolved(view, proposed.worlds), "conflicts": conflicts}

static func minimal_conflicts(view: Dictionary, cards: Array, knowledge: Dictionary = {}) -> Array:
	# Shrink one conflict, then explore excluding each member to discover other cores.
	# This avoids enumerating every unrelated subset for a simple alibi conflict.
	cards = cards.filter(func(c): return c.kind in ["report", "sighting", "alibi"])
	var result: Array = []
	_find_conflicts(view, cards, knowledge, result, {})
	return result

static func _find_conflicts(view: Dictionary, cards: Array, knowledge: Dictionary, result: Array, visited: Dictionary) -> void:
	var ids: Array = cards.map(func(c): return c.id)
	var key = str(ids)
	if visited.has(key):
		return
	visited[key] = true
	var solved = solve(view, cards, {}, {}, knowledge)
	if not solved.worlds.is_empty() and solved.movement_ok:
		return
	var core = cards.duplicate()
	for card in cards:
		var trial = core.filter(func(c): return c.id != card.id)
		var tested = solve(view, trial, {}, {}, knowledge)
		if tested.worlds.is_empty() or not tested.movement_ok:
			core = trial
	var conflict: Array = core.map(func(c): return c.id)
	if not result.has(conflict):
		result.append(conflict)
	for card in core:
		_find_conflicts(view, cards.filter(func(c): return c.id != card.id), knowledge, result, visited)

static func solve(view: Dictionary, cards: Array, positions: Dictionary = {}, answers: Dictionary = {}, knowledge: Dictionary = {}, epoch: String = "", temporal_positions: Array = []) -> Dictionary:
	var span = Profiler.open("constraint_solve")
	var result = _solve(view, cards, positions, answers, knowledge, epoch, temporal_positions)
	Profiler.close(span)
	return result

static func _solve(view: Dictionary, cards: Array, positions: Dictionary = {}, answers: Dictionary = {}, knowledge: Dictionary = {}, epoch: String = "", temporal_positions: Array = []) -> Dictionary:
	var ids: Array = person_ids(view)
	var universe_key = str([ids, view.evil_count, view.get("chronology", {}), view.get("world_options", {})])
	if not universes.has(universe_key):
		if universes.size() >= 16:
			universes.clear()
		var all_worlds: Array = []
		if Domain.live(view):
			all_worlds = Domain.worlds(view)
		else:
			for evil in range(1 << ids.size()):
				if popcount(evil) == view.evil_count:
					for marked in range(ids.size()):
						if not evil & (1 << marked):
							all_worlds.append_array(TimeModel.expand(view, {"evil": evil, "marked": marked}))
		universes[universe_key] = all_worlds
	var worlds: Array = universes[universe_key].duplicate()
	if Domain.live(view) and not Domain.claims_compatible(cards, knowledge):
		worlds = []
	var has_curse: bool = view.get("chronology", {}).has("curse")
	if not has_curse and temporal_positions.is_empty():
		var must_be_good: int = 0
		for id in knowledge.get("good", []):
			var idx = person_index(view, id)
			if idx >= 0:
				must_be_good |= (1 << idx)
		var must_be_evil: int = 0
		var marked_req: int = -2
		var other_positions: Array = []
		for id in positions:
			var idx = person_index(view, id)
			if idx < 0:
				continue
			var pos_val = positions[id]
			if pos_val == "good":
				must_be_good |= (1 << idx)
			elif pos_val == "evil":
				must_be_evil |= (1 << idx)
			elif pos_val == "marked":
				marked_req = idx
			else:
				other_positions.append({"id": id, "pos": pos_val})
		if must_be_good != 0 or must_be_evil != 0 or marked_req != -2:
			worlds = worlds.filter(func(w):
				if (w.evil & must_be_good) != 0:
					return false
				if (w.evil & must_be_evil) != must_be_evil:
					return false
				if marked_req >= 0 and w.marked != marked_req:
					return false
				return true)
		for entry in other_positions:
			worlds = worlds.filter(func(w): return position_holds(view, w, entry.id, entry.pos, epoch))
	else:
		for id in knowledge.get("good", []):
			worlds = worlds.filter(func(w): return position_holds(view, w, id, "good", knowledge.get("good_epoch", epoch)))
		for id in positions:
			worlds = worlds.filter(func(w): return position_holds(view, w, id, positions[id], epoch))
		for assignment in temporal_positions:
			for id in assignment.positions:
				worlds = worlds.filter(func(w): return position_holds(view, w, id, assignment.positions[id], assignment.at))
	for card in cards:
		if card.kind != "report" and not (card.kind == "event" and card.get("event") == "curse"):
			continue
		var positive: Array = answers.get(card.id, {}).get("positive", []).duplicate()
		positive.sort()
		var key = hash([universe_key, card.source, card.get("role", ""), card.observed_at, card.data, positive])
		if not report_matches.has(key):
			if report_matches.size() > 512:
				report_matches.clear()
			var matches = {}
			for world in universes[universe_key]:
				if _report_holds(view, world, card, {"positive": positive}):
					matches[world.get("__key", TimeModel.key(view, world))] = true
			report_matches[key] = matches
		var accepted: Dictionary = report_matches[key]
		worlds = worlds.filter(func(w): return accepted.has(w.get("__key", TimeModel.key(view, w))))
	var movement_cards: Array = cards.filter(func(c): return c.kind in ["sighting", "alibi"] or (c.kind == "report" and c.get("role", "") in ["nightwatch", "lamplighter"]))
	var movement = movement_for(view, movement_cards, answers, knowledge)
	return {"worlds": worlds, "movement": movement.nights, "movement_ok": movement.okay}

static func movement_for(view: Dictionary, cards: Array, answers: Dictionary = {}, knowledge: Dictionary = {}) -> Dictionary:
	var ids: Array = view.people.map(func(p): return p.id)
	var movement_cards: Array = cards.filter(func(c): return c.kind in ["sighting", "alibi"] or (c.kind == "report" and c.get("role", "") in ["nightwatch", "lamplighter"]))
	var movement_key = JSON.stringify([ids, movement_cards, answers, knowledge])
	if not movement_cache.has(movement_key):
		if movement_cache.size() > 256:
			movement_cache.clear()
		movement_cache[movement_key] = _movement(view, movement_cards, answers, knowledge)
	return movement_cache[movement_key]

static func popcount(bits: int) -> int:
	var count = 0
	while bits:
		bits &= bits - 1
		count += 1
	return count

static func _position_holds(ids: Array, world: Dictionary, id: String, position: String) -> bool:
	if id not in ids:
		return false
	var index = ids.find(id)
	var evil: bool = (world.evil & (1 << index)) != 0
	match position:
		"evil": return evil
		"good": return not evil
		"marked": return world.marked == index
		"unmarked": return world.marked != index
	return true

static func position_holds(view: Dictionary, world: Dictionary, id: String, position: String, epoch: String = "") -> bool:
	if position in ["marked", "unmarked"]:
		var index: int = person_index(view, id)
		if index < 0:
			return false
		return world.marked == index if position == "marked" else world.marked != index
	var evil: bool = TimeModel.evil_at(view, world, id, view.alignment_epoch if epoch == "" else epoch)
	return evil if position == "evil" else not evil

static func _report_holds(view: Dictionary, world: Dictionary, card: Dictionary, answer: Dictionary) -> bool:
	if card.kind == "event" and card.get("event") == "curse":
		return answer.get("positive", []).is_empty() or view.people[world.get("converted", -1)].id in answer.positive
	if card.kind != "report":
		return true
	if TimeModel.evil_at(view, world, card.source, card.observed_at):
		return false # An accepted healthy gift assumes a good source at the reading's time.
	if Domain.live(view):
		if not Domain.healthy(view, world, card.source) or not Domain.gift_time(card):
			return false
		if card.role == "rumormonger":
			return Domain.rumor(view, world, card, answer)
	var d: Dictionary = card.data
	if card.role == "rumormonger":
		var marked: int = world.marked
		var marked_id: String = _cached_ids[marked] if marked >= 0 and marked < _cached_ids.size() else ""
		return marked_id in d.pair and (answer.get("positive", []).is_empty() or marked_id in answer.positive)
	if card.role not in ["surveyor", "dowser"]:
		return true
	# A Marked person may register either way, independently for each reading.
	var evil_bits: int = world.evil
	if world.has("converted") and world.converted >= 0:
		if TimeModel.evil_index(view, world, world.converted, card.observed_at):
			evil_bits |= (1 << world.converted)
	var ans_pos = answer.get("positive", [])
	var ans_mask: int = 0
	for id in ans_pos:
		ans_mask |= (1 << person_index(view, id))
	var group_mask: int = 0
	if card.role == "dowser":
		for id in d.group:
			group_mask |= (1 << person_index(view, id))
	var is_surveyor: bool = card.role == "surveyor"
	var target_count: int = d.count
	_ensure_people(view)
	var b_masks: Array = _cached_block_masks.values()
	for mark_reads in [false, true]:
		var pos_mask: int = evil_bits | ((1 << world.marked) if (mark_reads and world.marked >= 0) else 0)
		if (pos_mask & ans_mask) != ans_mask:
			continue
		var count: int = 0
		if is_surveyor:
			for b in b_masks:
				if (pos_mask & b) != 0:
					count += 1
		else:
			count = popcount(pos_mask & group_mask)
		if count == target_count:
			return true
	return false

static func _movement(view: Dictionary, cards: Array, answers: Dictionary, knowledge: Dictionary = {}) -> Dictionary:
	var ids: Array = view.people.map(func(p): return p.id)
	var nights = {}
	var okay = true
	for night in [1, 2]:
		var relevant: Array = cards.filter(func(c): return not c.get("quoted", false) and c.get("night", -1) == night and (c.kind in ["sighting", "alibi"] or c.get("role", "") in ["nightwatch", "lamplighter"]))
		if relevant.is_empty():
			continue
		var possibilities: Array = []
		for mask in range(1 << ids.size()):
			var valid = true
			var own: String = knowledge.get("self", "")
			if own in ids and knowledge.get("own_outings", {}).has(str(night)):
				if ((mask & (1 << ids.find(own))) != 0) != knowledge.own_outings[str(night)]:
					continue
			for card in relevant:
				var required: Array = []
				if card.kind == "sighting":
					required = [card.subject]
				elif card.kind == "alibi":
					if mask & (1 << ids.find(card.subject)):
						valid = false
				elif card.role == "nightwatch":
					required = card.data.seen + [card.source]
				elif card.role == "lamplighter":
					required = [card.source] + answers.get(card.id, {}).get("positive", [])
					if popcount(mask & ~(1 << ids.find(card.source))) != card.data.count:
						valid = false
				for id in required:
					if not (mask & (1 << ids.find(id))):
						valid = false
				if not valid:
					break
			if valid:
				possibilities.append(mask)
		var forced: Array = []
		for i in range(ids.size()):
			if not possibilities.is_empty() and possibilities.all(func(mask): return (mask & (1 << i)) != 0):
				forced.append(ids[i])
		nights[str(night)] = {"compatible": possibilities.size(), "forced_out": forced}
		okay = okay and not possibilities.is_empty()
	return {"okay": okay, "nights": nights}

static func holds(view: Dictionary, world: Dictionary, conclusion: Dictionary) -> bool:
	var index: int = person_index(view, conclusion.target)
	if index < 0:
		return false
	match conclusion.type:
		"evil", "suspicious": return TimeModel.evil_index(view, world, index, conclusion.at)
		"good": return not TimeModel.evil_index(view, world, index, conclusion.at)
		"marked": return world.marked == index
	return false

static func describe_world(view: Dictionary, world: Dictionary, epoch: String = "") -> Dictionary:
	var ids: Array = person_ids(view)
	var result = {"evil": ids.filter(func(id): return TimeModel.evil_at(view, world, id, view.alignment_epoch if epoch == "" else epoch)), "marked": ids[world.marked] if world.marked >= 0 else ""}
	if Domain.live(view):
		result["modifier"] = world.modifier
		result["holders"] = ids.filter(func(id): return world.holders & (1 << ids.find(id)))
	if world.has("converted"):
		result["converted"] = ids[world.converted]
	return result

static func unresolved(view: Dictionary, worlds: Array) -> Array:
	var result: Array = []
	if worlds.is_empty():
		return result
	for p in view.people:
		var i: int = view.people.find(p)
		var evil_count = worlds.filter(func(w): return (w.evil & (1 << i)) != 0).size()
		if evil_count > 0 and evil_count < worlds.size():
			result.append(p.id)
	return result

static func assumption_labels(view: Dictionary, cards: Array) -> Array:
	var result: Array = []
	for card in cards:
		if card.kind == "report":
			result.append("%s: %s held %s, was good and healthy at %s %s, and reported honestly." % [card.id, person_name(view, card.source), card.role, card.observed_at.phase, card.observed_at.day])
		elif card.kind == "alibi":
			result.append("%s: challenge the home alibi using the linked observation; falsity is conditional on that observation, and does not prove intent or evil." % card.id if card.get("quoted", false) else "%s: accept the home alibi as accurate." % card.id)
	return result

static func validate_answers(view: Dictionary, case_data: Dictionary) -> Array:
	var errors: Array = []
	for id in case_data.answers:
		if id not in case_data.cards:
			continue
		var card = card_by_id(view, id)
		var positive: Array = case_data.answers[id].get("positive", [])
		var maximum: int = 1 if card.get("role", "") == "rumormonger" else card.get("data", {}).get("count", 10)
		if positive.size() > maximum:
			errors.append("%s: there are more answers than positive slots." % id)
		var unique: Array = []
		for person in positive:
			if person not in view.people.map(func(p): return p.id):
				errors.append("%s: choose a known person." % id)
				continue
			if person in unique:
				errors.append("%s: the same person fills two slots." % id)
			unique.append(person)
		if card.get("role", "") == "dowser" and not positive.all(func(p): return p in card.data.group):
			errors.append("%s: choose people in this reading's group." % id)
		if card.get("role", "") == "lamplighter" and card.source in positive:
			errors.append("%s: the count excludes the Lamplighter." % id)
		if card.get("role", "") == "surveyor":
			var blocks: Array = []
			for person in positive:
				var matches: Array = view.people.filter(func(p): return p.id == person)
				if matches.is_empty():
					continue
				var home: String = matches[0].home
				if home in blocks:
					errors.append("%s: each slot needs a different block." % id)
				blocks.append(home)
		if card.get("role", "") == "rumormonger" and not positive.all(func(p): return p in card.data.pair):
			errors.append("%s: choose someone in the named pair." % id)
	return errors

static func person_name(view: Dictionary, id: String) -> String:
	for p in view.people:
		if p.id == id:
			return p.name
	return id

==> workshop/support_model.gd <==
## Experimental mixture over shared credibility assumptions, not calibrated probabilities.
## A false accuracy assumption removes a constraint; it never negates the report.
extends RefCounted

const Solver = preload("res://workshop/solver.gd")
const Graph = preload("res://workshop/case_graph.gd")
const Reasoner = preload("res://workshop/reasoner.gd")
const Movement = preload("res://workshop/movement_model.gd")
const TimeModel = preload("res://workshop/chronology.gd")
const Profiler = preload("res://workshop/profiler.gd")
static var cache: Dictionary = {}
static var comparisons: Dictionary = {}
static var priors: Dictionary = {}
# Developer A/B switch for the reproducible profiler, not a gameplay setting.
static var reuse_priors = true
static var reuse_comparisons = true
static var next_solve_id: int = 1
static var next_rank_id: int = 1

static func assess(view: Dictionary, value: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, bias: Dictionary = {}, prepared: Dictionary = {}) -> Dictionary:
	var span = Profiler.open("support")
	var result = _assess(view, value, listener, bias, prepared)
	Profiler.close(span)
	return result

static func _assess(view: Dictionary, value: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, bias: Dictionary = {}, prepared: Dictionary = {}) -> Dictionary:
	var case_data = Graph.compile_case(view, value)
	var bounded: bool = listener.get("reasoning_mode", "full") == "bounded"
	var attention = Reasoner.prepare(view, value, listener) if prepared.is_empty() else prepared
	if bounded or case_data.conclusion.type == "conflict" or not prepared.is_empty():
		case_data = attention.case
	var cards: Array = Solver.selected(view, case_data)
	var factor_refs: Array = []
	factor_refs.append_array(Movement.claim_refs(view, case_data))
	for factor in case_data.test_factors:
		if not is_zero_approx(float(factor.log2_ratio)):
			for route in factor.routes:
				factor_refs.append_array(route)
	# A role assertion alone supplies no alignment constraint. Avoid enumerating
	# irrelevant reliability dimensions for optional claims with no test factor.
	cards = cards.filter(func(c): return c.kind != "claim" or c.id in factor_refs)
	var presented_reading: bool = cards.any(func(c): return c.kind == "report" and c.role in ["surveyor", "rumormonger", "dowser"])
	for id in listener.knowledge.get("own_reports", []):
		var card = Solver.card_by_id(view, id)
		if not cards.any(func(c): return Solver.semantic_key(c) == Solver.semantic_key(card)):
			cards.append(card)
	var atoms: Array = []
	var requirements = {}
	var fault_controls = {}
	for card in cards:
		var required: Array = []
		if card.get("quoted", false):
			requirements[card.id] = required
			continue # Known testimony exists; its disputed contents are not endorsed.
		if card.id in attention.private_checks.rejected:
			var known_key: String = "firsthand:" + Solver.semantic_key(card)
			_add_atom(atoms, known_key, 0.0)
			required.append(known_key)
		var source_key: String = TimeModel.source_key(view, card)
		_add_atom(atoms, source_key, source_quality(listener, card.source))
		required.append(source_key)
		if card.kind == "report":
