			if TimeModel.disowned(view, card, listener.knowledge.get("known_events", [])):
				_add_atom(atoms, "withdrawn:" + card.id, 0.0)
				required.append("withdrawn:" + card.id)
			var fault = TimeModel.fault_for(view, case_data, card)
			if not fault.is_empty() and fault.basis in case_data.cards:
				var basis = Solver.card_by_id(view, fault.basis)
				if basis.get("kind") == "fault_evidence" and basis.get("window") in case_data.get("faults", {}):
					var basis_key: String = TimeModel.source_key(view, basis)
					_add_atom(atoms, basis_key, source_quality(listener, basis.source))
					_add_atom(atoms, fault.key, 0.5)
					required.append(fault.key)
					fault_controls[fault.key] = basis_key
			var accuracy_key: String = "accuracy:" + card.get("accuracy_origin", Solver.semantic_key(card))
			_add_atom(atoms, accuracy_key, float(listener.get("reading_reliability", {}).get(card.get("accuracy_origin", Solver.semantic_key(card)), 1.0)))
			required.append(accuracy_key)
		requirements[card.id] = required
	var uncertain: Array = atoms.filter(func(a): return a.weight > 0.0 and a.weight < 1.0)
	var total = 0.0
	var agreement = 0.0
	var evidence_agreement = 0.0
	var conditional_mass = 0.0
	var forced_mass = 0.0
	var accuracy = {}
	for card in cards:
		accuracy[card.id] = 0.0
	var paths: Array = []
	var used_factors: Array = []
	var strongest = 0.0
	var states = 0
	var impossible = 0.0
	var ranks = prior_scores(view, listener, bias, case_data.conclusion.at)
	# Private accepted subsets repeat across credibility states for other sources.
	# Within this assessment their ranks, question, knowledge and factors are fixed.
	var private_comparisons: Dictionary = {}
	# Group evaluations by active card subset so identical subsets reuse the solve,
	# factors and comparisons, while keeping exact sequential float accumulation.
	var active_eval_cache: Dictionary = {}
	for mask in range(1 << uncertain.size()):
		var weight = 1.0
		var accepted = {}
		for atom in atoms:
			accepted[atom.key] = atom.weight >= 1.0
		for i in range(uncertain.size()):
			var atom: Dictionary = uncertain[i]
			var yes: bool = (mask & (1 << i)) != 0
			accepted[atom.key] = yes
			weight *= atom.weight if yes else (1.0 - atom.weight)
		if weight <= 0.0:
			continue
		for healthy in fault_controls:
			if not accepted[fault_controls[healthy]]:
				accepted[healthy] = true
		var card_mask: int = 0
		for j in range(cards.size()):
			if requirements[cards[j].id].all(func(key): return accepted[key]):
				card_mask |= (1 << j)
		var eval: Dictionary
		if active_eval_cache.has(card_mask):
			eval = active_eval_cache[card_mask]
		else:
			var active: Array = []
			for j in range(cards.size()):
				if (card_mask & (1 << j)) != 0:
					active.append(cards[j])
			var private_cards: Array = active.filter(func(c): return c.id in listener.knowledge.get("own_reports", []))
			var private_solved = _solve(view, private_cards, listener.knowledge)
			var private_worlds: Array = private_solved.worlds
			var solved = {}
			var searched = {}
			if bounded:
				searched = Reasoner.search(view, case_data, active, listener, attention.limits)
			else:
				solved = _solve(view, active, listener.knowledge)
			var is_inconsistent: bool = (bounded and searched.status == "inconsistent") or (not bounded and (solved.worlds.is_empty() or not solved.movement_ok))
			if is_inconsistent:
				eval = {"inconsistent": true}
			else:
				var factors: Array = case_data.test_factors.filter(func(f): return not is_zero_approx(float(f.log2_ratio)) and f.routes.any(func(route): return route.all(func(id): return active.any(func(c): return Solver.semantic_key(c) == Solver.semantic_key(Solver.card_by_id(view, id))))))
				factors.append_array(Movement.factors(view, active, case_data))
				for factor in factors:
					factor["epoch"] = case_data.conclusion.at
				for factor in factors:
					if factor.label not in used_factors and not is_zero_approx(float(factor.log2_ratio)):
						used_factors.append(factor.label)
				var contributing: Array = active.filter(func(c): return c.kind == "report" and c.role in ["surveyor", "rumormonger", "dowser"])
				var a = 0.0
				var solved_id: int = 0 if bounded else int(solved.get("__id", 0))
				var comparison: Dictionary = {} if bounded else _comparison(view, solved.worlds, case_data.conclusion, factors, ranks, solved_id)
				var all_hold: bool = searched.status == "entailed_in_attention" if bounded else comparison.support > 0.0 and comparison.rival == 0.0
				var any_hold: bool = not searched.support.is_empty() if bounded else comparison.support > 0.0
				var conditional: bool = false
				if all_hold and presented_reading:
					a = 1.0
					var path: Array = contributing.map(func(c): return c.id)
					path.sort()
					if not paths.has(path):
						paths.append(path)
				elif any_hold and ((presented_reading and not contributing.is_empty()) or not factors.is_empty()):
					if bounded and searched.status == "provisional":
						var ratio = _direct_ratio(factors, case_data.conclusion)
						if contributing.is_empty():
							a = (2.0 if Movement.available(view) else 0.16) * (ratio / (1.0 + ratio) - 0.5)
						else:
							a = minf(0.95, (0.76 if contributing.size() >= 2 else 0.3) * 2.0 * ratio / (1.0 + ratio))
						if not bias.is_empty() and not Solver.position_holds(view, searched.support, bias.target, bias.position, case_data.conclusion.at):
							a /= bias.factor
					elif bounded:
						a = possibility(view, [searched.support, searched.rival], case_data.conclusion, listener, factors, ranks)
					else:
						a = _possibility(comparison) if reuse_comparisons else possibility(view, solved.worlds, case_data.conclusion, listener, factors, ranks, solved_id)
					if Movement.available(view) and not (bounded and searched.status == "provisional") and factors.any(func(f): return f.get("origin") == "movement_model"):
						var candidates: Array = [searched.support] + ([] if searched.rival.is_empty() else [searched.rival]) if bounded else solved.worlds
						var candidates_id: int = 0 if bounded else solved_id
						var preferred = comparison if not bounded and reuse_comparisons else _comparison(view, candidates, case_data.conclusion, factors, ranks, candidates_id)
						var reference_factors: Array = factors.filter(func(f): return f.get("origin") != "movement_model")
						var neutral = _comparison(view, candidates, case_data.conclusion, reference_factors, ranks, candidates_id)
						var shift: float = preferred.support / maxf(preferred.support + preferred.rival, 1e-12) - neutral.support / maxf(neutral.support + neutral.rival, 1e-12)
						a = clampf(a + 2.0 * shift, -1.0, 1.0)
					if contributing.is_empty() and not (bounded and searched.status == "provisional"):
						var candidates: Array = [searched.support] + ([] if searched.rival.is_empty() else [searched.rival]) if bounded else solved.worlds
						var candidates_id: int = 0 if bounded else solved_id
						a -= possibility(view, candidates, case_data.conclusion, listener, [], ranks, candidates_id)
					var positions: Dictionary = case_data.positions.duplicate(true)
					positions.erase(case_data.conclusion.target)
					var answers: Dictionary = case_data.answers.duplicate(true)
					if case_data.conclusion.type == "marked":
						for card in contributing:
							if card.role == "rumormonger":
								answers.erase(card.id)
					if not positions.is_empty() or not answers.is_empty() or not case_data.get("temporal_positions", []).is_empty():
						if bounded:
							conditional = not searched.support.is_empty()
						else:
							var chosen = Solver.solve(view, active, positions, answers, listener.knowledge, case_data.conclusion.at, case_data.get("temporal_positions", []))
							conditional = not chosen.worlds.is_empty() and chosen.worlds.all(func(w): return Solver.holds(view, w, case_data.conclusion))
						if conditional:
							a = maxf(a, 0.4)
				var reference = 0.0
				if presented_reading and not contributing.is_empty() and not private_worlds.is_empty():
					var private_key = JSON.stringify(private_cards.map(func(c): return c.id))
					var prior: Dictionary
					if reuse_comparisons and private_comparisons.has(private_key):
						Profiler.count("private_comparison_reuse")
						prior = private_comparisons[private_key]
					else:
						prior = _comparison(view, private_worlds, case_data.conclusion, [], ranks, int(private_solved.get("__id", 0)))
						private_comparisons[private_key] = prior
					if prior.support > 0 and prior.rival == 0:
						reference = 1.0
					elif prior.support > 0:
						reference = 0.02 + 0.16 * prior.support / maxf(prior.support + prior.rival, 0.000000000001)
				eval = {
					"inconsistent": false,
					"active": active,
					"a": a,
					"reference": reference,
					"all_hold": all_hold,
					"conditional": conditional
				}
			active_eval_cache[card_mask] = eval

		states += 1
		if eval.inconsistent:
			impossible += weight
			continue
		total += weight
		for card in eval.active:
			accuracy[card.id] = accuracy.get(card.id, 0.0) + weight
		if eval.all_hold and presented_reading:
			forced_mass += weight
		if eval.conditional:
			conditional_mass += weight
		agreement += weight * eval.a
		evidence_agreement += weight * (eval.a - eval.reference)
		strongest = maxf(strongest, weight * eval.a)
	var minimal: Array = []
	for path in paths:
		if not paths.any(func(other): return other.size() < path.size() and other.all(func(id): return id in path)):
			minimal.append(path)
	minimal.sort_custom(func(a, b): return str(a) < str(b))
	if total > 0.0:
		agreement /= total
		evidence_agreement /= total
		forced_mass /= total
		conditional_mass /= total
		for id in accuracy:
			accuracy[id] /= total
	Profiler.count("credibility_states", states)
	return {"agreement": agreement, "evidence_agreement": evidence_agreement, "forced_mass": forced_mass, "conditional_mass": conditional_mass,
		"movement_comparison": Movement.factors(view, cards.filter(func(c): return c.get("quoted", false) or source_quality(listener, c.source) >= 0.4), case_data),
		"paths": minimal, "report_acceptance": accuracy, "atoms": atoms, "states": states,
		"quoted_cards": cards.filter(func(card): return card.get("quoted", false)).map(func(card): return card.id),
		"faults": fault_controls.keys(),
		"conflict_mass": impossible, "strongest_state": strongest, "factors": used_factors,
		"model": "Experimental shared route/activity/haunt likelihood. Conviction adds twice the preference shift; signed alignment is capped before social multipliers. Profile priors and conviction tuning are uncalibrated; conditional trace/drop mechanisms are tested against the generator." if Movement.available(view) else "Experimental credibility-state mixture. Supplied test factors and the fixed 2x challenged-alibi factor are uncalibrated; no general movement/trace likelihood is inferred."}

static func explain(view: Dictionary, value: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, prepared: Dictionary = {}) -> Dictionary:
	var span = Profiler.open("explain")
	var result = _explain(view, value, listener, prepared)
	Profiler.close(span)
	return result

static func _explain(view: Dictionary, value: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, prepared: Dictionary = {}) -> Dictionary:
	var attention = Reasoner.prepare(view, value, listener) if prepared.is_empty() else prepared
	var considered = attention.case
	var cards = Solver.selected(view, considered)
	for id in listener.knowledge.get("own_reports", []):
		if not cards.any(func(c): return c.id == id):
			cards.append(Solver.card_by_id(view, id))
	var accepted: Array = cards.filter(func(c): return not TimeModel.disowned(view, c, listener.knowledge.get("known_events", [])) and (c.get("quoted", false) or (c.id not in attention.private_checks.rejected and source_quality(listener, c.source) >= 0.4 and (c.kind != "report" or listener.reading_reliability.get(c.get("accuracy_origin", Solver.semantic_key(c)), 1.0) >= 0.5))))
	var limits: Dictionary = attention.limits if listener.get("reasoning_mode", "full") == "bounded" else {"support": 840, "rivals": 840, "complete": true}
	if Solver.Domain.live(view) and listener.get("reasoning_mode", "full") == "full":
		limits.support = Solver.Domain.worlds(view).size()
		limits.rivals = limits.support
	var result = Reasoner.search(view, considered, accepted, listener, limits)
	result.merge(attention)
	result.erase("case")
	result["accepted"] = accepted.map(func(c): return c.id)
	result["doubted"] = cards.filter(func(c): return c not in accepted).map(func(c): return c.id)
	var factors: Array = considered.test_factors.filter(func(f): return f.routes.any(func(route): return route.all(func(id): return accepted.any(func(c): return Solver.semantic_key(c) == Solver.semantic_key(Solver.card_by_id(view, id))))))
	factors.append_array(Movement.factors(view, accepted, considered))
	for factor in factors:
		factor["epoch"] = considered.conclusion.at
	var ranks = prior_scores(view, listener, {}, considered.conclusion.at)
	result["support_score"] = world_score(view, result.support, ranks, factors)
	result["rival_score"] = world_score(view, result.rival, ranks, factors)
	result["has_evidence"] = not Solver.selected(view, considered).is_empty()
	result["assumption_note"] = "Search explanation grants source weight >= 0.4 and reading accuracy >= 0.5; numerical conviction averages credibility states."
	return result

static func source_quality(listener: Dictionary, source: String) -> float:
	if source == listener.id:
		return 1.0
	if source == "player":
		return 0.9
	return clampf(0.2 + 0.65 * listener.npc_trust.get(source, 0.5) - 0.35 * listener.suspicion.get(source, 0.1), 0.05, 0.95)

static func _direct_ratio(factors: Array, conclusion: Dictionary) -> float:
	if conclusion.type not in ["evil", "good", "suspicious"]:
		return 1.0
	var exponent = 0.0
	for factor in factors:
		if factor.target == conclusion.target:
			exponent += float(factor.log2_ratio) * (-1.0 if conclusion.type == "good" else 1.0)
	return pow(2.0, exponent)

static func _add_atom(atoms: Array, key: String, weight: float) -> void:
	if not atoms.any(func(a): return a.key == key):
		atoms.append({"key": key, "weight": weight})

static func _solve(view: Dictionary, cards: Array, knowledge: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var span = Profiler.open("support_solve")
	var result = _solve_inner(view, cards, knowledge)
	Profiler.close(span)
	return result

static func _solve_inner(view: Dictionary, cards: Array, knowledge: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	# Hash lookup plus exact input comparison avoids rebuilding JSON signatures in
	# every credibility state / character projection. Equality guards collisions.
	var inputs = [view.people, view.evil_count, view.get("chronology", {}), view.get("world_options", {}), cards, knowledge]
	var key = hash(inputs)
	if cache.has(key) and cache[key].inputs == inputs:
		Profiler.count("solve_cache_hit")
		return cache[key].result
	Profiler.count("solve_cache_miss")
	if cache.size() > 512:
		cache.clear()
	var solved = Solver.solve(view, cards, {}, {}, knowledge)
	next_solve_id += 1
	solved["__id"] = next_solve_id
	cache[key] = {"inputs": inputs.duplicate(true), "result": solved}
	return solved

static func prior_scores(view: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, bias: Dictionary = {}, epoch: String = "") -> Dictionary:
	var span = Profiler.open("priors")
	var result = _prior_scores(view, listener, bias, epoch)
	Profiler.close(span)
	return result

static func _prior_scores(view: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, bias: Dictionary = {}, epoch: String = "") -> Dictionary:
	# The same baseline ranks every person's projection and every credibility
	# subset. Accuracy weights and the question's target do not enter these priors.
	var inputs = [view.people, view.evil_count, view.get("chronology", {}), view.get("world_options", {}),
		view.alignment_epoch, epoch, listener.knowledge, listener.suspicion, listener.npc_trust, bias]
	var key = hash(inputs)
	if reuse_priors and priors.has(key) and priors[key].inputs == inputs:
		Profiler.count("prior_cache_hit")
		return priors[key].ranks
	Profiler.count("prior_cache_miss")
	var ranks = {}
	for world in _solve(view, [], listener.knowledge).worlds:
		var score = 1.0
		for i in range(view.people.size()):
			var id: String = view.people[i].id
			var suspicion: float = listener.suspicion.get(id, 0.1)
			var trust: float = listener.npc_trust.get(id, 0.5)
			if TimeModel.evil_index(view, world, i, view.alignment_epoch if epoch == "" else epoch):
				score *= maxf(0.15 + suspicion * 1.2 - (trust - 0.5) * 0.5, 0.02)
			else:
				score *= maxf(0.3 + (0.6 - suspicion) * 0.6 + (trust - 0.5) * 0.4, 0.05)
		if not bias.is_empty() and Solver.position_holds(view, world, bias.target, bias.position, epoch):
			score *= bias.factor
		ranks[TimeModel.key(view, world)] = score
	next_rank_id += 1
	ranks["__id"] = next_rank_id
	if reuse_priors:
		if priors.size() >= 64:
			priors.clear()
		priors[key] = {"inputs": inputs.duplicate(true), "ranks": ranks}
	return ranks

static func world_score(view: Dictionary, world: Dictionary, ranks: Dictionary, factors: Array) -> float:
	if world.is_empty():
		return 0.0
	var score: float = ranks.get(TimeModel.key(view, world), 0.0)
	for factor in factors:
		var target: int = Solver.person_index(view, factor.target)
		if target >= 0 and TimeModel.evil_at(view, world, factor.target, factor.get("epoch", view.alignment_epoch)):
			score *= pow(2.0, float(factor.log2_ratio))
		elif target >= 0 and factor.get("origin", "") == "movement_model" and world.marked == target:
			score *= maxf(factor.good_marked_likelihood, 1e-12) / maxf(factor.good_likelihood, 1e-12)
	return score

static func possibility(view: Dictionary, worlds: Array, conclusion: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, factors: Array = [], ranks: Dictionary = {}, worlds_id: int = 0) -> float:
	if ranks.is_empty():
		ranks = prior_scores(view, listener)
	var comparison = _comparison(view, worlds, conclusion, factors, ranks, worlds_id)
	return _possibility(comparison)

static func _possibility(comparison: Dictionary) -> float:
	return 0.02 + 0.16 * comparison.support / maxf(comparison.support + comparison.rival, 0.000000000001)

static func _comparison(view: Dictionary, worlds: Array, conclusion: Dictionary, factors: Array, ranks: Dictionary, worlds_id: int = 0) -> Dictionary:
	var span = Profiler.open("world_comparison")
	var result = _comparison_inner(view, worlds, conclusion, factors, ranks, worlds_id)
	Profiler.close(span)
	return result

static func _comparison_inner(view: Dictionary, worlds: Array, conclusion: Dictionary, factors: Array, ranks: Dictionary, worlds_id: int = 0) -> Dictionary:
	# One set of ranked worlds answers all characters' alignment/Marked questions.
	# This cache only speeds the full/mixture calculation; bounded searches still
	# construct their own candidates and never receive unseen public worlds.
	var rank_id: int = int(ranks.get("__id", 0))
	var fast_mode: bool = worlds_id != 0 and rank_id != 0
	var inputs = [worlds_id, rank_id, factors, conclusion.at] if fast_mode else [view.people, view.get("chronology", {}), view.get("world_options", {}), worlds, factors, ranks, conclusion.at]
	var key = hash(inputs)
	if not comparisons.has(key) or comparisons[key].inputs != inputs:
		Profiler.count("comparison_cache_miss")
		if comparisons.size() > 512:
			comparisons.clear()
		var scores: Array = []
		for i in range(view.people.size()):
			scores.append({"evil": 0.0, "good": 0.0, "marked": 0.0, "unmarked": 0.0})
		for world in worlds:
			if world.is_empty():
				continue
			var score = world_score(view, world, ranks, factors)
			for i in range(scores.size()):
				var alignment: String = "evil" if TimeModel.evil_index(view, world, i, conclusion.at) else "good"
				var registration: String = "marked" if world.marked == i else "unmarked"
				scores[i][alignment] = maxf(scores[i][alignment], score)
				scores[i][registration] = maxf(scores[i][registration], score)
		comparisons[key] = {"inputs": inputs, "scores": scores}
	else:
		Profiler.count("comparison_cache_hit")
	var index: int = Solver.person_index(view, conclusion.target)
	if index < 0 or conclusion.type == "conflict":
		return {"support": 0.0, "rival": 0.0}
	var scores: Dictionary = comparisons[key].scores[index]
	var type: String = "evil" if conclusion.type == "suspicious" else conclusion.type
	var opposite: String = {"evil": "good", "good": "evil", "marked": "unmarked"}[type]
	return {"support": scores[type], "rival": scores[opposite]}

==> workshop/theory.gd <==
## A defeasible partial theory, separate from certainty and source credibility.
extends RefCounted
const Solver = preload("res://workshop/solver.gd")

static func prepare(view: Dictionary, value: Dictionary, listener: Dictionary, reasoning: Dictionary, input_key: String) -> Dictionary:
	var result = {"mode": listener.get("confidence_mode", "scale"), "status": "scale", "bias": {}, "position": "", "before": "", "threshold": 0.0,
		"note": "Confidence scales the incoming effect; no theory persistence is applied.",
		"guardrail": "Commitment changes hypothesis ranking only; source and reading reliability are not changed."}
	if result.mode != "persistence":
		return result
	if not listener.has("theories"):
		listener.theories = {}
	var target: String = value.conclusion.target
	var key: String = target + "|" + value.conclusion.at
	# Alignment and Marked are different questions. Alignment theories are shared
	# across accusing and defending the same person, not stored as opposing claims.
	if value.conclusion.type == "marked":
		key += "|marked"
	var old: Dictionary = listener.theories.get(key, {}).duplicate(true)
	result.before = old.get("position", "")
	result.threshold = 1.0 + 4.0 * listener.confidence
	if old.get("input_key", "") == input_key:
		result = old.decision.duplicate(true)
		result.status = "unchanged"
		return result
	var candidate: Dictionary = reasoning.support
	var candidate_score: float = reasoning.support_score
	var other: Dictionary = reasoning.rival
	var other_score: float = reasoning.rival_score
	if candidate.is_empty() or (not other.is_empty() and other_score > candidate_score):
		candidate = reasoning.rival
		candidate_score = reasoning.rival_score
		other = reasoning.support
		other_score = reasoning.support_score
	var position = _position(view, candidate, target, value.conclusion.type, value.conclusion.at)
	var status = "adopted"
	var apply_bias = false
	if reasoning.private_checks.fatal:
		position = "good" if target in listener.knowledge.get("good", []) else ""
		status = "private_fact"
	elif not reasoning.has_evidence:
		position = old.get("position", "")
		status = "unsupported"
	elif position == "":
		position = old.get("position", "")
		status = "unresolved"
	elif not old.is_empty() and old.get("position", "") != "":
		if position == old.position:
			status = "retained"
			apply_bias = true
		else:
			var certified: bool = value.positions.is_empty() and value.answers.is_empty() and value.get("temporal_positions", []).is_empty() and ((reasoning.support.is_empty() and reasoning.support_exhausted) or (reasoning.rival.is_empty() and reasoning.rivals_exhausted))
			var old_score = other_score
			var strong: bool = old_score > 0.0 and candidate_score / old_score >= result.threshold
			if certified or strong or (old_score == 0.0 and listener.confidence < 0.34):
				status = "revised"
			else:
				position = old.position
				status = "retained"
				apply_bias = true
	result.position = position
	result.status = status
	if apply_bias and position in ["evil", "good", "marked", "unmarked"]:
		result.bias = {"target": target, "position": position, "factor": pow(3.0, listener.confidence)}
	result.note = {"adopted": "I have a provisional working theory; other explanations can remain possible.",
		"retained": "I am holding my earlier theory. The new comparison has not outweighed that commitment.",
		"revised": "The contrary evidence changed my working theory.",
		"private_fact": "My firsthand knowledge overrides the proposed theory.",
		"unresolved": "I did not resolve a new explanation; my old theory is not a proof.",
		"unsupported": "No presented evidence supports an additional case effect. The earlier theory supplies no new evidence."}.get(status, "")
	listener.theories[key] = {"position": position, "input_key": input_key, "decision": result.duplicate(true),
		"shortlist": [reasoning.support.duplicate(true), reasoning.rival.duplicate(true)], "epoch": value.conclusion.at}
	return result

static func _position(view: Dictionary, world: Dictionary, target: String, type: String, epoch: String) -> String:
	if world.is_empty():
		return ""
	var index: int = Solver.person_index(view, target)
	if type == "marked":
		return "marked" if world.marked == index else "unmarked"
	return "evil" if Solver.position_holds(view, world, target, "evil", epoch) else "good"

==> workshop/world_domain.gd <==
## Legal live alignment/modifier possibilities, derived solely from public setup rules.
## Fixture domains stay unchanged. This is a domain, never a list of referee survivors.
extends RefCounted
const C = preload("res://sim/content.gd")
static var cache = {}
static var _cached_people = null
static var _cached_idx: Dictionary = {}

static func _person_index(view: Dictionary, id: String) -> int:
	if view.people != _cached_people:
		_cached_people = view.people
		_cached_idx = {}
		for i in range(view.people.size()):
			_cached_idx[view.people[i].id] = i
	return _cached_idx.get(id, -1)

static func live(view: Dictionary) -> bool:
	return view.has("world_options")

static func worlds(view: Dictionary) -> Array:
	var input = [view.people.map(func(p): return p.id), view.evil_count, view.world_options]
	var key = hash(input)
	if cache.has(key) and cache[key].input == input:
		return cache[key].worlds
	var result: Array = []
	var n: int = view.people.size()
	for evil in range(1 << n):
		if _count(evil) != view.evil_count:
			continue
		for kind in view.world_options.modifiers:
			for i in range(n):
				if evil & (1 << i):
					continue
				if kind != "lovers":
					var w = {"evil": evil, "marked": i if kind == "marked" else -1, "modifier": kind, "holders": 1 << i}
					w["__key"] = key(view, w)
					w["__i"] = result.size()
					result.append(w)
				else:
					for j in range(i + 1, n):
						if not evil & (1 << j):
							var w = {"evil": evil, "marked": -1, "modifier": kind, "holders": (1 << i) | (1 << j)}
							w["__key"] = key(view, w)
							w["__i"] = result.size()
							result.append(w)
	if cache.size() >= 16:
		cache.clear()
	cache[key] = {"input": input.duplicate(true), "worlds": result}
	return result

static func key(view: Dictionary, world: Dictionary) -> int:
	var n: int = view.people.size()
	return ((int(world.evil) * (n + 1) + int(world.marked) + 1) * 4 + C.SECRET_MOD_LIST.find(world.modifier)) * (1 << n) + int(world.holders)

static func claims_compatible(cards: Array, knowledge: Dictionary) -> bool:
	var sources = {}
	var roles = {}
	for card in cards:
		if card.kind != "report":
			continue
		if sources.has(card.source) and sources[card.source] != card.role:
			return false
		if roles.has(card.role) and roles[card.role] != card.source:
			return false
		if card.source == knowledge.get("self") and knowledge.get("own_role", card.role) != card.role:
			return false
		if card.source != knowledge.get("self") and card.role == knowledge.get("own_role", ""):
			return false
		sources[card.source] = card.role
		roles[card.role] = card.source
	return true

static func healthy(view: Dictionary, world: Dictionary, source: String) -> bool:
	var i: int = _person_index(view, source)
	if i < 0:
		return false
	return world.modifier != "delusional" or not (world.holders & (1 << i))

static func gift_time(card: Dictionary) -> bool:
	if card.role in ["surveyor", "rumormonger"]:
		return card.observed_at.day == 0 and card.observed_at.phase == "night"
	return card.role == "dowser" and card.observed_at.day >= 1 and card.observed_at.phase in ["morning", "afternoon", "evening"]

static func rumor(view: Dictionary, world: Dictionary, card: Dictionary, answer: Dictionary) -> bool:
	var source: int = _person_index(view, card.source)
	# The native gift excludes itself. With no other modifier holder, even a healthy
	# Rumormonger receives an arbitrary pair/secret fallback. Keep that legal world;
	# it cannot establish the reported pair or secret type.
	if source >= 0 and (world.holders & ~(1 << source)) == 0:
		return card.data.secret in C.SECRET_MOD_LIST and card.data.pair.all(func(id): return id != card.source) and answer.get("positive", []).is_empty()
	if world.modifier != card.data.secret:
		return false
	var pair_mask: int = 0
	for id in card.data.pair:
		var idx = _person_index(view, id)
		if idx >= 0:
			pair_mask |= (1 << idx)
	if not (world.holders & pair_mask):
		return false
	var ans_pos = answer.get("positive", [])
	if not ans_pos.is_empty():
		var ans_mask: int = 0
		for id in ans_pos:
			var idx = _person_index(view, id)
			if idx >= 0:
				ans_mask |= (1 << idx)
		if (world.holders & ans_mask) != ans_mask:
			return false
	return true

static func _count(value: int) -> int:
	var result = 0
	while value:
		value &= value - 1
		result += 1
	return result

==> game/workshop_bridge.gd <==
## Read-only boundary between simulation state and the portable evidence contract.
## Never pass the World, real evil mind, hidden gift causes or developer log onward.
extends RefCounted

const Evidence = preload("res://workshop/live_evidence.gd")
const C = preload("res://sim/content.gd")
const CardModel = preload("res://game/card_model.gd")

static func capture(g, selected: Array, listener_id: String = "", venue: String = "private", cover_mind = null) -> Dictionary:
	var now = {"day": g.w.day, "phase": g._phase_now()}
	var input = {"case_id": "case:%d:%d" % [g.w.seed_value, g.w.n_npcs], "now": now, "venue": venue,
		"people": [], "receipts": [], "selected": [], "arguments": [], "root": 0, "issues": [],
		"public_events": [], "rules": {"initial_evil_count": 1 + C.SETUPS.get(g.w.n_npcs, {"minions": 1}).minions,
			"modifier_options": C.SECRET_MOD_LIST.duplicate(), "one_modifier_kind": true,
			"lovers_holders": 2, "other_modifier_holders": 1, "roles": C.ROLES.keys()}}
	if venue not in ["private", "meeting", "nomination"]:
		input.issues.append({"code": "invalid_venue", "fact": "", "detail": venue})
	for n in g.w.npcs:
		input.people.append({"id": n.id, "name": n.name, "home": n.home_block, "alive": n.alive})
	for event in g.w.public_log:
		input.public_events.append({"kind": event.kind, "subjects": event.get("subjects", []).duplicate(),
			"by": event.get("by", ""), "at": {"day": event.day, "phase": "unknown"}})
	# Native death announcements are player-observed process cards, not a query of
	# the referee's death_cause field. Townspeople know these victims were good.
	for card in g.board:
		if card.get("kind") == "death" and card.get("source") == "player":
			input.public_events.append({"kind": "demon_death", "subjects": card.get("subject", []).duplicate(),
				"by": "town", "at": Evidence.stamp(card.get("when", {}))})
	var numbers: Array = []
	for number in selected:
		_collect(g.board, number, numbers, input.arguments, input.issues, [])
		if input.root == 0:
			input.root = number
	for number in numbers:
		var card: Dictionary = g.board[number - 1]
		if card.get("kind") != "deduction":
			input.selected.append(number)
			input.receipts.append({"card": card, "private": false})
	if listener_id != "":
		var real = g.w.by_id.get(listener_id)
		if real == null:
			input.issues.append({"code": "unknown_listener", "fact": "", "detail": listener_id})
		else:
			# Selecting an existing cover mind is routing, not an inference from evil truth.
			# Do not create/sync it: that would mutate the simulation or advance an RNG.
			var n = cover_mind if cover_mind != null else (real.shadow if real.is_evil() else real)
			if n == null:
				input.issues.append({"code": "cover_unavailable", "fact": "", "detail": listener_id})
			else:
				_listener(input, n, now)
	return Evidence.normalize(input)

static func _collect(board: Array, number, numbers: Array, arguments: Array, issues: Array, path: Array) -> void:
	if path.size() >= 64:
		issues.append({"code": "argument_depth", "fact": str(number), "detail": "The live adapter's deduction-depth limit was reached."})
		return
	if not number is int or number <= 0 or number > board.size() or board[number - 1].get("deleted", false):
		issues.append({"code": "missing_card", "fact": str(number), "detail": "Selected evidence is absent or deleted."})
		return
	if number in path:
		issues.append({"code": "cyclic_argument", "fact": str(number), "detail": "Circular deduction support."})
		return
	if number in numbers:
		return
	numbers.append(number)
	var card: Dictionary = board[number - 1]
	if card.get("kind") == "deduction":
		var conclusion = {}
		for key in ["type", "a", "b", "role", "night", "mod", "group", "card"]:
			if card.get("conclusion", {}).has(key):
				var value = card.conclusion[key]
				conclusion[key] = value.duplicate(true) if value is Array or value is Dictionary else value
		arguments.append({"board_number": number, "conclusion": conclusion, "supports": card.get("supports", []).duplicate()})
		if card.has("workshop_case"):
			arguments[-1]["workshop_case"] = card.workshop_case.duplicate(true)
		for support in card.get("supports", []):
			_collect(board, support, numbers, arguments, issues, path + [number])

static func _listener(input: Dictionary, n, now: Dictionary) -> void:
	input["listener"] = {"id": n.id, "name": n.name, "trust": n.trust, "own_gift": n.role,
		"suspicion": n.suspicion.duplicate(true), "npc_trust": n.npc_trust.duplicate(true),
		"confidence": n.traits.get("confidence", 0.5), "insight": n.traits.get("insight", 0.5),
		"discounted": n.discounted.duplicate(true), "honesty": n.honesty.duplicate(true),
		"known_claims": {}, "own_outings": {}, "role_beliefs": n.believed_roles.duplicate(true), "legacy_evidence": []}
	for entry in n.ledger.values():
		if entry.get("meta", {}).get("kind", "") not in ["reading", "arg", "tell"]:
			continue
		var supports: Array = entry.get("meta", {}).get("supports", []).duplicate(true)
		for justification in entry.get("justs", []):
			for support in justification.get("supports", []):
				if support not in supports:
					supports.append(support.duplicate())
		input.listener.legacy_evidence.append({"id": entry.get("eid", ""), "kind": entry.meta.kind,
			"rel": entry.get("rel", []).duplicate(true), "supports": supports})
	for key in n.history:
		if str(key).ends_with(":3"):
			input.listener.own_outings[str(key).trim_suffix(":3")] = not str(n.history[key]).begins_with("home")
	for e in n.info_log:
		var card = _reading(n.id, e)
		input.receipts.append({"card": card, "private": true})
	for source in n.known_claims:
		var claim: Dictionary = n.known_claims[source]
		input.listener.known_claims[source] = {"role": claim.get("role", ""), "via": claim.get("via", "")}
		for e in claim.get("readings", []):
			var card = _reading(source, e)
			card["via"] = claim.get("via", "")
			input.receipts.append({"card": card, "private": true})
	for belief in n.beliefs:
		if belief.get("kind") != "saw":
			continue
		var card = {"kind": "sighting", "source": n.id, "subject": [belief.who],
			"day": belief.day, "phase": belief.phase, "block": belief.get("block", ""), "loc": belief.get("loc", "")}
		card["when"] = CardModel.when(card, now.day, now.phase)
		input.receipts.append({"card": card, "private": true})
	# No role/alignment certainty is manufactured from truth or inferred from a gift.
	input.issues.append({"code": "listener_history_partial", "fact": "", "detail": "Legacy knowledge lacks complete report/learning times and withdrawal receipts; own and known readings are retained."})

static func _reading(source: String, e: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var card = {"kind": "info", "source": source, "subject": [], "role": e.get("role", ""),
		"day": e.get("day", -1), "data": e.get("data", {}), "text": e.get("text", "")}
	if e.has("phase"):
		card["phase"] = e.phase
	card["when"] = CardModel.when(card, card.day, -1)
	return card

==> game/workshop_canvas.gd <==
## Live canvas boundary. Only player board receipts cross into the editor.
extends RefCounted
const Bridge = preload("res://game/workshop_bridge.gd")
const Evidence = preload("res://workshop/live_evidence.gd")
const Assess = preload("res://workshop/live_assessment.gd")

static func view(g) -> Dictionary:
	var numbers: Array = g.board.filter(func(c): return not c.get("deleted", false) and c.kind != "deduction").map(func(c): return c.n)
	var snapshot = Bridge.capture(g, numbers)
	var cards: Array = []
	for receipt in snapshot.evidence:
		var content: Dictionary = receipt.content
		var note = "FALLBACK: this evidence has no live Workshop evaluator"
		if receipt.kind == "info" and content.get("role") in Assess.ROLES:
			note = "Reading adapter available · check whole-case support"
			if not Assess.Domain.gift_time({"role": content.role, "observed_at": receipt.observed_at}):
				note = "FALLBACK: this reading time is outside the live adapter"
			var ids: Array = snapshot.people.map(func(p): return p.id)
			if not Assess.reading_valid(receipt, ids, snapshot.rules.modifier_options):
				note = "FALLBACK: reading payload is incomplete or invalid"
		cards.append({"id": receipt.id, "fact_id": receipt.id, "kind": "report" if receipt.kind == "info" else receipt.kind,
			"source": receipt.source, "subject": receipt.subjects[0] if not receipt.subjects.is_empty() else "",
			"role": content.get("role", ""), "data": content.get("data", {}).duplicate(true),
			"night": receipt.observed_at.day if receipt.observed_at.phase == "night" else -1,
			"block": content.get("block", ""), "observed_at": receipt.observed_at,
			"reported_at": receipt.reported_at if receipt.reported_at != null else {"day": "?"},
			"learned_at": receipt.learned_at if receipt.learned_at != null else {"day": "?"},
			"acquired_day": receipt.learned_at.day if receipt.learned_at != null else snapshot.now.day,
			"text": receipt.text, "support_note": note})
	return {"seed": g.w.seed_value, "day": snapshot.now.day, "people": snapshot.people, "cards": cards,
		"evil_count": snapshot.rules.initial_evil_count, "alignment_epoch": "Original alignment",
		"world_options": {"modifiers": snapshot.rules.modifier_options}, "live": true}

## Resolve exact original facts afresh. Missing/deleted evidence is a blocker, never
## permission to file a weaker argument with fewer roots.
static func supports(g, value: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var numbers: Array = []
	var missing: Array = []
	for id in value.cards:
		var matches: Array = g.board.filter(func(c): return c.kind != "deduction" and not c.get("deleted", false) and Evidence.normalize_card(c).id == id)
		if matches.is_empty():
			missing.append("Missing or changed original evidence: " + id)
		else:
			numbers.append(matches[0].n)
	return {"numbers": numbers, "issues": missing}

static func snapshot(g, value: Dictionary, listener: String = "", venue: String = "private") -> Dictionary:
	var roots = supports(g, value)
	var input = Bridge.capture(g, roots.numbers, listener, venue)
	input.root = -1
	input.arguments = [{"board_number": -1, "supports": roots.numbers,
		"conclusion": {"type": value.conclusion.type, "a": value.conclusion.target}, "workshop_case": value.duplicate(true)}]
	for issue in roots.issues:
		input.issues.append({"code": "missing_card", "detail": issue})
	return input

static func status(g, value: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var input = snapshot(g, value)
	# Use a synthetic profile for capability checks, without running the expensive
	# numerical preview or looking at any real listener's private knowledge.
	input.listener = {"id": "ghost", "name": "Neutral reference", "trust": 0.5, "confidence": 0.5,
		"insight": 0.5, "npc_trust": {}, "suspicion": {}, "discounted": {}, "own_gift": ""}
	var prepared = Assess.prepare(input)
	var issues: Array = prepared.issues.duplicate()
	var blocked = not supports(g, value).issues.is_empty() or value.cards.is_empty()
	if value.cards.is_empty():
		issues.append("Connect evidence to the selected premise before filing.")
	return {"ready": prepared.ready and not blocked, "blocked": blocked, "issues": issues,
		"mode": "shared" if prepared.ready and g.workshop_effects else "legacy",
		"warning": "Fallback evaluates the headline and original cards using the existing argument system. Canvas answers, assumptions, intermediate claims and link meanings are NOT interpreted by that system."}

static func file(g, value: Dictionary, previous: int = 0) -> int:
	if g.workshop_presenting:
		g.last_error = "Wait for the current presentation to finish before changing a filed case."
		return -1
	if previous > 0 and previous <= g.board.size() and g.board[previous - 1].get("deleted", false):
		previous = 0
	var check = status(g, value)
	if check.blocked:
		g.last_error = "\n".join(PackedStringArray(check.issues))
		return -1
	var roots = supports(g, value)
	var conclusion = {"type": value.conclusion.type, "a": value.conclusion.target}
	if previous > 0 and previous <= g.board.size() and g.board[previous - 1].get("workshop_case", {}) == value:
		return previous
	# Build native fallback footing through the ordinary filer, then retain the
	# entire graph. A unique label prevents text-based card merging across drafts.
	var old_size: int = g.board.size()
	var number = g.file_deduction(conclusion, roots.numbers, {}, 0, true)
	if number < 1:
		return number
	var card: Dictionary = g.board[number - 1].duplicate(true)
	card["workshop_case"] = value.duplicate(true)
	card["text"] = "Case: %s (%d original reports)." % [g.Argue.conclusion_text(g.w, conclusion), roots.numbers.size()]
	# Stable board number for revisions; listeners keep their old receipt until the
	# next presentation. Editing a draft never changes anyone's beliefs.
	if previous > 0 and previous <= g.board.size() and g.board[previous - 1].has("workshop_case"):
		if g.board[previous - 1].workshop_case == value:
			return previous
		card.n = previous
		g.board[previous - 1] = card
		if number != previous and number > old_size:
			g.board[number - 1]["deleted"] = true
		g.w.dlog.add("workshop_canvas_filed", "player", conclusion.get("a", ""), {"card": previous, "revision": true, "case": value, "supports": roots.numbers})
		return previous
	# The native filer may have returned a pre-existing flat deduction. Never
	# overwrite that card or turn another draft into this graph.
	if number > old_size:
		g.board[number - 1] = card
		g.w.dlog.add("workshop_canvas_filed", "player", conclusion.get("a", ""), {"card": number, "revision": false, "case": value, "supports": roots.numbers})
		return number
	return g.add_card(card)

==> game/workshop_ownership.gd <==
## Reversible application boundary for the opt-in, restricted reading model.
extends RefCounted
const Shared = preload("res://workshop/shared_ledger.gd")
const Bridge = preload("res://game/workshop_bridge.gd")
const Mind = preload("res://sim/mind.gd")
const Profiler = preload("res://workshop/profiler.gd")

static func refresh(g, n, phase: String) -> void:
	var span = Profiler.open("ownership_" + phase)
	_refresh(g, n, phase)
	Profiler.close(span)

static func _refresh(g, n, phase: String) -> void:
	if g._workshop_busy or n.workshop_state.is_empty():
		return
	var state: Dictionary = n.workshop_state
	var key = n.id + (":cover" if n.is_shadow else ":good")
	if phase == "before":
		state.previous_owned = state.get("owned", {}).duplicate(true)
		if state.get("active", false):
			var removed = {}
			for id in state.outside:
				# Preserve direct native assignments too (ordinary adjustments go
				# through Mind.adjust and already maintain the outside layer).
				var expected = clampf(state.outside[id] + state.requested.get(id, 0.0), 0.0, 1.5)
				var before: float = n.suspicion.get(id, expected)
				var external_change: float = before - expected
				# Preserve the exact stored baseline when nothing changed. Adding
				# then subtracting the same float introduced rounding drift, which
				# broke exact cache/repetition keys and fed priors on every refresh.
				n.suspicion[id] = state.outside[id] if absf(external_change) < 0.000000001 else clampf(state.outside[id] + external_change, 0.0, 1.5)
				if not is_equal_approx(before, n.suspicion[id]):
					removed[id] = {"from": before, "to": n.suspicion[id], "delta": n.suspicion[id] - before}
			g.w.dlog.trace_event("workshop_retraction", n.id, "", {"removed": removed,
				"previous_owned": state.previous_owned}, "Retract the entire previous joint vector before reassessment; independent suspicion remains.")
		state.active = false
		state.requested = {}
		state.applied = {}
		state.owned = {}
		g._workshop_plans.erase(key)
		if not state.get("enabled", false):
			state.issues = ["Shared effects disabled; legacy contributions restored."]
			return
		var input = Bridge.capture(g, [], n.id, "private", n)
		var plan = Shared.prepare(input, state.get("presentations", {}), n.ledger, state.get("withdrawn", {}))
		state.issues = plan.issues
		state.facts = plan.facts
		state.owners = plan.owners
		state.quoted = plan.quotations.keys()
		state.reason_visibility = _visibility(n, plan.facts)
		state.owned = plan.owned
		if plan.ready:
			g._workshop_plans[key] = plan
	else:
		if not g._workshop_plans.has(key):
			return
		var plan: Dictionary = g._workshop_plans[key]
		plan.input.listener.discounted = n.discounted.duplicate(true)
		plan.input.listener.suspicion = n.suspicion.duplicate(true)
		plan = Shared.prepare(plan.input, state.get("presentations", {}), n.ledger, state.get("withdrawn", {}))
		if not plan.ready:
			state.owned = {}
			state.issues = plan.issues
			g._workshop_busy = true
			Mind.recompute_beliefs(g.w, n, " (shared capability fallback)")
			g._workshop_busy = false
			return
		# Derive interval doubt first; evaluate joint effects only after removing
		# the owned legacy grounds from honesty and the entire previous vector.
		var started = Time.get_ticks_msec()
		var inputs = [plan.prepared, n.suspicion, n.discounted]
		var cached: Dictionary = g._workshop_evaluations.get(key, {})
		var cache_hit = cached.get("input") == inputs
		var proposal: Dictionary
		if cache_hit:
			Profiler.count("union_cache_hit")
			proposal = cached.proposal
		else:
			Profiler.count("union_cache_miss")
			var union_span = Profiler.open("joint_effects")
			proposal = Shared.evaluate(plan, n.suspicion, n.discounted)
			Profiler.close(union_span)
			g._workshop_evaluations[key] = {"input": inputs.duplicate(true), "proposal": proposal}
		state.outside = n.suspicion.duplicate(true)
		state.requested = {}
		state.applied = {}
		for id in proposal.effects:
			if id == n.id or not n.suspicion.has(id):
				continue
			var desired: float = proposal.effects[id].requested
			state.requested[id] = desired
			var old: float = n.suspicion[id]
			n.suspicion[id] = clampf(old + desired, 0.0, 1.5)
			state.applied[id] = n.suspicion[id] - old
			if not is_zero_approx(state.applied[id]):
				g.w.dlog.trace_event("value_change", n.id, id, {"quantity": "suspicion", "from": old,
					"to": n.suspicion[id], "requested_delta": desired, "applied_delta": state.applied[id],
				"ledger_id": "workshop:shared"}, "Shared original reading evidence")
		state.active = true
		state.elapsed_ms = Time.get_ticks_msec() - started
		state.cache_hit = cache_hit
		g.w.dlog.trace_event("workshop_ownership", n.id, "", {"owned": state.owned, "facts": state.facts,
			"owners": state.owners, "requested": state.requested, "applied": state.applied,
			"elapsed_ms": state.elapsed_ms}, "One joint vector replaces certified legacy suspicion; preferences and role beliefs retain their owners.")

## Conservative disclosure: a joint inference is not automatically this NPC's
## own gift to reveal. Only explicit presentations/disclosures make roots citable.
static func _visibility(n, facts: Array) -> String:
	var public = {}
	var player = {}
	for id in n.workshop_state.get("disclosures", {}):
		var disclosure: Dictionary = n.workshop_state.disclosures[id]
		if disclosure.get("player", false):
			player[id] = true
		if disclosure.get("public", false):
			public[id] = true
	for snapshot in n.workshop_state.get("presentations", {}).values():
		for id in snapshot.selected:
			player[id] = true
			if snapshot.venue != "private":
				public[id] = true
	for entry in n.ledger.values():
		var meta: Dictionary = entry.get("meta", {})
		for receipt in meta.get("workshop_receipts", {}).values():
			if receipt.get("heard_in_public", false):
				public[receipt.id] = true
		if meta.get("workshop_public", false) and meta.has("workshop_card"):
			public[meta.workshop_card.id] = true
	for i in range(n.info_log.size()):
		var entry: Dictionary = n.info_log[i]
		var id: String = Shared.reading(n.id, entry).id
		if entry.get("shared", false):
			public[id] = true
		if i < n.told_readings:
			player[id] = true
	if not facts.is_empty() and facts.all(func(id): return public.has(id)):
		return "public"
	if not facts.is_empty() and facts.all(func(id): return public.has(id) or player.has(id)):
		return "shared_player"
	return "shared_private"

==> game/workshop_presentation.gd <==
## One supported presentation path: assessment, parked legacy receipt, social response.
## Runs only on a good mind (including evil's cover). No actual evil intelligence enters.
extends RefCounted
const Bridge = preload("res://game/workshop_bridge.gd")
const Evidence = preload("res://workshop/live_evidence.gd")
const Shared = preload("res://workshop/shared_ledger.gd")
const Assess = preload("res://workshop/live_assessment.gd")
const Mind = preload("res://sim/mind.gd")
const Argue = preload("res://sim/argue.gd")
const Profiler = preload("res://workshop/profiler.gd")

static func present(g, n, card: Dictionary, targets: Array, direction: String, venue: String, speak: bool) -> Dictionary:
	var span = Profiler.open("presentation")
	var result = _present(g, n, card, targets, direction, venue, speak)
	Profiler.close(span)
	return result

static func _present(g, n, card: Dictionary, targets: Array, direction: String, venue: String, speak: bool) -> Dictionary:
	if n.is_evil():
		return {}
	if card.has("workshop_case"):
		g.workshop_canvas_retire(n, card)
	var snapshot = _snapshot(g, n, card, targets, direction, venue)
	if snapshot.is_empty() or not Assess.prepare(snapshot).ready:
		return {}
	var state: Dictionary = n.workshop_state
	var original_ids: Array = snapshot.selected.duplicate()
	original_ids.sort()
	var target_ids = targets.duplicate()
	target_ids.sort()
	var origin = str(card.n) if card.get("n", 0) > 0 else JSON.stringify(original_ids).sha256_text()
	var key = str(card.n) if direction == "deduction" else "direct:%s:%s:%s" % [origin, direction, ",".join(PackedStringArray(target_ids))]
	var previous: Dictionary = state.get("presentations", {}).duplicate(true)
	var proposals: Dictionary = previous.duplicate(true)
	proposals[key] = snapshot
	var current = Bridge.capture(g, [], n.id, venue, n)
	var proposed = Shared.prepare(current, proposals, n.ledger, state.get("withdrawn", {}))
	if not proposed.ready:
		return {}
	var earlier: Array = state.get("facts", []).duplicate()
	var novelty_ids = Shared.endorsed_facts(snapshot, snapshot.root)
	var headline: Dictionary = snapshot.arguments.filter(func(a): return a.board_number == snapshot.root)[0]
	if headline.conclusion.type == "conflict":
		novelty_ids = snapshot.selected
	var fresh: Array = novelty_ids.filter(func(id): return id not in earlier and not state.get("rewarded_facts", {}).has(id))
	state.enabled = true
	state.presentations = proposals
	state.get("withdrawn", {}).erase(key)
	if not state.has("disclosures"):
		state.disclosures = {}
	for id in snapshot.selected:
		if not state.disclosures.has(id):
			state.disclosures[id] = {"player": true, "public": false}
		if venue != "private":
			state.disclosures[id].public = true
	var initial_span = Profiler.open("initial_refresh")
	Mind.recompute_beliefs(g.w, n, " (interpreted presentation)")
	Profiler.close(initial_span)
	if not state.get("active", false):
		# An interval-derived budget can change during recomputation. Do not leave
		# an unsupported endorsement behind when returning to native presentation.
		state.presentations = previous
		Mind.recompute_beliefs(g.w, n, " (presentation capability fallback)")
		return {}
	var listener_key = n.id + (":cover" if n.is_shadow else ":good")
	var plan: Dictionary = g._workshop_plans[listener_key]
	# Rebuild against final interval reliability, matching the applied union.
	plan.input.listener.discounted = n.discounted.duplicate(true)
	plan = Shared.prepare(plan.input, proposals, n.ledger, state.get("withdrawn", {}))
	var response_input = [plan.prepared, snapshot.arguments, snapshot.root, snapshot.board_map, snapshot.selected,
		snapshot.evidence.filter(func(e): return e.id in snapshot.selected), state.outside]
	var cached: Dictionary = g._workshop_responses.get(listener_key, {})
	var assessment: Dictionary
	if cached.get("input") == response_input:
		Profiler.count("response_cache_hit")
		assessment = cached.assessment
	else:
		Profiler.count("response_cache_miss")
		var response_span = Profiler.open("scoped_response")
		assessment = Shared.response(plan, snapshot, state.outside)
		Profiler.close(response_span)
		g._workshop_responses[listener_key] = {"input": response_input.duplicate(true), "assessment": assessment}
	if assessment.is_empty():
		return {}
	var root: Dictionary = snapshot.arguments.filter(func(a): return a.board_number == snapshot.root)[0]
	var graph_choices: Dictionary = root.get("workshop_case", {}).duplicate(true)
	graph_choices.erase("version")
	var signature = JSON.stringify(Evidence.canonical([root.conclusion, original_ids, graph_choices]))
	var inputs = JSON.stringify(Evidence.canonical([state.outside, plan.facts, n.discounted, n.trust, n.npc_trust, n.role, snapshot.public_events]))
	var discussion: Dictionary = state.get("discussion", {})
	var repeated: bool = discussion.get(signature, "") == inputs
	var audience = "player" if venue == "private" else "town"
	var delivery_key = signature + "|" + audience
	var text: String = assessment.response
	if repeated and state.get("delivered", {}).get(delivery_key, "") == inputs:
		text = "Those are the same reports. My assessment is unchanged."
	elif targets.size() > 1:
		for claim in assessment.claims:
			if claim.conclusion.target in targets.slice(1):
				text += " About %s: %s" % [g.w.by_id[claim.conclusion.target].name, claim.note]
	# Keep a compatible native contribution parked underneath the owner. It can be
	# restored on fallback/disable; its agreement comes from this assessment too.
	if direction == "deduction":
		var supports: Array = g.board.filter(func(c): return c.n in card.supports)
		var park_span = Profiler.open("park_native")
		Argue._apply(g.w, n, card, supports, maxf(0.0, assessment.agreement) * (1.0 if venue == "private" else 0.5 if venue == "nomination" else 0.6), g.board)
		Profiler.close(park_span)
	else:
		for target in targets:
			var sign_value = -0.4 if direction == "defend" else 0.45
			var eid = "workshop:tell:%s:%s" % [key, target]
			var meta = {"kind": "tell", "workshop_card": Evidence.normalize_card(card), "text": card.get("text", "")}
			if card.get("kind") == "deduction":
				meta = {"kind": "arg", "workshop_conclusion": root.conclusion.type, "workshop_roots": Shared.roots([card], g.board),
					"workshop_case": key, "supports": Argue.support_refs([card], g.board)}
			var scoped: Array = assessment.claims.filter(func(c): return c.conclusion.target == target and c.conclusion.type == root.conclusion.type)
			var agreement: float = scoped[0].agreement if not scoped.is_empty() else assessment.agreement
			Mind.ledger_hear(g.w, n, eid, [[target, sign_value, true, "Presented original report", ""]], maxf(0.0, agreement), Mind.support_ref(card), meta)
	if not state.has("rewarded_facts"):
		state.rewarded_facts = {}
	for id in snapshot.selected:
		state.rewarded_facts[id] = true
	var trust_before: float = n.trust
	var pressure_before: float = n.pressure
	if assessment.agreement >= 0.6 and not fresh.is_empty() and not repeated:
		var reward_span = Profiler.open("trust_reward")
		Mind.set_trust(g.w, n, n.trust + 0.02, "the presented reports support the argument")
		Mind.recompute_beliefs(g.w, n, " (presentation trust reward)")
		Profiler.close(reward_span)
	if root.conclusion.type == "conflict" and assessment.agreement >= 0.6 and not repeated:
		if not state.has("scrutinized"):
			state.scrutinized = {}
		var sources: Array = snapshot.evidence.filter(func(e): return e.id in snapshot.selected).map(func(e): return e.source)
		if not state.scrutinized.has(signature) and n.id in sources:
			n.pressure += 0.1 if venue == "private" else 0.2
			state.scrutinized[signature] = true
	if not state.has("discussion"):
		state.discussion = {}
	# Trust rewards are part of the new starting inputs on a repeat.
	state.discussion[signature] = JSON.stringify(Evidence.canonical([state.outside, state.facts, n.discounted, n.trust, n.npc_trust, n.role, snapshot.public_events]))
	if speak and not n.silenced:
		if not state.has("delivered"):
			state.delivered = {}
		state.delivered[delivery_key] = state.discussion[signature]
	var descriptor = {"kind": "argument", "key": signature, "facts": snapshot.selected, "stance": assessment.stance,
		"claim": card.get("text", "the presented case"), "target_id": root.conclusion.get("a", ""), "special": text}
	var lines: Array = [Mind.say(n, text)] if speak and not n.silenced else []
	g.w.dlog.trace_event("workshop_presentation", n.id, root.conclusion.get("a", ""),
		{"venue": venue, "key": key, "agreement": assessment.agreement, "stance": assessment.stance,
		"repeated": repeated, "fresh": fresh, "claims": assessment.claims, "response": text,
		"quoted": state.get("quoted", []), "trust_before": trust_before, "trust_after": n.trust,
		"pressure_delta": n.pressure - pressure_before, "requested": state.requested}, "One assessment controls supported presentation and shared belief ownership.")
	g.w.dlog.add("argument_stance", n.id, root.conclusion.get("a", ""), {"key": signature, "stance": assessment.stance,
		"evil": false, "repeat": repeated, "model": "shared"})
	return {"lines": lines, "facts": [], "agreement": assessment.agreement, "repeated": repeated,
		"dialogue": descriptor, "workshop": true, "assessment": assessment}

static func _snapshot(g, n, card: Dictionary, targets: Array, direction: String, venue: String) -> Dictionary:
	if direction == "deduction":
		if card.get("n", 0) <= 0 or card.n > g.board.size():
			return {}
		if g.board[card.n - 1].get("conclusion", {}) != card.conclusion or g.board[card.n - 1].get("supports", []) != card.supports or g.board[card.n - 1].get("workshop_case", {}) != card.get("workshop_case", {}):
			return {} # Do not assess a stale wrapper as though it were the current filed case.
		return Bridge.capture(g, [card.n], n.id, venue, n)
	if targets.is_empty():
		return {}
	if card.get("kind") == "deduction":
		if card.get("n", 0) <= 0 or card.n > g.board.size() or g.board[card.n - 1].get("supports", []) != card.get("supports", []) or g.board[card.n - 1].get("conclusion", {}) != card.get("conclusion", {}) or g.board[card.n - 1].get("workshop_case", {}) != card.get("workshop_case", {}):
			return {} # A relay of an old wrapper must not learn a later board revision.
		var cited = Bridge.capture(g, [card.n], n.id, venue, n)
		cited.root = -1
		cited.arguments.append({"board_number": -1, "conclusion": {"type": "good" if direction == "defend" else "suspicious", "a": targets[0]}, "supports": [card.n]})
		for i in range(1, targets.size()):
			cited.arguments.append({"board_number": -2 - i, "conclusion": {"type": "good" if direction == "defend" else "suspicious", "a": targets[i]}, "supports": [card.n]})
		return cited
	var input = Bridge.capture(g, [], n.id, venue, n)
	var receipt = Evidence.normalize_card(card)
	input.evidence.append(receipt)
	input.selected = [receipt.id]
	input.board_map[str(card.get("n", 0))] = receipt.id
	input.root = -1
	input.arguments = [{"board_number": -1, "conclusion": {"type": "good" if direction == "defend" else "suspicious", "a": targets[0]}, "supports": [card.get("n", 0)]}]
	for i in range(1, targets.size()):
		input.arguments.append({"board_number": -2 - i, "conclusion": {"type": "good" if direction == "defend" else "suspicious", "a": targets[i]}, "supports": [card.get("n", 0)]})
	return input

==> tools/profile_workshop_town.gd <==
## Reproducible native-game floor benchmark. Profiling begins after meeting setup.
extends SceneTree
const Game = preload("res://game/game.gd")
const Live = preload("res://game/workshop_canvas.gd")
const Canvas = preload("res://workshop/canvas_model.gd")
const Profile = preload("res://workshop/profiler.gd")
const Evidence = preload("res://workshop/live_evidence.gd")
var result: Dictionary = {}

func _initialize() -> void:
	call_deferred("_run")

func _run() -> void:
	var args = OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
	preload("res://workshop/support_model.gd").reuse_priors = "--no-prior-cache" not in args
	preload("res://workshop/support_model.gd").reuse_comparisons = "--no-comparison-reuse" not in args
	preload("res://workshop/reasoner.gd").reuse_matches = "--no-match-cache" not in args
	var seed_value = 692
	if "--seed" in args:
		seed_value = int(args[args.find("--seed") + 1])
	var g = Game.new()
	g.new_case(seed_value, 10)
	g.w.dlog.detailed = false
	g.w._day_gifts()
	var reports: Array = []
	for person in g.w.npcs:
		for reading in person.info_log:
			if reading.role in Live.Assess.ROLES:
				reports.append(g.add_card({"kind": "info", "source": person.id, "subject": [], "role": reading.role,
					"day": reading.day, "phase": reading.get("phase", 3 if reading.day == 0 else 0),
					"data": reading.data, "text": "Native %s: %s" % [reading.role, reading.text]}))
	var board = Canvas.new(Live.view(g))
	var root_id: String = board.selected
	var target = g.w.living_good()[0]
	var listener = g.w.living_good().filter(func(n): return n != target)[0]
	board.nodes[root_id].conclusion.target = target.id
	for number in reports:
		board.connect_cards("e:" + Evidence.normalize_card(g.board[number - 1]).id, root_id)
	var number = Live.file(g, board.compile(root_id))
	assert(number > 0)
	g.set_workshop_effects(true)
	g.state = "talk"
	g.talk_with = listener
	g.questions_left = 3
	g.command("convince %d" % number)
	g.command("bye")
	g.w.phase = 2
	g._start_meeting()
	g.workshop_deliveries.clear()
	var before_rng: int = g.w.rng.state
	print("Profiling native ten-person town, seed %d, %d presented reports" % [seed_value, reports.size()])
	Profile.start()
	var town_span = Profile.open("town")
	var output = await g.workshop_command("present %d" % number, func(_name): await process_frame)
	Profile.close(town_span)
	result = Profile.finish()
	result["seed"] = seed_value
	result["reports"] = reports.size()
	result["optimizations"] = {"priors": preload("res://workshop/support_model.gd").reuse_priors,
		"comparisons": preload("res://workshop/support_model.gd").reuse_comparisons,
		"candidate_checks": preload("res://workshop/reasoner.gd").reuse_matches}
	result["deliveries"] = g.workshop_deliveries.duplicate(true)
	result["output"] = output
	result["rng_before"] = str(before_rng)
	result["rng_after"] = str(g.w.rng.state)
	result["behavior"] = _behavior(g)
	result["behavior_hash"] = JSON.stringify(Evidence.canonical(result.behavior)).sha256_text()
	var totals = {}
	for row in result.rows:
		if row.stage == "listener":
			print("  %s: %.2f ms" % [row.listener, row.total_us / 1000.0])
		if not totals.has(row.stage):
			totals[row.stage] = {"calls": 0, "total_us": 0, "self_us": 0}
		for field in ["calls", "total_us", "self_us"]:
			totals[row.stage][field] += row[field]
	result["stages"] = totals
	print("Stage totals: ", JSON.stringify(totals))
	print("Town: %.2f ms; behavior %s" % [result.elapsed_us / 1000.0, result.behavior_hash])
	var path = "res://logs/workshop_profile.json"
	if "--output" in args:
		path = args[args.find("--output") + 1]
	DirAccess.make_dir_recursive_absolute(ProjectSettings.globalize_path(path.get_base_dir()))
	var file = FileAccess.open(path, FileAccess.WRITE)
	file.store_string(JSON.stringify(result, "\t"))
	quit()

func _behavior(g) -> Dictionary:
	var people = {}
	for n in g.w.npcs:
		people[n.id] = _mind(n)
		if n.shadow != null:
			people[n.id]["cover"] = _mind(n.shadow)
	return {"people": people, "output": g.render(g.last_events), "deliveries": g.workshop_deliveries,
		"rng": str(g.w.rng.state), "state": g.state, "presented": g.presented_today}

func _mind(n) -> Dictionary:
	var value = {}
	for field in ["suspicion", "trust", "npc_trust", "discounted", "honesty", "ledger", "pressure", "believed_roles", "used_facts", "heard", "dialogue_memory"]:
		value[field] = n.get(field)
	var state: Dictionary = n.workshop_state.duplicate(true)
	for field in ["elapsed_ms", "cache_hit"]:
		state.erase(field)
	value["workshop_state"] = state
	return value
